FILE: older/scripts/publish.sh
====================================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Every 10 minutes: push the dashboard's data files (no keys) to the GitHub branch "older-data" so the run can be watched remotely.
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"; OUT="${OLDER_OUT:-/mnt/volume_1794164707392/older}"; URL="$(git -C "$HERE" remote get-url origin)"
while true; do
  sleep 600
  [ -f "$OUT/pub/state.json" ] || continue
  if grep -rIlE 'api-key=[A-Za-z0-9]{8}|jup_[0-9a-f]{16}|X-APIKEY' "$OUT/pub" >/dev/null 2>&1; then echo "possible key in pub files - not publishing"; continue; fi
  T="$(mktemp -d)"; cp "$OUT"/pub/*.json "$T"/ 2>/dev/null; pm2 logs older --nostream --lines 30 2>/dev/null | sed -E 's/api-key=[A-Za-z0-9-]+/api-key=***/g; s/jup_[0-9a-f]+/jup_***/g' > "$T/log-tail.txt" || true
  ( cd "$T" && git init -q && git checkout -q -b older-data && git add . && git -c user.name="older-data" -c user.email="noreply@example.com" commit -q -m "older data $(date -u +%FT%TZ)" && git push -q -f "$URL" older-data ) >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "published $(date -u +%T)" || echo "publish failed $(date -u +%T)"
  rm -rf "$T"
done


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/scripts/seed-db.mjs
====================================================================================================
// seed-db.mjs — older-11: prepare the database of a NEW run from the database of the previous one.
//   node scripts/seed-db.mjs <previous.db> <new.db> [--live-only]
// The previous database is opened READ-ONLY and is not changed in any way. Copied into the new one: what is known about each
// COIN'S IDENTITY (its pool, its creation time and where that came from, its token program / transfer-fee reading) and the
// list of pools, plus the date of the last daily pool inventory — so the new run does not have to ask the providers again for
// tens of thousands of creation times (it could not buy a coin until it knew its age).
// NOT copied: buys, sales, one-buy marks, refusals, minute bars, feed events, "counting since" dates, the first-start date.
// The new run starts with no trades and no history of decisions. --live-only leaves out coins that were watched by sampling.
// Safe to repeat: if the new database already holds coins it is left alone. No key or secret is read or written.
import Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";

export function seed(prevPath, newPath, { liveOnly = false } = {}) {
  if (!existsSync(prevPath)) throw new Error(`previous database not found: ${prevPath}`);
  if (prevPath === newPath) throw new Error("the new database must be a different file");
  const st = new Store(newPath), db = st.db, n = (t) => db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM ${t}`).get().n;
  try {
    if (n("entries") > 0) return { seeded: false, why: `the new database already holds ${n("entries")} buys — left alone` };
    if (n("coins") > 0) return { seeded: false, why: `the new database already holds ${n("coins")} coins — left alone` };
    const prev = new Database(prevPath, { readonly: true, fileMustExist: true }); let coins = 0, skipped = 0, pools = 0;
    try {
      const insC = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO coins (mint, pool, create_ts, create_src, pump_origin, first_seen, info) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)"), insP = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO pools VALUES (?,?,?,?)");
      db.transaction(() => {
        for (const r of prev.prepare("SELECT mint, pool, create_ts, create_src, pump_origin, first_seen, info FROM coins").iterate()) {
          if (liveOnly && r.info && /"monitoring_mode":"polled"/.test(r.info)) { skipped++; continue; }
          insC.run(r.mint, r.pool, r.create_ts, r.create_src, r.pump_origin, r.first_seen, r.info); coins++; }
        for (const r of prev.prepare("SELECT pool, mint, quote, creator FROM pools").iterate()) { insP.run(r.pool, r.mint, r.quote, r.creator); pools++; }
        const inv = prev.prepare("SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k='inventory'").get(); if (inv) db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta VALUES ('inventory', ?)").run(inv.v);
        db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta VALUES ('seeded_from', ?)").run(JSON.stringify({ from: prevPath, at: new Date().toISOString(), coins, pools, live_only: liveOnly }));
      })();
    } finally { prev.close(); }
    return { seeded: true, coins, skipped_sampled: skipped, pools, entries: n("entries"), decisions: n("decisions"), bars: n("bars") };
  } finally { db.close(); }
}

if (import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`) {
  const [prev, next, ...rest] = process.argv.slice(2);
  if (!prev || !next) { console.error("usage: node scripts/seed-db.mjs <previous.db> <new.db> [--live-only]"); process.exit(2); }
  try { const r = seed(prev, next, { liveOnly: rest.includes("--live-only") }); console.log(r.seeded ? `new database prepared: ${r.coins} coins and ${r.pools} pools known (no buys, no decisions, no bars)${r.skipped_sampled ? `; ${r.skipped_sampled} sampled coins left out` : ""}` : `not prepared: ${r.why}`); }
  catch (e) { console.error("seed failed: " + e.message); process.exit(1); }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/scripts/start.sh
====================================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Start (or restart) the older-coin PAPER run under pm2. Nothing here signs or sends a transaction; no key is printed.
#   bash older/scripts/start.sh [name=shakedown] [entry-days] [settings] [previous-name]
# entry-days (e.g. 7): new entries stop that many days after this name's FIRST start (the date is written once to
# <name>.until and reused on every restart, so a restart never extends the window); exits and runner tracking continue.
# Use "" to leave it open. Each name has its own database on the volume, so one run never mixes with another.
# settings (older-11): the run's rule set. Written once to <name>.profile and reused on every restart of that name, so a
#   restart can never silently change what a run buys. Empty = the settings of older-10 (every legacy book, no gates).
#   older-11 = the owner's 13 books; every signal paper-bought as before and LABELLED with his GMGN checks (passed in time /
#              failed / late / no reading — OLDER_GATE_MODE=label, the default; =cancel in the environment cancels instead);
#              coins from 6 hours old; buying again off; pump.fun pool coins only — the sampled "rest of Solana" adapter is
#              OFF (its buys are priced from quotes and the checks are not applied to them).
# previous-name (older-11): only when <name> has no database yet. The new database is prepared from that run's database —
#   coin identities and pools only, read-only on the old file, NO buys / decisions / bars (scripts/seed-db.mjs).
#   PRICE MEMORY is carried in ONLY from a run that had the SAME settings (e.g. from the validation run into the scored run):
#   then the hour-scale books do not start empty. From a run with other settings — the old bot — nothing but identities
#   comes across and the start is COLD: its price memory was built by code with known faults (second opinion, 25 Nov).
#   The previous run's database is kept exactly as it is. Nothing is deleted. Positions still open in it are no longer
#   managed once it is stopped: they stay in that database as they were, and are not results.
set -euo pipefail
NAME="${1:-shakedown}"; HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"; cd "$HERE"
OUT="${OLDER_OUT:-/mnt/volume_1794164707392/older}"; mkdir -p "$OUT/pub"
[ -d node_modules ] || npm install --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund >/dev/null 2>&1
SITE_KEY="$(grep -E '^WEB_KEY=' /root/older/.env 2>/dev/null | head -1 | cut -d= -f2- | tr -d '"'"'"'' || true)"
DAYS="${2:-}"; UNTIL=""
if [ -f "$OUT/$NAME.until" ]; then UNTIL="$(cat "$OUT/$NAME.until")"; elif [ -n "$DAYS" ]; then UNTIL="$(( $(date +%s) + DAYS * 86400 ))"; echo "$UNTIL" > "$OUT/$NAME.until"; fi
# ---- the run's settings: fixed at the first start of this name
PROFILE="${3:-}"; FROM="${4:-}"; DB="$OUT/older-$NAME.db"
if [ -f "$OUT/$NAME.profile" ]; then SAVED="$(cat "$OUT/$NAME.profile")"
  if [ -n "$PROFILE" ] && [ "$PROFILE" != "$SAVED" ]; then echo "run '$NAME' was started with settings '$SAVED' — it cannot be restarted as '$PROFILE'. Use a new name for a new rule set. Nothing was changed."; exit 1; fi
  PROFILE="$SAVED"
elif [ -n "$PROFILE" ] && [ -f "$DB" ]; then echo "run '$NAME' already has a database that was started WITHOUT settings '$PROFILE'. Use a new name for a new rule set. Nothing was changed."; exit 1; fi
BOOKS=""; GATES=""; REBUY=""; MINAGE=""; WIDE=1
case "$PROFILE" in
  "") ;;
  older-11) BOOKS=older-11; GATES=older-11; REBUY=0; MINAGE=6; WIDE=0 ;;
  *) echo "unknown settings '$PROFILE' (known: older-11). Nothing was changed."; exit 1 ;;
esac
# ---- a new run prepared from a previous one (before anything is stopped: a failure here leaves the running bot untouched)
WARM_FROM=""
if [ -n "$FROM" ] && [ ! -f "$DB" ]; then
  [ -f "$OUT/older-$FROM.db" ] || { echo "previous run '$FROM' has no database at $OUT/older-$FROM.db. Nothing was changed."; exit 1; }
  node scripts/seed-db.mjs "$OUT/older-$FROM.db" "$DB" $([ "$WIDE" = 0 ] && echo --live-only)
  if [ -n "$PROFILE" ] && [ -f "$OUT/$FROM.profile" ] && [ "$(cat "$OUT/$FROM.profile")" = "$PROFILE" ]; then WARM_FROM="$OUT/older-$FROM.db.warm"; echo "price memory will be carried from run '$FROM' (same settings)"; else echo "cold start: run '$FROM' had other settings — only coin identities come across, no price memory"; fi
fi
[ -n "$PROFILE" ] && [ ! -f "$OUT/$NAME.profile" ] && echo "$PROFILE" > "$OUT/$NAME.profile"
pm2 delete older older-pub >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
OLDER_OUT="$OUT" OLDER_DB="$DB" OLDER_SOLANA_WIDE="$WIDE" OLDER_SITE_KEY="$SITE_KEY" OLDER_RUN_NAME="$NAME" OLDER_ENTRIES_UNTIL="$UNTIL" OLDER_COMMIT="$(git -C "$HERE" rev-parse HEAD 2>/dev/null || true)" \
  OLDER_BOOKS="$BOOKS" OLDER_GATES="$GATES" OLDER_REBUY="$REBUY" OLDER_MIN_AGE_H="$MINAGE" OLDER_WARM_FROM="$WARM_FROM" OLDER_GATE_MODE="${OLDER_GATE_MODE:-}" OLDER_REPAIR="${OLDER_REPAIR:-}" \
  pm2 start src/main.mjs --name older --cwd "$HERE" --node-args="--max-old-space-size=1100" --kill-timeout 20000 --time >/dev/null
OLDER_OUT="$OUT" pm2 start scripts/publish.sh --name older-pub --cwd "$HERE" --interpreter bash >/dev/null
pm2 save --force >/dev/null
IP="$(hostname -I | awk '{print $1}')"
echo "started: run '$NAME'${PROFILE:+ (settings $PROFILE)}, database $DB, code $(git -C "$HERE" rev-parse --short HEAD 2>/dev/null)${UNTIL:+, entries until $(date -u -d @"$UNTIL" '+%d %b %H:%M UTC')}"
echo "dashboard: http://$IP:8793/${SITE_KEY:+?k=$SITE_KEY}"
pm2 ls | grep -E "older|name" || true


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/scripts/study-family.mjs
====================================================================================================
// study-family.mjs — READ-ONLY study of one book: the price path of every filled buy, from the stored one-minute bars.
//   node older/scripts/study-family.mjs [FAMILY=HH-BREAK] [run=shakedown]
// Opens the database read-only (the running bot is not touched), writes <OUT>/pub/study-<FAMILY>.json (picked up by the
// 10-minute publisher) and prints a short summary. No key is read or written. Minute bars are a coarser record than the live
// feed: multiples here are bar closes / highs against the signal price — good for "when did it peak, what was it worth after
// N hours", not for exact fills.
import Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
const FAM = process.argv[2] || "HH-BREAK", RUN = process.argv[3] || "shakedown", OUT = process.env.OLDER_OUT || "/mnt/volume_1794164707392/older";
const db = new Database(`${OUT}/older-${RUN}.db`, { readonly: true, fileMustExist: true });
const now = Date.now() / 1000, CK = [5, 15, 30, 60, 90, 120, 180, 240, 300, 360, 480, 600, 720, 960, 1200, 1440], r3 = (x) => (x == null || !isFinite(x) ? null : +x.toFixed(3));
const bars = db.prepare("SELECT m, h, l, c, v FROM bars WHERE mint = ? AND m >= ? ORDER BY m");
const out = [];
for (const row of db.prepare("SELECT json FROM entries WHERE status = 'filled'").all()) {
  const e = JSON.parse(row.json); if (!e.families?.includes(FAM) || !e.fill || !(e.signalPx > 0)) continue;
  const m0 = Math.floor(e.fill.t / 60), bs = bars.all(e.mint, m0), px = e.signalPx, age = Math.floor((now - e.fill.t) / 60);
  let peak = 1, peakMin = 0, vol = 0; const at = {}, hiAfter = {}; let last = 1, i = 0;
  for (const k of CK) { if (k > age) break; while (i < bs.length && bs[i].m - m0 <= k) { const b = bs[i], x = b.h / px; if (x > peak) { peak = x; peakMin = b.m - m0; } last = b.c / px; vol += b.v; i++; } at[k] = r3(last); }
  for (; i < bs.length; i++) { const b = bs[i], x = b.h / px; if (x > peak) { peak = x; peakMin = b.m - m0; } last = b.c / px; vol += b.v; }
  for (const k of [60, 120, 180, 240, 360, 480]) { if (k > age) break; let h = null; for (const b of bs) if (b.m - m0 > k && (h === null || b.h > h)) h = b.h; hiAfter[k] = h === null ? null : r3(h / px); } // the best price seen AFTER k minutes
  const ver = {}; for (const v of e.versions || []) { if (v.stall) continue; ver[(v.policy === "trail" ? "t" : "h") + Math.round(v.stop * 100)] = { st: v.status, ret: r3(v.exit?.ret), hold_m: v.exit ? Math.round(v.exit.hold_s / 60) : null, why: v.reason ?? null, now_x: v.exit ? null : r3(v.lastX), max_x: r3(v.maxX) }; }
  out.push({ id: e.id, sym: e.token?.symbol ?? null, mint: e.mint.slice(0, 8), ag: e.ag, fill_t: Math.round(e.fill.t), age_min: age, mc: Math.round(e.signalMc), fams: e.families.length, max_x: r3(e.runner?.maxX), reached: e.runner?.reached ?? {}, bars: bs.length, vol_usd_after: Math.round(vol), peak: r3(peak), peak_min: peakMin, last: r3(last), at, hi_after: hiAfter, ver, v5: e.features?.w5?.vol_usd ?? null });
}
mkdirSync(`${OUT}/pub`, { recursive: true }); const file = `${OUT}/pub/study-${FAM}.json`;
writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify({ family: FAM, run: RUN, at: Math.round(now), n: out.length, checkpoints_min: CK, entries: out }));
const open = out.filter((x) => x.ver.t40 && x.ver.t40.st !== "closed").length, pk = out.map((x) => x.peak_min).sort((a, b) => a - b);
console.log(`${FAM}: ${out.length} filled buys written to ${file} (${open} still open on the 40% stop; median minutes to the highest price ${pk.length ? pk[pk.length >> 1] : "-"}). It will be published within 10 minutes.`);


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/scripts/validate.sh
====================================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Read-only technical validation on the server. PAPER ONLY — nothing here signs or sends a transaction, and no key is printed.
#   bash older/scripts/validate.sh [minutes=10]
# Writes results to $OLDER_OUT (default: the 100 GB volume) and publishes ONLY those result files to the GitHub branch
# "older-validation" so they can be reviewed. It does not touch the tracker, its database or its branch.
set -euo pipefail
MIN="${1:-10}"; HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"; cd "$HERE"
OUT="${OLDER_OUT:-/mnt/volume_1794164707392/older}"; mkdir -p "$OUT"; export OLDER_OUT="$OUT"
command -v node >/dev/null || { echo "node not found"; exit 1; }
[ -d node_modules ] || npm install --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund >/dev/null 2>&1
echo "[validate] 1/4 live feed for ${MIN} min (own database on the volume; port 8793)"
# the previous validation database is ARCHIVED, never deleted (no historical data is destroyed by this script)
if ls "$OUT"/older-validation.db* >/dev/null 2>&1; then mkdir -p "$OUT/archive"; STAMP="$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)"; for f in "$OUT"/older-validation.db*; do mv "$f" "$OUT/archive/$(basename "$f").$STAMP"; done; fi
OLDER_DB="$OUT/older-validation.db" INV_MAX_POOLS="${INV_MAX_POOLS:-60000}" OLDER_RUN_MINUTES="$MIN" node --max-old-space-size=900 src/main.mjs > "$OUT/run.log" 2>&1 || echo "[validate] run ended with an error — see run.log"
echo "[validate] 2/4 transaction evidence (120 recent PumpSwap transactions)"
node src/validate-tx.mjs 120 > "$OUT/tx-validation.json" 2> "$OUT/tx-validation.err" || true
echo "[validate] 3/4 GMGN (paced) and 4/4 Jupiter (keyless unless a key is set)"
node src/probe-gmgn.mjs > "$OUT/gmgn-probe.json" 2>/dev/null || true
node src/probe-jupiter.mjs > "$OUT/jupiter-probe.json" 2>/dev/null || true
tail -n 40 "$OUT/run.log" | sed -E 's/api-key=[A-Za-z0-9-]+/api-key=***/g' > "$OUT/run-tail.txt"
# publish the result files only
PUB="$(mktemp -d)"; cp "$OUT"/validation-run.json "$OUT"/tx-validation.json "$OUT"/gmgn-probe.json "$OUT"/jupiter-probe.json "$OUT"/run-tail.txt "$PUB"/ 2>/dev/null || true
if grep -rIlE 'api-key=[A-Za-z0-9]{8}|X-APIKEY' "$PUB" >/dev/null 2>&1; then echo "[validate] a result file looks like it contains a key — NOT publishing"; exit 1; fi
cd "$PUB" && git init -q && git checkout -q -b older-validation && git add . && git -c user.name="older-validation" -c user.email="noreply@example.com" commit -q -m "older validation results $(date -u +%FT%TZ)" \
  && git push -q -f "$(git -C "$HERE" remote get-url origin)" older-validation && echo "[validate] results published to branch older-validation" || echo "[validate] could not publish — results are in $OUT"


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/scripts/validate10.sh
====================================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# 60-MINUTE PAPER VALIDATION of older-10 — launcher for reports/older/2026-11-24/VALIDATION-PROTOCOL.md
#
#   FOR REVIEW. NOT RUN YET. Do not run before (1) Codex has reviewed the commit, (2) the owner has said "go" and named the
#   commit and the option. PAPER ONLY: nothing here builds, signs or sends a transaction, and no key is printed or published.
#
# Usage — from a SEPARATE checkout of branch older-10-review (never from /root/older, the shakedown bot's own checkout):
#   bash older/scripts/validate10.sh <full commit sha> beside     option A: the shakedown bot keeps running (two-process contention test)
#   bash older/scripts/validate10.sh <full commit sha> alone      option B: the shakedown bot was ALREADY stopped by the owner
#
# This script NEVER stops, starts, restarts or deletes the shakedown processes (pm2 "older", "older-pub"). It only reads their
# state. Option B's stop and restart of the shakedown bot are the owner's own, separate commands.
# It never deletes or moves an existing file: if its database or output folder already exists it refuses to start.
set -euo pipefail
die() { echo "[validate10] REFUSED: $*" >&2; exit 1; }
SHA="${1:-}"; MODE="${2:-}"
[ "${#SHA}" = 40 ] || die "give the full 40-character commit to validate"
[ "$MODE" = beside ] || [ "$MODE" = alone ] || die "second argument must be 'beside' (option A) or 'alone' (option B)"
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"                       # …/older of THIS checkout
case "$HERE" in /root/older|/root/older/*) die "this is the shakedown bot's checkout — use a separate one" ;; esac
[ "$(git -C "$HERE" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$SHA" ] || die "this checkout is not at $SHA"
[ -z "$(git -C "$HERE" status --porcelain -- .)" ] || die "this checkout has local changes"
command -v node >/dev/null || die "node not found"; command -v pm2 >/dev/null || die "pm2 not found"; command -v curl >/dev/null || die "curl not found"

ROOT="${OLDER_VAL_ROOT:-/mnt/volume_1794164707392/older}"; OUT="$ROOT/validation10"; DB="$OUT/older-validation10.db"; PORT=8794; NAME=older-val
[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || die "$OUT already exists — a previous validation must be kept; choose a new folder with OLDER_VAL_ROOT"
FREE_GB="$(df -BG --output=avail "$ROOT" | tail -1 | tr -dc 0-9)"; [ "${FREE_GB:-0}" -ge 10 ] || die "less than 10 GB free on $ROOT"

# state of the shakedown bot (read only). pm2 jlist contains environment values, so it is always filtered through node.
pm2state() { pm2 jlist 2>/dev/null | node -e 'let d="";process.stdin.on("data",x=>d+=x).on("end",()=>{let a=[];try{a=JSON.parse(d)}catch{};const want=process.argv.slice(1);console.log(JSON.stringify(a.filter(p=>!want.length||want.includes(p.name)).map(p=>({name:p.name,pid:p.pid,status:p.pm2_env&&p.pm2_env.status,restarts:p.pm2_env&&p.pm2_env.restart_time,uptime_ms:p.pm2_env&&p.pm2_env.pm_uptime?Date.now()-p.pm2_env.pm_uptime:null,cpu_pct:p.monit&&p.monit.cpu,mem_mb:p.monit?Math.round(p.monit.memory/1048576):null}))))})' "$@"; }
SHAKE="$(pm2state older | node -e 'let d="";process.stdin.on("data",x=>d+=x).on("end",()=>{const a=JSON.parse(d||"[]");console.log(a[0]?a[0].status:"absent")})')"
if [ "$MODE" = alone ]; then [ "$SHAKE" != online ] || die "option B needs the shakedown bot stopped first (the owner's own command); it is online"
else [ "$SHAKE" = online ] || die "option A is a test beside the running shakedown bot; it is '$SHAKE'"; fi
pm2state "$NAME" | grep -q '"name"' && die "a pm2 process called $NAME already exists"

mkdir -p "$OUT/snap"; umask 077
ENVF="${OLDER_ENV:-/root/older/.env}"
KEYF="$(mktemp)"; trap 'rm -f "$KEYF"' EXIT          # the page key, in a private temp file: never on a command line, never in the output
grep -E '^WEB_KEY=' "$ENVF" 2>/dev/null | head -1 | cut -d= -f2- | tr -d '"'"'"'\r\n' > "$KEYF" || true
SITE_KEY="$(cat "$KEYF")"
[ -d "$HERE/node_modules" ] || (cd "$HERE" && npm install --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund >/dev/null 2>&1)

ev() { echo "$(date -u +%FT%T.%3NZ) $*" | tee -a "$OUT/events.txt"; }
snap() { # $1 = label. One status read (no keys in it), the filtered process table, and the server's load.
  local f="$OUT/snap/$1"
  curl -s -m 8 --get --data-urlencode "k@$KEYF" "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/api/state" -o "$f.state.json" -w '%{time_total}\n' > "$f.http_s.txt" 2>/dev/null || echo "status read failed" > "$f.http_s.txt"
  pm2state older older-pub "$NAME" > "$f.proc.json" || true
  { date -u +%FT%TZ; uptime; free -m; df -BM --output=avail "$ROOT" | tail -1; ls -l "$DB"* 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $5, $9}'; } > "$f.sys.txt" 2>&1 || true
}
# non-secret settings only, by name (an allow-list — nothing else is copied out of the settings file)
CONF="$(grep -E '^(OLDER_SOLANA_WIDE|OLDER_REBUY|GMGN_WPS|JUP_RPS|INV_MIN_MC_USD|INV_MAX_POOLS|OLDER_QUOTE_FAST_S|OLDER_QUOTE_SLOW_S|OLDER_HOT_S|OLDER_COLD_S|OLDER_RES_MODE)=' "$ENVF" 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ';' || true)"

T0="$(date +%s)"; ev "START option=$MODE commit=$SHA node=$(node -v) shakedown=$SHAKE db=$DB port=$PORT settings=[OLDER_SOLANA_WIDE=1(set by launcher);${CONF}]"
( cd "$HERE" && OLDER_OUT="$OUT" OLDER_DB="$DB" OLDER_PORT="$PORT" OLDER_SOLANA_WIDE=1 OLDER_SITE_KEY="$SITE_KEY" OLDER_RUN_NAME=validation10 OLDER_COMMIT="$SHA" \
    pm2 start src/main.mjs --name "$NAME" --cwd "$HERE" --node-args="--max-old-space-size=1100" --kill-timeout 20000 --time >/dev/null )
# deliberately NO "pm2 save": the validation process must not come back after a reboot
ev "EPOCH 1 begins (cold start, empty database)"

RESTART_MIN=35; END_MIN=60; restarted=0
for min in $(seq 0 "$END_MIN"); do
  until [ "$(( $(date +%s) - T0 ))" -ge "$(( min * 60 ))" ]; do sleep 1; done
  if [ "$min" = "$RESTART_MIN" ] && [ "$restarted" = 0 ]; then
    snap "$(printf '%03d' "$min")-before-restart"; ev "RESTART of $NAME only (planned stop, then start) — EPOCH 1 ends"
    pm2 restart "$NAME" >/dev/null; restarted=1; ev "EPOCH 2 begins (same database; price memory read back if the stop was under 60 s)"
    sleep 5; snap "$(printf '%03d' "$min")-after-restart"; continue
  fi
  snap "$(printf '%03d' "$min")"; [ "$min" = 15 ] && ev "MARK minute 15: end of the cold-start segment"
  pm2state "$NAME" | grep -q '"status":"online"' || ev "WARNING: $NAME is not online at minute $min"
done
snap "final"; ev "STOP of $NAME"
pm2 stop "$NAME" >/dev/null || true; sleep 3
pm2 logs "$NAME" --nostream --lines 400 2>/dev/null | sed -E 's/api-key=[A-Za-z0-9-]+/api-key=***/g; s/jup_[0-9a-f]+/jup_***/g' > "$OUT/log-tail.txt" || true
pm2 delete "$NAME" >/dev/null || true
ev "END. Database kept at $DB (not published by this script). Shakedown bot state now: $(pm2state older | tr -d '\n')"

# ---- publish the small result files only (never the database), after a key check
PUB="$(mktemp -d)"; cp -r "$OUT/snap" "$OUT/events.txt" "$OUT/log-tail.txt" "$PUB"/ 2>/dev/null || true
BAD=0; grep -rIlE 'api-key=[A-Za-z0-9]{8}|jup_[0-9a-f]{16}|X-APIKEY|PRIVATE KEY' "$PUB" >/dev/null 2>&1 && BAD=1
[ -s "$KEYF" ] && grep -rIlF -f "$KEYF" "$PUB" >/dev/null 2>&1 && BAD=1
if [ "$BAD" = 1 ]; then echo "[validate10] a result file looks like it contains a key — NOT publishing; results stay in $OUT"; exit 1; fi
( cd "$PUB" && git init -q && git checkout -q -b older-validation10 && git add . && git -c user.name="older-validation" -c user.email="noreply@example.com" commit -q -m "older-10 validation $SHA ($MODE) $(date -u +%FT%TZ)" \
  && git push -q -f "$(git -C "$HERE" remote get-url origin)" older-validation10 ) && echo "[validate10] result files published to branch older-validation10" || echo "[validate10] could not publish — results are in $OUT"
rm -rf "$PUB"


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/backfill.mjs
====================================================================================================
// backfill.mjs — fills in what a live feed cannot know: token creation time, the history before we started watching (or before
// the pool existed: the bonding-curve period), the age-group reference prices, and feed-outage gaps for coins that matter.
// Source: GMGN (token info + 1-minute USD klines). One queue, paced by the GMGN client; priority: open positions → coins in or
// near the band → the rest. Every outcome is counted; nothing is invented when GMGN has no data (the coin stays not-covered / MISSING).
import * as S from "./spec.mjs";
import { creationTime, minuteBars, gmgnOn } from "./gmgn.mjs";
export const bf = { queued: 0, done: 0, created: 0, noCreation: 0, bars: 0, refsOk: 0, refsMissing: 0, gapsPatched: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "" };

/** WHICH GAPS TO ASK BACK-FILL FOR (Codex's review of 161d545). `gaps` is the engine's SIGNAL-coverage list (engine.cov.gaps) —
 *  the list every coin's hour-scale windows are checked against — and `seen` how many of its entries were handled before.
 *  It must be THIS list, not the pool-trust list (engine.feedCov.gaps):
 *    - a late spell or a silence is a gap in signal coverage only, so the pool-trust list never contains it;
 *    - since older-10 a reconnect's signal gap ends at the first delay measurement, a little AFTER the pool-trust gap ends, and
 *      a repair recorded for the shorter interval does not cover the gap the window test looks for.
 *  New gaps whose minute ranges touch or overlap are merged into ONE request (one set of minute bars covers them all), so a
 *  flapping stream does not multiply requests. Returns the merged [from, to] intervals and the new `seen`.
 *  Repair is by 1-minute bars and therefore only ever satisfies hour-scale windows: exact windows (FAST, fresh crossings) are
 *  never repaired, and nothing here touches pool-state trust. */
export function gapRequests(gaps, seen = 0) {
  const fresh = gaps.slice(seen).filter((g) => g && g[1] > g[0]).map(([a, b]) => [a, b]).sort((p, q) => p[0] - q[0]), out = [];
  for (const g of fresh) { const last = out[out.length - 1]; if (last && Math.floor(g[0] / 60) * 60 <= Math.ceil(last[1] / 60) * 60) { if (g[1] > last[1]) last[1] = g[1]; } else out.push(g); }
  return { requests: out, seen: gaps.length };
}
export function startBackfill({ engine, store, jup = null, log = console.log, paused = () => false }) { // paused(): older-11 — history repair switched off for now (main.mjs): nothing is asked of GMGN here, the queue is kept
  const q = [], inQ = new Set(), needC = new Set(), askedC = new Set();
  // CREATION TIME IN BULK (measured 20 Nov: GMGN's free plan managed 38 coins in 10 minutes, far too slow for thousands).
  // Jupiter's token search takes 100 mints a call. For a pump.fun coin its "first pool" IS the bonding curve (firstPool.id = the
  // mint), created in the same transaction as the token — so that timestamp is token creation, not a later pool listing.
  // Anything else (no record, another launchpad, a first pool that is not the curve) falls back to GMGN and is labelled.
  async function creationLoop() {
    while (true) {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
      const batch = [...needC].slice(0, 100); if (!batch.length) continue; for (const m of batch) { needC.delete(m); askedC.add(m); }
      let got = []; try { got = await jup.tokens(batch); } catch (e) { bf.errors++; bf.lastErr = "jupiter creation: " + String(e.message).slice(0, 120); for (const m of batch) askedC.delete(m); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 3000)); continue; }
      const by = new Map(got.map((t) => [t.mint, t]));
      for (const m of batch) { const t = by.get(m), c = engine.coin(m);
        if (t?.symbol && !c.info.symbol) c.info.symbol = t.symbol;
        if (t && t.launchpad === "pump.fun" && t.first_pool?.id === m && t.first_pool.created) { if (!c.createTs) { c.createTs = t.first_pool.created; c.info.create_src = "jupiter firstPool = pump.fun bonding curve (token creation)"; c.info.token_program = t.token_program; c.info.symbol = t.symbol; bf.created++; bf.createdJup = (bf.createdJup || 0) + 1; store?.saveCoin(m, { createTs: c.createTs, createSrc: "jupiter curve" }); } if (t.mcap >= 20000 && t.mcap <= 2e6) wantBars(m, 1); }
        else { bf.jupNoCreation = (bf.jupNoCreation || 0) + 1; wantBars(m, 2); } }
    }
  }
  const wantBars = (mint, prio = 1) => { if (inQ.has(mint)) return; inQ.add(mint); q.push({ mint, prio }); q.sort((a, b) => a.prio - b.prio); bf.queued++; };
  // creation first (bulk, fast); history bars (GMGN, slow) are then queued by the creation loop, in-band coins first
  const want = (mint, prio = 1) => { const c = engine.coin(mint); if (jup && !c.createTs && !askedC.has(mint)) { needC.add(mint); return; } if (prio <= 1 || !jup) wantBars(mint, prio); };
  async function one(mint) {
    const c = engine.coin(mint), now = Date.now() / 1000;
    if (!c.createTs) { const r = await creationTime(mint); if (!r) { bf.noCreation++; store?.saveNote(mint, { ag: null, family: "*", t: now, reason: "creation time unavailable", detail: {} }); return; } c.createTs = r.t; bf.created++; store?.saveCoin(mint, { createTs: r.t, createSrc: r.src }); }
    const sr = c.series, liveFrom = sr.histFrom ?? now;
    // history window needed now: 4 h 15 min (+5 min margin) before the live start, but not before creation
    const from = Math.max(c.createTs, Math.floor(liveFrom - (4 * 3600 + 20 * 60))), to = Math.floor(liveFrom);
    if (to > from) { const bars = await minuteBars(mint, from, to); bf.bars += bars.length; const r = sr.addBars(bars, { from, to }); if (!r.accepted) bf.emptyHistory = (bf.emptyHistory || 0) + 1; for (const b of bars) store?.saveBackfillBar(mint, b.m, b, r.accepted && sr.bf.has(b.m)); }
    // AGE-DOUBLE reference for the coin's current group (and the next one if it starts before live coverage)
    const ag = S.ageGroupOf(now - c.createTs);
    if (ag) {
      const b0 = c.createTs + S.ageStartOf(ag);
      if (!sr.covered(b0 - 60, b0)) {
        const bars = await minuteBars(mint, Math.floor(b0 - 3 * 3600), Math.floor(b0)); const last = bars.filter((x) => (x.m + 1) * 60 <= b0).pop();
        if (last) { engine.setRef(mint, ag, { status: "OK", mc: last.c * c.supply, px: last.c, t: last.m * 60 + 59, quietS: Math.round(b0 - (last.m * 60 + 59)), src: "gmgn-1m", boundary: b0 }); bf.refsOk++; }
        else bf.refsMissing++; // stays MISSING: no trade within 3 h before the boundary in GMGN's history
      }
    }
  }
  async function loop() {
    while (true) {
      if (paused()) { bf.paused = true; await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000)); continue; } bf.paused = false;
      const it = q.shift(); if (!it) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000)); continue; }
      inQ.delete(it.mint);
      try { await one(it.mint); bf.done++; } catch (e) { bf.errors++; bf.lastErr = String(e.message).slice(0, 160); if (/rate limited/i.test(e.message)) { q.unshift(it); inQ.add(it.mint); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 3000)); } }
    }
  }
  /** after an outage: re-fill the gap from 1-minute bars for coins with open positions or near the band */
  async function patchGap(a, b, mints) { if (paused()) { bf.gapsSkippedPaused = (bf.gapsSkippedPaused || 0) + mints.length; return; } for (const mint of mints) { if (paused()) return; try { const bars = await minuteBars(mint, Math.floor(a / 60) * 60, Math.ceil(b / 60) * 60); const r = engine.coin(mint).series.addBars(bars, { from: a, to: b, patchGap: true }); if (r.accepted) bf.gapsPatched++; else bf.gapsNotPatched = (bf.gapsNotPatched || 0) + 1; } catch (e) { bf.errors++; bf.lastErr = String(e.message).slice(0, 160); } } }
  if (jup) creationLoop();
  if (gmgnOn()) loop(); else log("[backfill] no GMGN key: creation times and history cannot be filled — coins stay not-covered");
  return { want, patchGap, size: () => q.length + needC.size };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/book.mjs
====================================================================================================
// book.mjs — shared entry inventory and independent exit versions (spec older-2).
// One ENTRY per (mint, decision observation, age group): every family whose candidate came from that same observation shares it.
// Each entry carries exit VERSIONS: stop (20/25/30/40/60%) × policy (trail | scale) and, when CONT-STALL is among its families,
// the same set again with the 60-minute stall rule. Versions never affect each other.
//
// EXECUTION MODEL (declared assumptions): buy at signal availability + 1 s, sell at trigger availability + 0.7 s, priced from
// the pool state IN FORCE at that scheduled moment (the latest reconstructed state — a pool can be traded without anyone else
// trading). The state must be trustworthy at that moment (continuous feed coverage since it was observed). If it is not:
//   buy  → the first trustworthy state within 5 s is used (delay recorded); otherwise a pricing/data timeout cancels the entry;
//   sell → stays triggered and waits for the first trustworthy state (delay recorded); unresolved after 24 h. Never dropped.
import * as S from "./spec.mjs";
import { buyTokens, liqValue, sellSol, sellQuote, feeSrc, feeBuy, feeSell, isQuote } from "./fills.mjs";

let NEXT = 1;
export const COST = S.STAKE_SOL + S.TX_COST_SOL; // acquisition cost: stake + buy transaction

export const newVersion = (stop, policy, stall) => ({ stop, policy, stall, status: "open", tok: 0, got: 0, peak: null, maxX: null, half: null, trigT: null, reason: null, schedT: null, exit: null, stallChecked: false });
/** pick(tag, policy, stop): which rows this buy carries (default: all). A first buy carries every row; a re-buy only the rows on
 *  which its book no longer holds the coin (spec REBUY). */
export function newEntry({ mint, pool, ag, t, families, features, mc, px, evidence = null, startup = false, pick = null }) {
  const fam = [...new Set(families)], versions = [];
  const sets = [...new Set(fam.map(S.clockTag))].sort((a, b) => (a === false ? -1 : b === false ? 1 : 0)); // plain versions first, then one set per clock book
  for (const stall of sets) for (const policy of S.POLICIES) for (const stop of S.STOPS) if (!pick || pick(stall, policy, stop)) versions.push(newVersion(stop, policy, stall));
  return { id: NEXT++, mint, pool, ag, decisionT: t, schedT: t + S.BUY_DELAY_MS / 1000, families: fam, features, signalMc: mc, signalPx: px, evidence, startup,
    status: "pending", fill: null, versions, runner: { maxX: null, horizonDone: false, partial: false, reached: {} }, flags: [] };
}
export const versionsFor = (e, family, policy = "trail") => e.versions.filter((v) => v.stall === S.clockTag(family) && v.policy === policy);
export function setNextId(n) { NEXT = Math.max(NEXT, n); }
const live = (v) => v.status === "open" || v.status === "scaling" || v.status === "selling";

function fillBuy(e, st, at, delay, minReq = null) {
  const tok = buyTokens(st, S.STAKE_SOL, minReq);
  if (tok == null) return false; // this state cannot price the 0.2 SOL buy (e.g. a quote state without a buy quote): keep waiting
  if (!(tok > 0)) { e.status = "cancelled"; e.flags.push("pool state could not price the buy"); return true; }
  e.status = "filled";
  const q = isQuote(st);
  e.fill = { t: at, tok, cost: COST, delay_s: +delay.toFixed(3), state_age_s: q ? null : +(at - st.t).toFixed(3), fee_buy: q ? null : feeBuy(st), fee_src: feeSrc(st, "buy"),
    quote_sol: q ? null : st.quote, base: q ? null : st.base, px_sol: q ? S.STAKE_SOL / tok : (S.STAKE_SOL / (1 + feeBuy(st))) / tok,
    exec: q ? "routed-quote" : "reconstructed-pool-state", ...(q ? {} : stRef(st)), ...(q ? { quote: { req_t: st.buy.reqT ?? st.reqT, resp_t: st.buy.t ?? st.t, route: st.buy.route ?? null, router: st.buy.router ?? null, fee_bps: st.buy.feeBps ?? null, xfer_bps: st.buy.xfer_bps ?? 0, raw_tok: st.buy.raw_tok ?? null, xfer_in_quote: st.buy.xfer_in_quote ?? null, impact: st.buy.impact ?? null } } : {}) };
  // the position's first valuation: from the same state when it can price the sale (pool state). A quote-priced entry starts
  // its high-water mark at COST and takes its first real valuation from its first sell quote. This is deliberate: if the price
  // falls between the buy and the first sell quote, the stop must still be measured from what was paid — starting the peak at
  // the first (already fallen) valuation would leave such a position without a stop. A missing valuation is never treated as a fall.
  const v0 = liqValue(st, tok) ?? COST; e.runner.maxX = v0 / COST;
  for (const v of e.versions) { v.tok = tok; v.peak = v0; v.maxX = v0 / COST; v.maxEq = v0 / COST; v.lastX = v0 / COST; v.lastT = at; }
  return true;
}
/** the pool a sale was priced on: effective SOL side, token side and the sell fee in force. Evidence only — kept so the same
 *  trade can be re-priced at another stake (atStake). No decision reads it. */
const stRef = (st) => (st.st_sig ? { st_sig: st.st_sig, st_slot: st.st_slot ?? null } : {}); // which transaction committed the state that priced this (evidence only; absent on routed quotes and on states saved before this field existed)
const poolOf = (st) => ({ q: st.quote, b: st.base, f: feeSell(st), ...stRef(st) });
function trigger(v, t, reason, value) { v.status = "selling"; v.trigT = t; v.reason = reason; v.trigValue = value; v.schedT = t + S.SELL_DELAY_MS / 1000; }
/** returns false when this state cannot price the order (it then stays pending — a sale is never dropped or priced at zero).
 *  minReq (routed quotes): every quote used must have been requested at or after the order's scheduled time. Realised proceeds are
 *  NOT clipped at zero: a near-worthless sale still pays its transaction cost. */
function execSell(e, v, st, at, delay, cov, minReq = null) {
  const q = isQuote(st);
  if (v.status === "scaling") {
    // SCALE-OUT FORMULA. part = half of the ORIGINAL tokens. One sale, one transaction cost:  got += sell(part) − TX.
    // High-water mark: peak_new = peak_old × value(remaining) / value(held before), both valued on this same state.
    // ⇒ drawdown from the peak is IDENTICAL before and after the sale: selling half neither creates a 50% "fall" nor resets the trail.
    const part = e.fill.tok * S.SCALE_FRACTION, qp = sellQuote(st, part, minReq), qb = sellQuote(st, v.tok, minReq), qa = sellQuote(st, v.tok - part, minReq);
    if (!qp || !qb || !qa) return false;
    const val = (x) => Math.max(0, x.sol - S.TX_COST_SOL), before = val(qb), after = val(qa), sol = qp.sol - S.TX_COST_SOL;
    // ROUTED QUOTES: the half, the full holding and the remainder are separate requests. They are one pricing state only if
    // they were requested close together; otherwise the banked proceeds and the rescaled peak would mix different market
    // moments. Too far apart → this order is NOT executed on them: it waits for a fresh, coherent set.
    let spread = null;
    if (q) { const rs = [qp.reqT, qb.reqT, qa.reqT]; spread = Math.max(...rs) - Math.min(...rs);
      if (!(spread <= S.SCALE_QUOTE_MAX_SPREAD_S)) { v.scaleWaits = (v.scaleWaits || 0) + 1; return false; }
      at = Math.max(qp.t, qb.t, qa.t); delay = at - v.schedT; }
    v.got += sol; v.tok -= part; if (before > v.peak) v.peak = before; // a new high seen at execution counts before rescaling
    v.peak = before > 0 ? v.peak * (after / before) : after;
    v.half = { t: at, sol, delay_s: +delay.toFixed(3), trigger_value: v.trigValue, exec: q ? "routed-quote" : "reconstructed-pool-state", ...(q ? { quotes: { half: { req_t: qp.reqT, resp_t: qp.t }, held_before: { req_t: qb.reqT, resp_t: qb.t }, remaining: { req_t: qa.reqT, resp_t: qa.t } }, quote_spread_s: +spread.toFixed(3), waited_for_coherent_quotes: v.scaleWaits || 0 } : { state_age_s: +(at - st.t).toFixed(3), pool: poolOf(st) }) };
    v.status = "open"; v.schedT = null; return true;
  }
  let sol = 0, used = null; if (v.tok > 0) { used = sellQuote(st, v.tok, minReq); if (!used) return false; sol = used.sol - S.TX_COST_SOL; }
  if (q && used) { at = used.t; delay = at - v.schedT; }
  const total = v.got + sol;
  v.status = "closed"; v.tok = 0;
  v.exit = { t: at, sol: total, last_sol: sol, ret: total / COST - 1, delay_s: +delay.toFixed(3), hold_s: Math.round(at - e.fill.t), trigger_value: v.trigValue ?? null, fee_src: feeSrc(st, "sell"), exec: q ? "routed-quote" : "reconstructed-pool-state", ...(q ? { quote: { req_t: used?.reqT ?? null, resp_t: used?.t ?? null, route: used?.route ?? null, xfer_bps: used?.xfer_bps ?? 0, raw_sol: used?.raw_sol ?? null, xfer_in_quote: used?.xfer_in_quote ?? null } } : { state_age_s: +(at - st.t).toFixed(3), pool: poolOf(st) }) };
  if (v.unresolvedSince) v.exit.was_unresolved_s = Math.round(at - v.unresolvedSince);
  if (cov && cov.gapsIn(e.fill.t, at).length) v.exit.outage_during = true;
  return true;
}

/** Everything scheduled at or before `upTo` is executed against `st`, the state in force until then. trusted(at) says whether
 *  that state is known-good at time `at`. Called before a new state replaces `st`, and on every clock tick. */
/** Returns true when it changed something that a status comparison does not show (a clock check done, the runner horizon
 *  closed): the engine check-points the entry then. Status changes are detected by the caller. */
export function advance(e, upTo, st, trusted, cov) {
  let mut = false;
  if (e.status === "pending") {
    if (e.schedT > upTo) return false;
    if (st && trusted(e.schedT) && fillBuy(e, st, e.schedT, 0)) { /* filled or cancelled */ }
    else if (upTo > e.schedT + S.BUY_PRICING_TIMEOUT_S) { e.status = "cancelled"; e.flags.push("pricing/data timeout: no trustworthy pool state within 5 s of the scheduled buy"); return false; }
    else return false;
  }
  if (e.status !== "filled") return false;
  for (let guard = 0; guard < 6; guard++) {
    let changed = false;
    for (const v of e.versions) {
      if (v.status === "open" || v.status === "scaling") { // time-based triggers, in time order
        const ck = v.stall ? S.clockOf(v.stall) : null, stallT = ck ? e.fill.t + ck.at : Infinity, endT = e.fill.t + S.MAX_HOLD_S;
        // the clock book's timed sale: at its time, sold if the position never reached minPeak (CONT-STALL) or simply if still open (HH-BREAK-4H)
        if (ck && !v.stallChecked && stallT <= upTo) { v.stallChecked = true; mut = true; if (ck.minPeak == null || v.maxX < ck.minPeak) { trigger(v, stallT, ck.reason, null); changed = true; continue; } }
        if (endT <= upTo) { trigger(v, endT, "max-hold", null); changed = true; continue; }
      }
      if ((v.status === "selling" || v.status === "scaling") && v.schedT != null && v.schedT <= upTo) {
        if (st && trusted(v.schedT) && execSell(e, v, st, v.schedT, 0, cov)) changed = true;
        // 24 h without a price: the order is MARKED unresolved (visible exposure) but stays pending — it keeps asking for a
        // price and still closes when one returns. Unresolved is never a terminal state and never removes the position.
        else if (!v.unresolvedSince && upTo > v.schedT + S.SELL_UNRESOLVED_S) { v.unresolvedSince = v.schedT + S.SELL_UNRESOLVED_S; v.unresolvedWhy = "no trustworthy price for 24 h after the scheduled sale"; e.touched = true; }
      }
    }
    if (!changed) break;
  }
  if (!e.runner.horizonDone && upTo >= e.fill.t + S.RUNNER_HORIZON_S) { e.runner.horizonDone = true; mut = true; if (cov && cov.gapsIn(e.fill.t, e.fill.t + S.RUNNER_HORIZON_S).length) e.runner.partial = true; }
  return mut;
}

/** A new trustworthy state `st` was observed at time t (after advance() ran with the previous state).
 *  For a routed-quote state, t is when the RESPONSE became available and st.reqT when it was requested: an order may only be
 *  priced by a quote requested at or after its scheduled time (a later quote is never back-dated to the scheduled instant). */
export function observe(e, st, t, cov) {
  const q = isQuote(st);
  if (e.status === "pending") { // scheduled time passed without a trustworthy state: the first one within 5 s prices the buy
    const bt = q ? (st.buy?.t ?? st.t) : t;
    if (bt >= e.schedT && bt <= e.schedT + S.BUY_PRICING_TIMEOUT_S) fillBuy(e, st, bt, bt - e.schedT, q ? e.schedT : null);
    return;
  }
  if (e.status !== "filled") return;
  if (!q) e.mark = { ...poolOf(st), t }; // the latest pool this position was valued on (stake views of open positions only)
  const full = liqValue(st, e.fill.tok), age = t - e.fill.t;
  if (full != null) { const x = full / COST; if (age <= S.RUNNER_HORIZON_S) { if (x > e.runner.maxX) e.runner.maxX = x; for (const k of S.RUNNER_X) if (x >= k && !e.runner.reached[k]) e.runner.reached[k] = Math.round(age); } }
  for (const v of e.versions) {
    if ((v.status === "selling" || v.status === "scaling") && v.schedT <= t) { execSell(e, v, st, t, t - v.schedT, cov, q ? v.schedT : null); if (v.status === "closed") continue; } // overdue order: first trustworthy state
    if (v.status !== "open" && v.status !== "scaling") continue;
    const val = liqValue(st, v.tok); if (val == null) continue; // this state does not price this holding: no peak, no trigger
    if (val > v.peak) v.peak = val; if (full != null && full / COST > v.maxX) v.maxX = full / COST;
    if (!(v.maxEq >= (v.got + val) / COST)) v.maxEq = (v.got + val) / COST; // DISPLAY ONLY: the best the row was actually worth (banked half + what is still held); maxX is the full original position
    v.lastX = (v.got + val) / COST; v.lastT = q ? (sellQuote(st, v.tok)?.t ?? t) : t; // what this row would be worth if sold now (banked half included); for a routed quote, the time of THAT quantity's own quote — another quantity's newer reply never makes it look current
    if (val <= v.peak * (1 - v.stop)) trigger(v, t, "trail", val); // also replaces a pending half-sale: one order, never two on the same tokens
    else if (v.status === "open" && v.policy === "scale" && !v.half && full != null && full >= S.SCALE_AT_X * COST) { v.status = "scaling"; v.trigT = t; v.trigValue = full; v.schedT = t + S.SELL_DELAY_MS / 1000; }
  }
}
/** the distinct token quantities an entry currently needs a sell quote for (a routed-quote provider is asked for exactly these) */
export function inventories(e) { if (e.status !== "filled") return []; const s = new Set(); for (const v of e.versions) if (live(v) && v.tok > 0) { s.add(v.tok); if (v.status === "scaling" || (v.policy === "scale" && !v.half)) s.add(e.fill.tok * S.SCALE_FRACTION); } if (!e.runner.horizonDone) s.add(e.fill.tok); return [...s]; }
/** the earliest scheduled order still waiting for a price (buy or sell), or null */
export function dueAt(e) { if (e.status === "pending") return e.schedT; let m = null; if (e.status === "filled") for (const v of e.versions) if ((v.status === "selling" || v.status === "scaling") && v.schedT != null && (m === null || v.schedT < m)) m = v.schedT; return m; }
/** rows still holding tokens whose sale has had no price for 24 h */
export const unresolvedCount = (e) => (e.status === "filled" ? e.versions.filter((v) => live(v) && v.unresolvedSince).length : 0);
export const entryDone = (e) => e.status === "cancelled" || (e.status === "filled" && e.runner.horizonDone && e.versions.every((v) => !live(v)));
export const exposure = (e) => (e.status === "filled" ? e.versions.filter(live).length : 0);

/** STAKE VIEW — website only, never a decision input. What this row's trade is worth at another stake, re-priced with the same
 *  constant-product formulas on the pool reserves SAVED at the recorded trade's own buy, half-sale and exit (open rows: the latest
 *  pool the position was valued on). Fees and price impact therefore scale with the stake; one transaction cost per leg, as in
 *  the paper trade. At STAKE_SOL it reproduces the recorded result exactly.
 *  DECLARED LIMITS: (1) the moments are those of the 0.2 SOL trade — a larger position loses more to impact, so its own stop
 *  would trigger slightly earlier and its half-sale slightly later; (2) each leg is priced on the pool as it was observed, i.e.
 *  WITHOUT the larger buy's own effect on that pool; (3) nobody can know how other traders would have reacted to a larger buy.
 *  It is an estimate of size sensitivity, not a second simulation.
 *  SALES RECORDED BEFORE RESERVES WERE SAVED (owner, 21 Nov 2026: "just do an estimation … we've got statistics now that we
 *  need to keep"): the pool at that sale is rebuilt from the sale itself — the unique constant-product pool with the BUY pool's
 *  k (= quote × base at the buy) on which the recorded tokens fetch the recorded SOL. Exact at 0.2 SOL by construction; at other
 *  stakes it is off by however much k changed between buy and sale (fees accruing to the pool, liquidity added or removed).
 *  Such a result carries est: true.
 *  null = cannot be re-priced: a routed-quote trade (its quotes were for the 0.2 SOL size only). Never scaled linearly. */
export function atStake(e, v, stake) {
  const f = e.fill; if (e.status !== "filled" || !f || f.exec !== "reconstructed-pool-state" || !(f.quote_sol > 0) || !(f.base > 0) || !(stake > 0)) return null;
  const P = (p) => ({ quote: p.q, base: p.b, feeSell: p.f, feeBuy: p.f });
  const tok = buyTokens({ quote: f.quote_sol, base: f.base, feeBuy: f.fee_buy, feeSell: f.fee_buy }, stake), cost = stake + S.TX_COST_SOL;
  let got = 0, left = tok, half = null, est = false;
  // the pool of a sale: saved with it, or rebuilt from it (sold `t0` tokens for `net` SOL after fee and transaction cost)
  const fee0 = f.fee_buy ?? 0.003, k = f.quote_sol * f.base;
  const poolAt = (leg, t0, net) => { if (leg.pool) return P(leg.pool); if (leg.exec !== "reconstructed-pool-state") return null;
    const g = (net + S.TX_COST_SOL) / (1 - fee0); if (!(g > 0) || !(t0 > 0)) return null; est = true;
    const b = (-t0 + Math.sqrt(t0 * t0 + 4 * k * t0 / g)) / 2; return { quote: k / b, base: b, feeSell: fee0, feeBuy: fee0 }; };
  const part0 = f.tok * S.SCALE_FRACTION;
  if (v.half) { const p = poolAt(v.half, part0, v.half.sol); if (!p) return null; const part = tok * S.SCALE_FRACTION; half = sellSol(p, part) - S.TX_COST_SOL; got += half; left -= part; }
  if (v.exit) { const p = poolAt(v.exit, v.half ? f.tok - part0 : f.tok, v.exit.last_sol); if (!p) return null; const sol = got + sellSol(p, left) - S.TX_COST_SOL; return { stake, cost, closed: true, sol, net: sol - cost, ret: sol / cost - 1, half, est }; }
  if (!e.mark) return null;
  return { stake, cost, closed: false, now_x: (got + liqValue(P(e.mark), left)) / cost, half, est };
}

// capacity.mjs — request budget for the quote/price provider. Not "requests × 50 tokens": every job is budgeted separately.
/** all rates in requests per second; credits per 30-day month. */
export function budget({ hot = 0, hotEvery = 10, cold = 0, coldEvery = 60, inventories = 0, quoteEvery = 2, entriesPerDay = 0, exitsPerDay = 0, halfSalesPerDay = 0, discoveryPerMin = 2, metaTokensPerDay = 0, headroom = 0.3 } = {}) {
  const j = {
    price_scan_hot: Math.ceil(hot / 50) / hotEvery, price_scan_cold: Math.ceil(cold / 50) / coldEvery,
    position_quotes: inventories / quoteEvery,                         // one request per DISTINCT inventory, never per result row
    entry_exit_quotes: (entriesPerDay + exitsPerDay + halfSalesPerDay) * 3 / 86400, // scheduled quote + up to 2 retries inside the 5 s window
    discovery: discoveryPerMin / 60, metadata: Math.ceil(metaTokensPerDay / 100) / 86400,
  };
  const rps = Object.values(j).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), need = rps * (1 + headroom);
  const sec = 30 * 86400, credits = (j.price_scan_hot + j.price_scan_cold + j.position_quotes + j.entry_exit_quotes) * sec + j.discovery * sec * 5 + j.metadata * sec * 10;
  const TIERS = [["Keyless", 0.5, Infinity, 0], ["Free", 1, Infinity, 0], ["Developer", 10, 25e6, 25], ["Launch", 50, 100e6, 100], ["Pro", 150, 500e6, 500]];
  const tier = TIERS.find(([, r, c]) => r >= need && c >= credits) || ["Enterprise", null, null, null];
  return { jobs: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(j).map(([k, v]) => [k, +v.toFixed(3)])), rps: +rps.toFixed(2), rps_with_headroom: +need.toFixed(2), credits_month: Math.round(credits), tier: tier[0], tier_usd_month: tier[3] };
}

// decoder.mjs — PumpSwap (pump.fun AMM, program pAMMBay…) event decoder, written against the PUBLISHED IDL:
//   pump-fun/pump-public-docs, idl/pump_amm.json (pinned: see IDL_PIN; field order and discriminators copied from it).
// Decodes every reserve-changing event: BuyEvent, SellEvent, DepositEvent, WithdrawEvent, InitBoostEvent, BoostBuyAndBurnEvent,
// plus CreatePoolEvent and SweepPoolFeeEvent (price-neutral; kept for bookkeeping).
// Pricing uses EFFECTIVE quote reserves = quote vault + signed virtual_quote_reserves (i128), as the docs require.
// Fees are NOT summed from field names (creator fee / holder rewards / cashback overlap): the fee on each side is measured as the
// user's net debit or credit against the amount that moved on the curve (user_quote_amount_in / quote_amount_in − 1 for a buy,
// 1 − user_quote_amount_out / quote_amount_out for a sell). Individual components are kept for the record only.
//
// OPEN QUESTION, NOT ASSUMED: are an event's pool_*_token_reserves the reserves BEFORE or AFTER that event? The docs do not say.
// RES_MODE pins ONE interpretation for the whole run ("pre" by default). Both hypotheses are scored on every swap
// (dec.fitPre / dec.fitPost / dec.fitNeither) and consecutive events of a pool are chained (dec.chainOk / dec.chainBad), so the
// live evidence shows which is right. Reserve-based fills are labelled unvalidated until validate-tx.mjs has been run on real transactions.
export const IDL_PIN = "pump-public-docs @ main, read 20 Nov 2026 (BuyEvent 40 fields, SellEvent 33, Pool 18)";
export const PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM = "pAMMBay6oceH9fJKBRHGP5D4bD4sWpmSwMn52FMfXEA";
export const PUMP_PROGRAM = "6EF8rrecthR5Dkzon8Nwu78hRvfCKubJ14M5uBEwF6P";
export const WSOL = "So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112";
export const RES_MODE = process.env.OLDER_RES_MODE === "post" ? "post" : "pre";
const B58 = "123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";
export function bs58(buf) { let n = BigInt("0x" + (Buffer.from(buf).toString("hex") || "0")), s = ""; while (n > 0n) { s = B58[Number(n % 58n)] + s; n /= 58n; } for (const b of buf) { if (b === 0) s = "1" + s; else break; } return s; }
export function bs58dec(str) { let n = 0n; for (const ch of str) { const i = B58.indexOf(ch); if (i < 0) throw new Error("bad base58"); n = n * 58n + BigInt(i); } let hex = n.toString(16); if (hex.length % 2) hex = "0" + hex; let b = n === 0n ? Buffer.alloc(0) : Buffer.from(hex, "hex"); let z = 0; for (const ch of str) { if (ch === "1") z++; else break; } return Buffer.concat([Buffer.alloc(z), b]); }
export const DISC = { // from the IDL's `events[].discriminator`
  BuyEvent: [103, 244, 82, 31, 44, 245, 119, 119], SellEvent: [62, 47, 55, 10, 165, 3, 220, 42], DepositEvent: [120, 248, 61, 83, 31, 142, 107, 144], WithdrawEvent: [22, 9, 133, 26, 160, 44, 71, 192],
  CreatePoolEvent: [177, 49, 12, 210, 160, 118, 167, 116], InitBoostEvent: [174, 124, 74, 249, 4, 81, 246, 17], BoostBuyAndBurnEvent: [63, 69, 28, 22, 48, 92, 194, 185], SweepPoolFeeEvent: [130, 164, 36, 97, 228, 130, 135, 165] };
const DKEY = new Map(Object.entries(DISC).map(([k, v]) => [Buffer.from(v).toString("hex"), k]));
export const dec = { buy: 0, sell: 0, deposit: 0, withdraw: 0, pool: 0, boost: 0, sweep: 0, short: 0, fitPre: 0, fitPost: 0, fitBoth: 0, fitNeither: 0, neitherBy: {}, feeOdd: 0, feeOddBy: {}, chainOk: 0, chainBad: 0, chainBadQuote: 0, chainBadAfterBoost: 0, chainBadQuoteAfterBoost: 0, chainQuoteMinor: 0, chainQuoteMaxRel: 0, foreign: 0, noVirtual: 0, negVirtual: 0, unknownVirtual: 0, feeNotMeasured: 0, feeNotMeasuredBy: {}, mode: RES_MODE };
// A truncated event must FAIL to decode (pk() throws) rather than yield a short, invalid address — the live run showed
// 'Invalid param: WrongSize' on pool look-ups from exactly that.
class R {
  constructor(b) { this.b = b; this.o = 0; }
  u64() { const v = this.b.readBigUInt64LE(this.o); this.o += 8; return v; } i64() { const v = this.b.readBigInt64LE(this.o); this.o += 8; return v; }
  i128() { const lo = this.b.readBigUInt64LE(this.o), hi = this.b.readBigInt64LE(this.o + 8); this.o += 16; return (hi << 64n) + lo; }
  u16() { const v = this.b.readUInt16LE(this.o); this.o += 2; return v; } u8() { return this.b[this.o++]; } bool() { return this.b[this.o++] !== 0; }
  pk() { if (this.o + 32 > this.b.length) throw new RangeError("event too short for a public key"); const v = bs58(this.b.subarray(this.o, this.o + 32)); this.o += 32; return v; } str() { const n = this.b.readUInt32LE(this.o); this.o += 4; const s = this.b.subarray(this.o, this.o + n).toString("utf8"); this.o += n; return s; }
  left() { return this.b.length - this.o; }
}

export function decodeEvent(b64) {
  let b; try { b = Buffer.from(b64, "base64"); } catch { return null; }
  if (b.length < 16) return null; const name = DKEY.get(b.subarray(0, 8).toString("hex")); if (!name) return null;
  const r = new R(b.subarray(8));
  try {
    if (name === "BuyEvent" || name === "SellEvent") {
      const isBuy = name === "BuyEvent", e = { type: isBuy ? "buy" : "sell", isBuy };
      e.ts = Number(r.i64()); e.baseAmt = r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u64();
      e.poolBase = r.u64(); e.poolQuote = r.u64(); e.quoteAmt = r.u64();
      e.lpBps = Number(r.u64()); e.lpFee = r.u64(); e.protoBps = Number(r.u64()); e.protoFee = r.u64(); e.quoteWithLp = r.u64(); e.userQuote = r.u64();
      e.pool = r.pk(); e.user = r.pk();
      e.creatorBps = null; e.cashbackBps = null; e.buybackBps = null; e.holderBps = null; e.virtualQuote = null; e.baseSupply = null; e.ix = null;
      if (r.left() >= 32 * 5 + 16) { r.pk(); r.pk(); r.pk(); r.pk(); e.coinCreator = r.pk(); e.creatorBps = Number(r.u64()); e.creatorFee = r.u64(); }
      if (isBuy && r.left() >= 1 + 8 * 5) { r.bool(); r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u64(); r.i64(); r.u64(); if (r.left() >= 4) e.ix = r.str(); }
      if (r.left() >= 32) { e.cashbackBps = Number(r.u64()); e.cashback = r.u64(); e.buybackBps = Number(r.u64()); e.buybackFee = r.u64(); }
      if (r.left() >= 16) e.virtualQuote = r.i128();
      if (r.left() >= 9) { e.canBoost = r.bool(); e.baseSupply = r.u64(); }
      if (r.left() >= 16) { e.holderBps = Number(r.u64()); e.holderRewards = r.u64(); }
      if (r.left() >= 8) e.creatorFeeUnclaimed = r.u64();
      dec[e.type]++; return e;
    }
    if (name === "DepositEvent" || name === "WithdrawEvent") {
      const e = { type: name === "DepositEvent" ? "deposit" : "withdraw" };
      e.ts = Number(r.i64()); r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u64(); e.poolBase = r.u64(); e.poolQuote = r.u64(); e.baseAmt = r.u64(); e.quoteAmt = r.u64(); r.u64(); e.pool = r.pk(); e.user = r.pk();
      dec[e.type]++; return e;
    }
    if (name === "CreatePoolEvent") {
      const e = { type: "pool" }; e.ts = Number(r.i64()); e.index = r.u16(); e.creator = r.pk(); e.baseMint = r.pk(); e.quoteMint = r.pk(); e.baseDec = r.u8(); e.quoteDec = r.u8();
      r.u64(); r.u64(); e.poolBase = r.u64(); e.poolQuote = r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u64(); r.u8(); e.pool = r.pk();
      dec.pool++; return e;
    }
    if (name === "InitBoostEvent") { const e = { type: "boost", sub: "init" }; e.ts = Number(r.i64()); e.mint = r.pk(); r.pk(); e.pool = r.pk(); e.virtualQuote = r.i128(); e.realQuoteAfter = r.u64(); dec.boost++; return e; }
    if (name === "BoostBuyAndBurnEvent") { const e = { type: "boost", sub: "burn" }; e.ts = Number(r.i64()); e.mint = r.pk(); r.pk(); e.pool = r.pk(); r.pk(); r.u64(); e.quoteUsed = r.u64(); e.baseBurned = r.u64(); e.virtualQuote = r.i128(); e.realQuoteAfter = r.u64(); e.baseAfter = r.u64(); dec.boost++; return e; }
    if (name === "SweepPoolFeeEvent") { const e = { type: "sweep" }; e.ts = Number(r.i64()); e.pool = r.pk(); r.pk(); r.pk(); r.pk(); r.pk(); e.amount = r.u64(); dec.sweep++; return e; }
  } catch { dec.short++; return null; }
  return null;
}
/** Events from a transaction's logs. PROVENANCE: a "Program data:" line is accepted only while the PumpSwap program itself is the
 *  program executing (tracked through the "Program … invoke [n]" / "… success|failed" lines), so a foreign program that prints
 *  look-alike bytes is ignored (counted in dec.foreign). */
export function eventsFromLogs(logs, program = PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM) {
  const out = [], stack = [];
  for (const l of logs || []) {
    if (l.startsWith("Program data: ")) { if (stack.length && stack[stack.length - 1] !== program) { if (decodeEventPeek(l.slice(14))) dec.foreign++; continue; } const e = decodeEvent(l.slice(14)); if (e) out.push(e); continue; }
    const m = /^Program (\w+) invoke \[\d+\]/.exec(l); if (m) { stack.push(m[1]); continue; }
    if (/^Program \w+ (success|failed)/.test(l)) stack.pop();
  }
  return out;
}
const decodeEventPeek = (b64) => { try { const b = Buffer.from(b64, "base64"); return b.length >= 8 && DKEY.has(b.subarray(0, 8).toString("hex")); } catch { return false; } };

import { QUOTE_CHAIN_TOL, FEE_MAX } from "./spec.mjs";
// instructions whose user-amount fields were validated against real transactions (tx-validation.json, 20 Nov 2026). The fee of any
// other instruction (multi_hop_swap: the user's amounts are in another token) is NOT measured from its event.
const FEE_IX = new Set(["buy", "buy_v2", "buy_exact_quote_in", "buy_exact_quote_in_v2"]);
const TOL = 0.003; // relative tolerance of the constant-product check (integer rounding on tiny trades)
/** Reconstructs one pool's state across its events. `p` = per-pool memory { base, quote (effective), vq (virtual, lamports), feeBuy, feeSell, supply }.
 *  Returns { state, sol, changed } or null when the event cannot be turned into a trustworthy state (counted; caller marks the coin lost). */
export function applyEvent(p, e, baseDec = 6) {
  const U = 10 ** baseDec;
  if (e.type === "buy" || e.type === "sell") {
    if (e.virtualQuote == null) dec.noVirtual++; else if (e.virtualQuote < 0n) dec.negVirtual++;
    // UNKNOWN virtual reserves are never replaced by zero: an event without the field, on a pool whose virtual reserves we have
    // not yet seen, cannot be turned into a trustworthy state
    if (e.virtualQuote == null && p.vq == null) { dec.unknownVirtual++; return null; }
    const vq = e.virtualQuote ?? BigInt(Math.round(p.vq)); p.vq = Number(vq);
    // Which field is the amount that moved on the curve? VALIDATED on real transactions (20 Nov 2026, tx-validation.json):
    //   buy (exact base out) and sell : quote_amount_in/out is the curve amount; user_quote_amount_* is what the user paid/received.
    //   buy_exact_quote_in[_v2]      : the two are the other way round — quote_amount_in is the user's total spend (fees inside),
    //                                  user_quote_amount_in is the part that reached the curve.
    const exactIn = e.isBuy && typeof e.ix === "string" && e.ix.startsWith("buy_exact_quote_in");
    const B = Number(e.poolBase), E = Number(e.poolQuote + vq), b = Number(e.baseAmt), lp = Number(e.lpFee);
    const q = Number(exactIn ? e.userQuote : e.quoteAmt), paid = Number(exactIn ? e.quoteAmt : e.userQuote);
    if (!(B > 0 && E > 0 && b > 0 && q > 0)) return null;
    // which interpretation reproduces the traded amount on the curve?  pre: reported reserves are before the swap; post: after it
    const preQ = e.isBuy ? (E * b) / (B - b) : (E * b) / (B + b), postQ = e.isBuy ? (E * b) / (B + b) : (E * b) / (B - b);
    const tol = Math.max(TOL, 3 / q); // integer rounding matters on very small trades
    const okPre = B > b && Math.abs(preQ - q) / q <= tol, okPost = Math.abs(postQ - q) / q <= tol;
    if (okPre && okPost) dec.fitBoth++; else if (okPre) dec.fitPre++; else if (okPost) dec.fitPost++; else { dec.fitNeither++; const k = (e.ix || "no-ix") + "|" + e.type; dec.neitherBy[k] = (dec.neitherBy[k] || 0) + 1; }
    if (!(RES_MODE === "pre" ? okPre : okPost)) return null;
    // chain evidence: under the pinned mode, this event's implied pre-state must equal the state we derived from the previous event
    const preBase = RES_MODE === "pre" ? B : e.isBuy ? B + b : B - b;
    // A break in the chain (this event does not start where the previous one ended) means something changed the pool that we
    // did not see or mis-derived. The NEW state is still a real observation, but the interval before it is not known: the
    // result is flagged `discontinuity` and the feed restarts the coin's history there, so no order or crossing uses that interval.
    // BOTH sides are enforced. Base: exact (1e-6). Quote: a mismatch above QUOTE_CHAIN_TOL (0.1% of effective reserves, i.e. a
    // price error above 0.1%) is a discontinuity; a smaller one (fee/rounding dust) is counted (chainQuoteMinor, largest seen kept).
    let discontinuity = false;
    if (p.base != null) {
      if (Math.abs(preBase / U - p.base) / p.base < 1e-6) dec.chainOk++; else { dec.chainBad++; discontinuity = true; if (p.afterBoost) dec.chainBadAfterBoost++; }
      const preQuote = RES_MODE === "pre" ? E : e.isBuy ? E - q - lp : E + q - lp;
      if (p.quote != null) { const d = Math.abs(preQuote / 1e9 - p.quote), rel = p.quote > 0 ? d / p.quote : Infinity;
        if (rel > QUOTE_CHAIN_TOL) { dec.chainBadQuote++; discontinuity = true; if (p.afterBoost) dec.chainBadQuoteAfterBoost++; }
        else if (d > Math.max(1e-6 * p.quote, 2e-8)) { dec.chainQuoteMinor++; if (rel > dec.chainQuoteMaxRel) dec.chainQuoteMaxRel = +rel.toExponential(2); } }
    }
    p.afterBoost = false;
    if (RES_MODE === "pre") { p.base = (e.isBuy ? B - b : B + b) / U; p.quote = (e.isBuy ? E + q + lp : E - q + lp) / 1e9; } // the LP fee stays in the pool as liquidity
    else { p.base = B / U; p.quote = E / 1e9; }
    // FEE MEASUREMENT. A rate is installed only when it is a real measurement: from an instruction whose amounts were validated
    // (FEE_IX; sell events carry no instruction name) AND inside 0–10%. Anything else — a multi_hop_swap, a negative rate, a rate
    // above 10% — is a FAILED measurement: counted, never installed, never clamped to a tidy number. The pool keeps the last
    // rate it really showed on that side (or none: fills then say fee_src 'other-side' / 'assumed').
    if (paid > 0) { const f = e.isBuy ? paid / q - 1 : 1 - paid / q, okIx = !e.isBuy || e.ix == null || FEE_IX.has(e.ix) /* an old-format buy event carries no instruction name */, odd = !(f >= 0 && f <= FEE_MAX);
      if (odd) { dec.feeOdd++; const k = (e.ix || "no-ix") + "|" + e.type + (f < 0 ? "|negative" : "|over10%"); dec.feeOddBy[k] = (dec.feeOddBy[k] || 0) + 1; }
      if (okIx && !odd) { if (e.isBuy) p.feeBuy = f; else p.feeSell = f; }
      else { dec.feeNotMeasured++; const k = (e.ix || "no-ix") + "|" + e.type; dec.feeNotMeasuredBy[k] = (dec.feeNotMeasuredBy[k] || 0) + 1; } }
    if (e.baseSupply != null && e.baseSupply > 0n) p.supply = Number(e.baseSupply) / U;
    return { state: { base: p.base, quote: p.quote, feeBuy: p.feeBuy ?? null, feeSell: p.feeSell ?? null, supply: p.supply ?? null }, sol: q / 1e9, changed: true, discontinuity };
  }
  if (e.type === "deposit" || e.type === "withdraw") {
    if (p.vq == null) { dec.unknownVirtual++; return null; } // deposit/withdraw events do not carry the virtual reserves: unknown is not zero
    const s = e.type === "deposit" ? 1 : -1, vq = p.vq;
    const B = Number(e.poolBase) + (RES_MODE === "pre" ? s * Number(e.baseAmt) : 0), Q = Number(e.poolQuote) + (RES_MODE === "pre" ? s * Number(e.quoteAmt) : 0) + vq;
    if (!(B > 0 && Q > 0)) return null;
    p.base = B / U; p.quote = Q / 1e9;
    // a deposit/withdrawal reports the real vaults but adds the virtual reserves WE hold: after a boost those are unconfirmed, so the
    // quarantine continues — only the next swap (which carries its own virtual reserves and is chain-checked) releases it
    return { state: { base: p.base, quote: p.quote, feeBuy: p.feeBuy ?? null, feeSell: p.feeSell ?? null, supply: p.supply ?? null }, sol: 0, changed: true, ...(p.afterBoost ? { approx: "boost" } : {}) };
  }
  if (e.type === "boost") { // effective = real quote reserves + boost reserves; waiting fees are inside virtual with opposite sign, so real_after + virtual is exact up to unswept fees
    p.vq = Number(e.virtualQuote); const Q = Number(e.realQuoteAfter) + p.vq; if (e.baseAfter != null) p.base = Number(e.baseAfter) / U;
    if (e.baseBurned != null && p.supply != null) p.supply = Math.max(0, p.supply - Number(e.baseBurned) / U); // a buy-and-burn reduces supply now, not at the next swap
    p.afterBoost = true; // the next swap's chain check tells us whether this boost state was exact
    if (!(p.base > 0 && Q > 0)) return null; p.quote = Q / 1e9;
    return { state: { base: p.base, quote: p.quote, feeBuy: p.feeBuy ?? null, feeSell: p.feeSell ?? null, supply: p.supply ?? null }, sol: 0, changed: true, approx: "boost" };
  }
  if (e.type === "sweep") { if (p.vq != null) p.vq += Number(e.amount); return { state: null, sol: 0, changed: false }; } // vault −amount, virtual +amount: effective reserves unchanged
  return null;
}

/** All of ONE transaction's events on ONE pool, applied in order → the single COMMITTED state after the last of them.
 *  A transaction is atomic: nobody can trade against the pool between two of its events, so intermediate states are never
 *  observations. Returns { last (applyEvent result or null), sol, buys, sells, user (first buyer), buyers: [[wallet, SOL bought]…]
 *  (EVERY buyer in the transaction, each with its own volume), blockTs, lost, breaks: [events] }. */
export function reduceTx(p, evs, onReset = () => {}) {
  const out = { last: null, sol: 0, buys: 0, sells: 0, user: null, buyers: [], blockTs: null, lost: false, rejected: 0, breaks: [] };
  for (const e of evs) {
    const r = applyEvent(p, e);
    if (!r) { if (e.type !== "sweep") { out.rejected++; out.lost = true; for (const k of Object.keys(p)) delete p[k]; onReset(); out.last = null; out.buyers = []; } continue; }
    if (r.discontinuity) { out.lost = true; out.breaks.push(e); }
    if (!r.changed) continue;
    out.last = r; out.sol += r.sol; if (e.type === "buy") { out.buys++; out.user = out.user || e.user; if (e.user) { const b = out.buyers.find((x) => x[0] === e.user); if (b) b[1] += r.sol; else out.buyers.push([e.user, r.sol]); } } else if (e.type === "sell") out.sells++; out.blockTs = e.ts || out.blockTs;
  }
  return out;
}

// ---- canonical pool check (pump-public-docs FEE_PROGRAM_README): pool.creator == PDA(["pool-authority", base_mint], PUMP_PROGRAM)
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
const P = 2n ** 255n - 19n, D = ((-121665n * modinv(121666n)) % P + P) % P;
function modpow(b, e, m) { let r = 1n; b %= m; while (e > 0n) { if (e & 1n) r = (r * b) % m; b = (b * b) % m; e >>= 1n; } return r; }
function modinv(a) { return modpow(((a % P) + P) % P, P - 2n, P); }
/** is this 32-byte value a valid ed25519 point? (program-derived addresses must NOT be) */
export function onCurve(buf) {
  const yb = Buffer.from(buf); yb[31] &= 0x7f; let y = 0n; for (let i = 31; i >= 0; i--) y = (y << 8n) + BigInt(yb[i]);
  if (y >= P) return false;
  const y2 = (y * y) % P, u = (y2 - 1n + P) % P, v = (D * y2 + 1n) % P, x2 = (u * modinv(v)) % P;
  if (x2 === 0n) return true;
  return modpow(x2, (P - 1n) / 2n, P) === 1n; // Euler's criterion: x² is a quadratic residue
}
export function findProgramAddress(seeds, programId) {
  const pid = bs58dec(programId);
  for (let bump = 255; bump >= 0; bump--) { const h = createHash("sha256").update(Buffer.concat([...seeds, Buffer.from([bump]), pid, Buffer.from("ProgramDerivedAddress")])).digest(); if (!onCurve(h)) return [bs58(h), bump]; }
  throw new Error("no PDA");
}
const pad32 = (b) => (b.length === 32 ? b : Buffer.concat([Buffer.alloc(32 - b.length), b]));
export const pumpPoolAuthority = (mint) => findProgramAddress([Buffer.from("pool-authority"), pad32(bs58dec(mint))], PUMP_PROGRAM)[0];
/** the canonical PumpSwap pool of a pump.fun coin: PDA(["pool", 0u16 LE, pool_authority, base_mint, quote_mint], PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM) */
export function canonicalPool(mint, quote = WSOL) { const auth = pumpPoolAuthority(mint); return findProgramAddress([Buffer.from("pool"), Buffer.from([0, 0]), pad32(bs58dec(auth)), pad32(bs58dec(mint)), pad32(bs58dec(quote))], PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM)[0]; }
export const isCanonical = (mint, poolCreator) => pumpPoolAuthority(mint) === poolCreator;


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/engine.mjs
====================================================================================================
// engine.mjs — ties the feed to series, signals, entries and storage (spec older-2). One collector for every book.
// Input: reconstructed PumpSwap pool states (one per reserve-changing event: buy, sell, deposit, withdraw, boost) and a clock tick.
// The engine never fetches anything itself; providers (creation time, back-fill, SOL/USD, entry verification) are injected.
// Clocks: `t` is OUR receive time (seconds). A signal exists when our engine can use it, so execution delays start there, never at
// the transaction's block time. Both times are stored. Replayed (held) events update history but never create signals.
import * as S from "./spec.mjs";
import { Series, Coverage } from "./series.mjs";
import { newCoin, evaluate } from "./signals.mjs";
import { newEntry, advance, observe, entryDone, inventories, dueAt, unresolvedCount } from "./book.mjs";
import { liquidityUsd, staticImpact } from "./fills.mjs";
import { feeKnown } from "./risk.mjs";
import { parseInfo, judge } from "./gates.mjs";
/** why a coin cannot be bought on the LIVE method (pool-state fills model no transfer fee), or null */
const LAG_EDGES = [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 30, 60, Infinity]; // upper edges (seconds) of the stream-delay histogram kept for diagnostics
const liveBlock = (info) => { const rk = info?.risk; return rk?.status === "restricted" ? `restricted (${(rk.blocking || []).join(", ") || "transfer restriction"})` : rk?.fee_bps > 0 ? `a transfer fee of ${rk.fee_bps} bps, which the live method does not model` : !feeKnown(info) ? "transfer fee not known" : null; };
const ALL_ROWS = S.POLICIES.flatMap((p) => S.STOPS.map((s) => p + "|" + s)), ROWS = ALL_ROWS.length; // the rows of one book on one buy: exit policy × stop

const POLLED_UNSUPPORTED = new Set(["HH-BREAK", "HH-BREAK-4H", ...S.HH_FAST.map((x) => x[0]), ...S.ANY_AGE]), NEVER = () => false;
export class Engine {
  constructor({ store = null, solUsd = () => null, needCreation = () => {}, onFill = () => {}, onEntry = () => {}, log = () => {}, firstStart = null } = {}) {
    this.cov = new Coverage(); this.feedCov = new Coverage(); /* feed + restarts only: what pool-state trust and exit flags depend on */ this.coins = new Map(); this.entries = new Map(); this.byMint = new Map();
    this.store = store; this.solUsd = solUsd; this.needCreation = needCreation; this.onFill = onFill; this.onEntry = onEntry; this.log = log; this.firstStart = firstStart;
    this.stats = { events: 0, swaps: 0, otherPool: 0, noSolUsd: 0, noCreation: 0, evals: 0, cands: 0, entries: 0, notes: 0, replayed: 0, tooOld: 0, lastT: null, evalMsMax: 0 };
    this.dirtyBars = new Map(); this.usdDown = false; this.dirty = new Set(); this.lastCkpt = 0; this.entriesUntil = null; this.onOwnership = () => {};
    this.gates = null; this.readGate = null; this.clock = () => Date.now() / 1000; // older-11: the owner's gates (spec GATES_11) — null = nothing asked, nothing gated; readGate(mint) → the provider's token-info answer; clock = the receive clock the answers are timed on
    this.gateMode = "cancel"; this.readGateLate = null; // older-11: "cancel" = a buy that fails the gates or has no reading in time is cancelled; "label" = every signal is bought as before and carries the verdict (see _gateLabel)
    this.minAgeS = S.MIN_AGE_S; // older-11: the age from which the books that normally start at 8 hours are evaluated in this run (default: 8 hours, as always)
    this.inactive = new Set(S.FAMILIES.filter((f) => !S.BOOKS_LEGACY.includes(f))); // older-11: books that make no new buy in this run (default: the legacy 29 buy, VOL100K does not) — see setBooks
    this.stream = { at: null, lag: null, first: null, open: -Infinity, kind: "connect", need: 0, goodSince: null, promote: false, spans: [], acctAt: null, diag: { n: 0, sum: 0, max: 0, hist: LAG_EDGES.map(() => 0), secs: { current: 0, late: 0, recovering: 0, silence: 0, unknown: 0 }, spells: { late: 0, silence: 0, connect: 0 }, longest: { late: 0, silence: 0, connect: 0 }, recent: [] } }; this.cov.reasons.add("stream"); /* stream freshness: ONE state machine for pricing and for signal coverage — see tx() */ this.rebuy = true; /* spec REBUY: buying again after a sale; false = older-8 */ this.pollCov = new Coverage(); // continuity of PRICE POLLING (the Solana-wide adapter); independent of the PumpSwap WebSocket
    this.stats.prices = 0; this.stats.riskBlocked = 0; this.stats.quotes = 0;
  }
  /** the time the program was away. warm = the price memory was read back (warm.mjs): the signal coverage then closes the gap
   *  itself when the feed is up again (Coverage.resume / up), tolerated or not by its real length. Pool-state trust and exit
   *  flags (feedCov) always treat a restart as a plain gap. */
  restartGap(from, to, { warm = false } = {}) { if (from != null && to > from) { if (!warm) this.cov.gaps.push([from, to]); this.feedCov.gaps.push([from, to]); this.store?.feedEvent(to, warm ? "restart-gap (warm: price memory read back)" : "restart-gap", `${from}`); } }
  /** STREAM FRESHNESS (ninth review B; state machine corrected after Codex's review of 3537ada, findings B1, B3, B4).
   *  `t` is OUR receive time; every decoded transaction also carries its block time, so t − blockTs measures how far behind
   *  the chain the stream is running at that moment (normally 1.0–1.3 s). A stream can stay connected and still be late: on
