FILE: older/src/gates.mjs
====================================================================================================
// gates.mjs — older-11: the owner's holder gates (decisions of 24–25 Nov 2026), read from ONE provider answer per buy signal.
//
// What a reading is. The token-data provider's "token info" answer for the coin (GMGN /v1/token/info, weight 1), asked for at
// the buy signal. Field map verified against the provider's own website panel on 25 Nov 2026 (coin AGmKjJ…Cqxqpump: website
// Top 10 21.87% / Snipers 2.17% / Insiders 0% / Bundler 3.4% / Total Fees 64.72 SOL against 0.2281 / 0.0217 / 0 / 0.0344 /
// 64.56 read 37 minutes earlier):
//   top 10 holders   stat.top_10_holder_rate            fraction 0–1
//   bundlers         stat.top_bundler_trader_percentage fraction 0–1
//   insiders         stat.top_rat_trader_percentage     fraction 0–1 (the provider's "rat traders")
//   snipers          stat.top70_sniper_hold_rate        fraction 0–1
//   total fees       total_fee                          SOL (the "Total Fees" / "F" figure on the provider's pages)
//   Twitter          link.twitter_username              text; a single tweet is stored as "<user>/status/<id>"; "" = none
//   all-time high    ath_price                          USD per token; × 1e9 (a pump.fun coin's initial supply) = market cap,
//                                                       which is how the provider itself states it (dev.ath_token_info.ath_mc)
// A field that is absent, empty or not a number is UNKNOWN (null) — never zero. An unknown figure fails its gate as "missing".
// Nothing here decides alone: engine.mjs applies the verdict to a pending buy at the time of the buy (see _gate).
import { CLOCK } from "./spec.mjs";

// A figure is a number, or text that is a plain decimal number. Anything else — a list, an object, true / false, text that is
// not a number — is UNKNOWN, never coerced (review of bbe1115: Number([]) is 0, so an empty list used to read as "0%").
export const num = (v) => { const n = typeof v === "number" ? v : typeof v === "string" && /^\s*[-+]?(\d+\.?\d*|\.\d+)([eE][-+]?\d+)?\s*$/.test(v) ? Number(v) : NaN; return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null; }; // "1e999" is text that looks like a number and is not a finite one: unknown
export const PUMP_SUPPLY = 1e9; // a pump.fun coin is created with 1,000,000,000 tokens and can only burn from there
/** the figures the gates use, plus what is recorded beside them. `info` is the provider's answer object. */
/** ALL-TIME-HIGH MARKET CAP (review of bbe1115, finding 5). The provider gives the highest PRICE. A market cap needs a supply,
 *  and none is assumed: `supply` is what the chain reported with the coin's latest swap; failing that, the provider's own
 *  total supply. The figure used is price × that supply, and only when the supply is no more than a pump.fun coin's initial
 *  1,000,000,000 — such a coin can only have burned tokens since its high, so the result is a LOWER bound of the true figure
 *  (never an overstatement; a few per cent under the provider's website figure, which multiplies by the full billion, when
 *  tokens have been burned). No supply evidence, or a supply above the initial one (tokens were minted): UNKNOWN — which fails
 *  the "$100K" gate as missing. The basis is recorded with the reading. */
export function athUsd(athPrice, supplyChain, supplyProvider, pump = false) {
  // burn-only is a property of a verified pump.fun coin, not of "a supply under a billion" (another token could have minted from
  // 100M to 900M after its high): without that evidence the market cap at the high is not established (second opinion, 7)
  if (!pump) return { usd: null, basis: "not a verified pump.fun coin: the market cap at the high is not established" };
  const s = supplyChain > 0 ? supplyChain : supplyProvider > 0 ? supplyProvider : null, src = supplyChain > 0 ? "chain (latest swap)" : supplyProvider > 0 ? "provider total_supply" : null;
  if (athPrice == null) return { usd: null, basis: "no all-time-high price in the reading" };
  if (s == null) return { usd: null, basis: "no supply evidence" };
  if (s > PUMP_SUPPLY * (1 + 1e-6)) return { usd: null, basis: `supply ${Math.round(s)} (${src}) is above 1,000,000,000: tokens were minted, the market cap at the high is not established` };
  return { usd: athPrice * s, basis: `highest price × supply ${Math.round(s)} (${src}) — a lower bound` };
}
export function parseInfo(info, supplyChain = null, pump = false) {
  const st = info?.stat ?? null, link = info?.link ?? null, w = info?.wallet_tags_stat ?? null, ath = athUsd(num(info?.ath_price), num(supplyChain), num(info?.total_supply), pump);
  const tw = link && typeof link.twitter_username === "string" ? link.twitter_username.trim() : null; // link block absent = unknown; "" = known: none
  return {
    top10: num(st?.top_10_holder_rate), bundlers: num(st?.top_bundler_trader_percentage), insiders: num(st?.top_rat_trader_percentage), snipers: num(st?.top70_sniper_hold_rate),
    fees_sol: num(info?.total_fee), twitter: tw, ath_usd: ath.usd, ath_basis: ath.basis, ath_price: num(info?.ath_price),
    // recorded, not gated (owner: "everything else recorded with time since these figures change")
    trade_fee_sol: num(info?.trade_fee), holders: num(info?.holder_count ?? st?.holder_count), dev_hold: num(st?.dev_team_hold_rate), creator_hold: num(st?.creator_hold_rate),
    creator_status: info?.dev?.creator_token_status ?? null, sniper_wallets: num(w?.sniper_wallets), insider_wallets: num(w?.rat_trader_wallets), bundler_wallets: num(w?.bundler_wallets),
    total_supply: num(info?.total_supply), created: num(info?.creation_timestamp), pool_opened: num(info?.open_timestamp), launchpad: info?.launchpad ?? null,
  };
}
const pct = (x) => (x * 100).toFixed(x * 100 < 10 && x !== 0 ? 2 : 1).replace(/\.?0+$/, "") + "%";
const inside = (v, [lo, hi], exHi = false) => v >= lo && (hi == null || (exHi ? v < hi : v <= hi));
const NAMES = { top10: "top 10 holders", bundlers: "bundlers", insiders: "insiders", snipers: "snipers" };
/** the gate settings that apply to one book: the run's gates with the book's own overrides (a clock book uses its base book's) */
export function gatesFor(gates, family) { const f = CLOCK[family]?.base ?? family, o = gates.byFamily?.[f]; return o ? { ...gates, ...o } : gates; }
/** verdict of one reading for one book. { pass, fails: [text], missing: [name], options } — options are the owner's main /
 *  second ranges for bundlers and insiders, true / false / null (figure unknown), recorded on every buy that is filled. */
export function judge(reading, gates, family) {
  const G = gatesFor(gates, family), fails = [], missing = [], r = reading ?? {};
  for (const k of ["top10", "bundlers", "insiders", "snipers"]) { const g = G[k]; if (!g) continue; const v = r[k];
    if (v == null) { missing.push(NAMES[k]); continue; }
    if (!inside(v, g.buy, !!g.exclusiveHi)) fails.push(v < g.buy[0] ? `${NAMES[k]} ${pct(v)}: below ${pct(g.buy[0])}` : `${NAMES[k]} ${pct(v)}: ${g.exclusiveHi ? "not under" : "above"} ${pct(g.buy[1])}`); }
  if (G.fees_sol) { if (r.fees_sol == null) missing.push("total fees"); else if (!inside(r.fees_sol, G.fees_sol.buy)) fails.push(`total fees ${+r.fees_sol.toFixed(2)} SOL: below ${G.fees_sol.buy[0]} SOL`); }
  if (G.twitter) { if (r.twitter == null) missing.push("Twitter link"); else if (!r.twitter) fails.push("no Twitter link"); }
  if (G.ath_usd) { if (r.ath_usd == null) missing.push("all-time high"); else if (!inside(r.ath_usd, G.ath_usd.buy)) fails.push(`all-time high $${Math.round(r.ath_usd / 1000)}K: has not reached $${Math.round(G.ath_usd.buy[0] / 1000)}K`); }
  if (G.bothZeroBlocks && r.bundlers === 0 && r.insiders === 0 && !fails.some((x) => /^bundlers|^insiders/.test(x))) fails.push("bundlers 0% and insiders 0%");
  const opt = (k, which) => (G[k]?.[which] ? (r[k] == null ? null : inside(r[k], G[k][which])) : undefined);
  const options = { bundlers_main: opt("bundlers", "main"), bundlers_second: opt("bundlers", "second"), insiders_main: opt("insiders", "main"), insiders_second: opt("insiders", "second") };
  for (const k of Object.keys(options)) if (options[k] === undefined) delete options[k];
  return { pass: !fails.length && !missing.length, fails, missing, options };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/gmgn.mjs
====================================================================================================
// gmgn.mjs — GMGN OpenAPI client for this project (read-only). Auth as in the tracker's client: header X-APIKEY + query
// timestamp + client_id. The key is read from the environment (GMGN_API_KEY) and is never logged or stored.
// Pacing: a leaky bucket in "weight per second" (GMGN_WPS). The limit of OUR key is read from the response headers
// (X-RateLimit-*) and shown on the status page. Plan weights (GMGN plan page, 20 Nov 2026): Free 5, Plus 20, Pro 50.
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
const HOST = process.env.GMGN_HOST || "https://openapi.gmgn.ai", KEY = () => process.env.GMGN_API_KEY || "";
const WPS = Number(process.env.GMGN_WPS || 4); // our key is on the FREE plan: 5 weight/s (verified 20 Nov 2026, x-ratelimit-limit: 5); 4 leaves headroom
export const WEIGHT = { "/v1/market/token_kline": 2, "/v1/market/rank": 3, "/v1/trenches": 2, "/v1/token/info": 1, "/v1/token/security": 1 }; // documented weights
export const gm = { calls: 0, errors: 0, limited: 0, banned: 0, lastErr: "", headers: {}, lastOk: null,
  first: { calls: 0, ok: 0, refused: 0, not_sent: 0, ms_sum: 0, ms_max: 0 }, held_back: 0, reserve: 0 }; // older-11: "first" = buy-check readings (see below)
let next = 0, pausedUntil = 0; const nap = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
// older-11 — TWO KINDS OF CALL share this key's allowance:
//   first : a reading for a pending buy (gcall(..., { first: true })). It is never queued behind anything and never paced: it
//           goes out at once. If the provider has told us to wait (a refusal's reset time) for longer than maxWaitMs, it is
//           NOT sent and fails at once ("allowance exhausted") — a reading that cannot arrive in time is of no use to the buy.
//   rest  : history repair and everything else, in order, paced at GMGN_WPS as before. When gm.reserve > 0 (set by a run that
//           uses the gates) such a call is held back while the allowance the provider last reported, minus the call's weight,
//           is below the reserve — so a buy-check that arrives finds units left. Once the reported reset time has passed the
//           allowance is taken as refilled. With gm.reserve = 0 (the default) the rest behaves exactly as in older-10.
// What the provider's allowance really is (5 units per second on the plan page; refusals with a reset about 15 s ahead in
// practice, 25 Nov 2026) is not assumed here: the client only uses the remaining / reset figures of the latest answer.
let chain = Promise.resolve(), remaining = null, resetAt = 0;
function slot(path) { const w = WEIGHT[path] ?? 3; const p = chain.then(async () => {
    for (;;) { if (Date.now() < pausedUntil) { await nap(Math.min(500, pausedUntil - Date.now() + 5)); continue; }
      const d = next - Date.now(); if (d > 0) { await nap(Math.min(d, 250)); continue; }
      if (gm.reserve > 0 && remaining != null && Date.now() < resetAt && remaining - w < gm.reserve) { gm.held_back++; await nap(Math.min(250, resetAt - Date.now() + 5)); continue; }
      break; }
    next = Math.max(Date.now(), next) + (w / WPS) * 1000; }); chain = p.catch(() => {}); return p; }
function note(r) { for (const [k, v] of r.headers) if (/ratelimit|quota|plan|tier/i.test(k)) gm.headers[k] = v; // limit evidence only — never auth headers
  const rem = Number(r.headers.get("x-ratelimit-remaining")), rs = Number(r.headers.get("x-ratelimit-reset")); // ONE SHARED COUNT for calls in flight: an answer to an earlier call reports the allowance as it was when THAT call was
  // served; calls sent since are already counted here, so inside one window the lower figure stands. A new window (a later reset time) starts from the answer.
  const has = Number.isFinite(rem) && r.headers.get("x-ratelimit-remaining") != null, newWin = rs > 1e9 && rs * 1000 > resetAt + 500;
  if (has) remaining = newWin || remaining == null ? rem : Math.min(remaining, rem); if (rs > 1e9) resetAt = rs * 1000; }
export const gmgnOn = () => !!KEY();
export async function gcall(path, params = {}, { method = "GET", body = null, first = false, maxWaitMs = 0 } = {}) {
  if (!KEY()) throw new Error("no GMGN key configured");
  const t0 = Date.now();
  // a reading is not sent into an allowance the provider has just reported as used up (review of bbe1115): it would be refused,
  // and refusals are what the provider answers with longer bans. "Used up" = the units left after the calls sent since that
  // report are fewer than this call's weight, and the reported reset time has not passed.
  if (first) { gm.first.calls++; const now = Date.now(), w = WEIGHT[path] ?? 3, wait = Math.max(pausedUntil - now, remaining != null && remaining < w && now < resetAt ? resetAt - now : 0); if (wait > maxWaitMs) { gm.first.not_sent++; throw new Error(`GMGN allowance exhausted for another ${(wait / 1000).toFixed(1)} s`); } if (wait > 0) await nap(wait);
    if (Date.now() >= resetAt) remaining = null; // the reported window is over: the allowance is not known until the next answer
    next = Math.max(Date.now(), next) + ((WEIGHT[path] ?? 3) / WPS) * 1000; if (remaining != null) remaining -= WEIGHT[path] ?? 3; }
  else { await slot(path); if (remaining != null && Date.now() < resetAt) remaining -= WEIGHT[path] ?? 3; } // every call that is sent is counted against the last reported allowance, whichever kind it is
  gm.calls++;
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ ...(method === "GET" ? params : params.chain ? { chain: params.chain } : {}), timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), client_id: randomUUID() });
  const r = await fetch(`${HOST}${path}?${q}`, { method, headers: { "X-APIKEY": KEY(), "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: method === "POST" ? JSON.stringify(body ?? params) : undefined, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000) });
  note(r);
  const text = await r.text(); let j; try { j = JSON.parse(text); } catch { gm.errors++; throw new Error(`GMGN HTTP ${r.status}`); }
  if (r.status === 429 || /RATE_LIMIT/.test(j.error || "")) { gm.limited++; if (/BANNED/.test(j.error || "")) gm.banned++; const reset = Number(r.headers.get("x-ratelimit-reset")); pausedUntil = reset > 1e9 ? reset * 1000 + 500 : Date.now() + 5000; if (first) gm.first.refused++; throw new Error("GMGN rate limited"); }
  if (j.code !== 0) { gm.errors++; gm.lastErr = `${path}: ${j.error || j.code} ${j.message || ""}`.slice(0, 160); throw new Error(gm.lastErr); }
  gm.lastOk = Date.now(); if (first) { const ms = gm.lastOk - t0; gm.first.ok++; gm.first.ms_sum += ms; if (ms > gm.first.ms_max) gm.first.ms_max = ms; }
  return j.data && j.data.data && typeof j.data.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(j.data.data) && j.data.code !== undefined ? j.data.data : j.data;
}
const N = (v) => (v == null || v === "" || isNaN(Number(v)) ? null : Number(v));

/** older-11: the reading for a pending buy — one token-info answer, sent ahead of everything else (see "first" above) */
export const tokenInfoFirst = (mint, maxWaitMs = 300) => gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: mint }, { first: true, maxWaitMs });
/** older-11, label mode: the same reading as an ORDINARY queued call (waits its turn and for the allowance) — used once when the first request failed, so a buy gets a late label rather than none */
export const tokenInfoLate = (mint) => gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: mint });
/** test hook: forget pacing and allowance state */
export function _reset() { next = 0; pausedUntil = 0; remaining = null; resetAt = 0; chain = Promise.resolve(); }
/** token creation time (unix s) — GMGN token info; null if GMGN does not give one */
export async function creationTime(mint) {
  const i = await gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: mint });
  let v = N(i?.creation_timestamp ?? i?.open_timestamp ?? i?.created_at); if (v > 1e12) v = Math.floor(v / 1000);
  return v ? { t: v, src: i?.creation_timestamp != null ? "gmgn.creation_timestamp" : "gmgn.open_timestamp (UNVERIFIED: must be shown to mean token creation, not pool opening)" } : null;
}
/** 1-minute USD bars in [from, to) (unix s); GMGN returns ≤ 100 candles a call, so this walks in 100-minute steps */
export async function minuteBars(mint, from, to) {
  const out = [];
  for (let a = from; a < to; a += 100 * 60) {
    const b = Math.min(to, a + 100 * 60);
    const d = await gcall("/v1/market/token_kline", { chain: "sol", address: mint, resolution: "1m", from: String(a * 1000), to: String(b * 1000) });
    for (const c of d?.list || (Array.isArray(d) ? d : [])) { const t = Math.floor(Number(c.time) / 1000); if (t >= from && t < to && N(c.close) > 0) out.push({ m: Math.floor(t / 60), o: N(c.open) ?? N(c.close), h: N(c.high), l: N(c.low), c: N(c.close), v: N(c.volume) ?? 0 }); }
  }
  return out.sort((x, y) => x.m - y.m).filter((x, i, a) => i === 0 || x.m !== a[i - 1].m);
}

FILE: older/src/inventory.mjs
====================================================================================================
// inventory.mjs — initial discovery of EXISTING canonical PumpSwap pools (a live feed only sees future trades).
// Pages through the PumpSwap program's SOL-quoted pool accounts (quote mint at offset 75; first 261 bytes: token, creator, vaults,
// virtual reserves) and prices each page straight away from the vaults' balances (getMultipleAccounts, 100 accounts a call), so
// memory stays bounded however many pools exist. Uses Helius' paginated getProgramAccountsV2 when the RPC offers it, else the
// plain call (which can be too large for a busy program — a failure is reported, never hidden: the feed then resolves pools as
// they trade, and the page shows the inventory as missing).
// Pump.fun origin is VERIFIED, not assumed: a pool is canonical when pool.creator equals the pump pool-authority PDA of its base
// mint (pump-public-docs). Non-canonical pools are counted. Coins at or above INV_MIN_MC_USD (default $5K) become the watch population.
import { rpc, parsePool, poolClass } from "./feed.mjs";
import { WSOL, PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM } from "./decoder.mjs";

export async function inventory({ store, solUsd, minMc = Number(process.env.INV_MIN_MC_USD || 5000), maxPools = Number(process.env.INV_MAX_POOLS || 0), log = console.log }) {
  const rep = { at: new Date().toISOString(), method: null, pages: 0, pools: 0, solPools: 0, canonical: 0, nonCanonical: 0, pumpSuffix: 0, priced: 0, aboveMin: 0, inBand: 0, errors: 0, minMc, complete: false };
  const su = solUsd(), watch = [], cfg = { encoding: "base64", dataSlice: { offset: 0, length: 261 }, filters: [{ memcmp: { offset: 75, bytes: WSOL } }] };
  async function page(accts) {
    rep.pages++; rep.pools += accts.length; const list = [];
    for (const a of accts) { const p = parsePool(a.account.data[0]); if (!p || p.quoteMint !== WSOL) continue; rep.solPools++; const info = { mint: p.baseMint, quote: p.quoteMint, creator: p.creator }; store.savePool(a.pubkey, info); if (poolClass(info) !== "canonical") { rep.nonCanonical++; continue; } rep.canonical++; list.push({ pool: a.pubkey, ...p }); }
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 50) {
      const chunk = list.slice(i, i + 50), keys = chunk.flatMap((p) => [p.poolBaseAta, p.poolQuoteAta]);
      let res; try { res = await rpc("getMultipleAccounts", [keys, { encoding: "base64", dataSlice: { offset: 64, length: 8 } }]); } catch { rep.errors++; continue; }
      chunk.forEach((p, j) => {
        const b = res.value[2 * j], q = res.value[2 * j + 1]; if (!b || !q) return;
        const base = Number(Buffer.from(b.data[0], "base64").readBigUInt64LE(0)) / 1e6, quote = Number(Buffer.from(q.data[0], "base64").readBigUInt64LE(0) + (p.virtualQuote ?? 0n)) / 1e9; // effective quote reserves
        if (!(base > 0 && quote > 0)) return; rep.priced++; if (p.baseMint.endsWith("pump")) rep.pumpSuffix++;
        const mc = (quote / base) * 1e9 * su; // supply taken as 1e9 here for screening only; the live feed uses each event's base_supply
        if (mc >= minMc) { rep.aboveMin++; if (mc >= 40000 && mc <= 1e6) rep.inBand++; watch.push({ pool: p.pool, mint: p.baseMint, mc }); store.saveCoin(p.baseMint, { pool: p.pool, pumpOrigin: "pump.fun", info: { population: "pumpswap-canonical", origin: "pump.fun", origin_evidence: "canonical pool: creator = pump pool-authority PDA", venues: ["PumpSwap"], discovery_source: "program accounts inventory", price_source: "pool events", monitoring_mode: "live" } }); }
      });
    }
  }
  try { // paginated (Helius getProgramAccountsV2)
    let key = null; rep.method = "getProgramAccountsV2 (paginated)";
    do { const r = await rpc("getProgramAccountsV2", [PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM, { ...cfg, limit: 5000, ...(key ? { paginationKey: key } : {}) }]); const accts = r?.accounts || []; if (!accts.length) break; await page(accts); key = r.paginationKey || null; if (maxPools && rep.pools >= maxPools) { rep.stopped = `INV_MAX_POOLS=${maxPools}`; break; } } while (key);
    rep.complete = !key && !rep.stopped;
  } catch (e) {
    if (rep.pages) rep.error = "pagination stopped: " + String(e.message).slice(0, 120);
    else try { rep.method = "getProgramAccounts (single call)"; await page(await rpc("getProgramAccounts", [PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM, cfg])); rep.complete = true; } catch (e2) { rep.error = "inventory unavailable: " + String(e2.message).slice(0, 120); }
  }
  store.db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta VALUES ('inventory', ?)").run(JSON.stringify(rep));
  log(`[inventory] ${JSON.stringify(rep)}`);
  return { rep, watch };
}

FILE: older/src/jupiter.mjs

// jupiter.mjs — Jupiter API client (read-only: prices, token metadata/risk, routed QUOTES). Never signs, never calls /execute.
// Docs read 20 Nov 2026 (developers.jup.ag): base https://api.jup.ag, header x-api-key (optional; keyless = 0.5 req/s).
//   GET /price/v3?ids=…            ≤ 50 mints  → { mint: { usdPrice, liquidity, createdAt, decimals, blockId, priceChange24h } }; unreliable/inactive mints are OMITTED
//   GET /tokens/v2/search?query=…  ≤ 100 mints → identity, tokenProgram, audit flags, mcap, fdv, liquidity, holderCount, 5m/1h/6h/24h stats   (10 credits)
//   GET /tokens/v2/recent                       → mints ordered by first-pool creation
//   GET /tokens/v2/{category}/{interval}        → toptrending | toptraded | toporganicscore                                                    (5 credits)
//   GET /swap/v2/order?inputMint&outputMint&amount   (no `taker` → quote only, no transaction)                                               (1 credit)
// One shared rate bucket for all of the above (Keyless 0.5/s, Free 1/s, Developer 10/s, Launch 50/s). JUP_RPS sets our pacing.
// Field names of /order beyond inAmount/outAmount/feeBps/router are read defensively and the raw reply's keys are recorded,
// because the full response schema could not be fetched from this workspace (marked UNVERIFIED in docs/REVIEW.md).
export const WSOL = "So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112";
const BASE = () => process.env.JUP_BASE || "https://api.jup.ag";
export const CREDITS = { price: 1, search: 10, recent: 1, category: 5, order: 1 };
export const ju = { calls: {}, credits: 0, errors: 0, limited: 0, lastErr: "", omitted: 0, requested: 0, lastOk: null, headers: {} };
let fetchImpl = (...a) => fetch(...a), next = 0;
export const setFetch = (f) => { fetchImpl = f; };
const nap = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const rps = () => Number(process.env.JUP_RPS || (process.env.JUP_API_KEY ? 1 : 0.5));
// Pacing respects BOTH limits: requests per second, and the plan's monthly credits (endpoints cost 1–50 credits each, so a
// request cap alone does not protect the allowance). JUP_CREDITS_MONTH (e.g. 25000000 on Developer) spreads credits evenly.
const creditsPerSec = () => (Number(process.env.JUP_CREDITS_MONTH || 0) > 0 ? Number(process.env.JUP_CREDITS_MONTH) / (30 * 86400) : Infinity);
// PRIORITY LANES on the one rate bucket: an executable quote for a scheduled order is never queued behind background work.
//   0 = routed quotes for orders that are DUE (a scheduled buy or sale waiting for its price)
//   1 = price samples AND routine position valuations, first come first served — measured live on the free key (20 Nov, 19:02 UK):
//       with valuations in lane 0 they took the whole allowance (269 quotes against 11 price requests in 5 minutes) and price sampling stalled
//   2 = discovery / metadata · 3 = background measurements
// A request that has waited is promoted one lane per 10 s (so background work is delayed, never starved) but nothing is ever
// promoted into lane 0.
const PRIO = { order: 0, price: 1, recent: 2, category: 2, search: 2 }; const waiting = []; let pumping = false, seq = 0;
async function pump() { if (pumping) return; pumping = true; try { while (waiting.length) { const d = next - Date.now(); if (d > 0) await nap(d);
  const now = Date.now(), eff = (w) => (w.prio === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(1, w.prio - Math.floor((now - w.at) / 10000))); waiting.sort((a, b) => eff(a) - eff(b) || a.seq - b.seq);
  const w = waiting.shift(); next = Math.max(Date.now(), next) + 1000 * Math.max(1 / rps(), (CREDITS[w.kind] ?? 1) / creditsPerSec()); w.go(); } } finally { pumping = false; } }
function slot(kind, prio = PRIO[kind] ?? 2) { return new Promise((go) => { waiting.push({ kind, prio, seq: seq++, at: Date.now(), go }); pump(); }); }
export const queueDepth = () => waiting.length;
async function get(kind, path, { paced = true, prio } = {}) {
  if (paced) await slot(kind, prio);
  ju.calls[kind] = (ju.calls[kind] || 0) + 1; ju.credits += CREDITS[kind] ?? 1;
  const req = Date.now() / 1000;
  let r; try { r = await fetchImpl(BASE() + path, { headers: process.env.JUP_API_KEY ? { "x-api-key": process.env.JUP_API_KEY } : {}, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000) }); } catch (e) { ju.errors++; ju.lastErr = `${kind}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 160); throw e; }
  for (const [k, v] of r.headers || []) if (/ratelimit|credit|retry-after/i.test(k)) ju.headers[k] = v;
  if (r.status === 429) { ju.limited++; next = Date.now() + 2000; throw new Error("jupiter rate limited"); }
  let j; try { j = await r.json(); } catch { ju.errors++; throw new Error(`jupiter ${kind} HTTP ${r.status}`); }
  if (r.status >= 400) { ju.errors++; ju.lastErr = `${kind}: HTTP ${r.status} ${j?.error || j?.message || ""}`.slice(0, 160); const e = new Error(ju.lastErr); e.status = r.status; e.body = j; throw e; }
  ju.lastOk = Date.now() / 1000;
  return { j, req, resp: Date.now() / 1000 };
}
const num = (v) => (v == null || v === "" || isNaN(Number(v)) ? null : Number(v));

/** batched current prices. Returns { t, req, prices: Map(mint → {px, liqUsd, createdAt, decimals, blockId}), omitted: [mint…] }.
 *  A mint missing from the reply is MISSING COVERAGE — not a zero price, not a safe token, not a strategy failure. */
export async function prices(mints) {
  if (mints.length > 50) throw new Error("≤ 50 mints per price request");
  const { j, req, resp } = await get("price", `/price/v3?ids=${mints.join(",")}`);
  const out = new Map(), omitted = [];
  for (const m of mints) { const x = j?.[m]; const px = num(x?.usdPrice); if (px > 0) out.set(m, { px, liqUsd: num(x.liquidity), createdAt: x.createdAt ? Math.floor(Date.parse(x.createdAt) / 1000) || num(x.createdAt) : null, decimals: num(x.decimals), blockId: num(x.blockId) }); else omitted.push(m); }
  ju.requested += mints.length; ju.omitted += omitted.length;
  return { t: resp, req, prices: out, omitted };
}
/** token metadata + risk indicators for ≤ 100 mints */
export async function tokens(mints) {
  if (mints.length > 100) throw new Error("≤ 100 mints per search");
  const { j } = await get("search", `/tokens/v2/search?query=${mints.join(",")}`);
  return (Array.isArray(j) ? j : []).map(normToken);
}
const numsOf = (o) => { if (!o || typeof o !== "object") return null; const out = {}; for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(o)) if (typeof v !== "boolean" && num(v) != null) out[k] = num(v); return out; };
/** RECORDED MEASUREMENT for one token at an entry: holder count and the provider's own 5-minute and 1-hour statistics, kept
 *  with the request and reply times. The statistics objects are stored as returned (numeric fields only): their field names
 *  are the provider's, not ours, and nothing here is used as a filter. */
export async function tokenStats(mint) {
  const { j, req, resp } = await get("search", `/tokens/v2/search?query=${mint}`, { prio: 3 }); // background lane: never ahead of a quote or a price sample
  const x = (Array.isArray(j) ? j : []).find((y) => y.id === mint);
  if (!x) return { ok: false, req, t: resp, why: "token not returned" };
  return { ok: true, req, t: resp, holders: num(x.holderCount), top_holders_pct: num(x.audit?.topHoldersPercentage), organic: num(x.organicScore), liq_usd: num(x.liquidity), mcap: num(x.mcap), stats5m: numsOf(x.stats5m), stats1h: numsOf(x.stats1h), updated: x.updatedAt ?? null };
}
export async function recent() { const { j } = await get("recent", "/tokens/v2/recent"); return (Array.isArray(j) ? j : []).map(normToken); }
export async function category(cat, interval, limit = 100) { const { j } = await get("category", `/tokens/v2/${cat}/${interval}?limit=${limit}`); return (Array.isArray(j) ? j : []).map(normToken); }
export function normToken(x) {
  const a = x.audit || {};
  return { mint: x.id, symbol: x.symbol ?? null, decimals: num(x.decimals), token_program: x.tokenProgram ?? null, px: num(x.usdPrice), mcap: num(x.mcap), fdv: num(x.fdv), liq_usd: num(x.liquidity), holders: num(x.holderCount),
    circ_supply: num(x.circSupply), total_supply: num(x.totalSupply), launchpad: x.launchpad ?? null, first_pool: x.firstPool ? { id: x.firstPool.id ?? null, created: x.firstPool.createdAt ? Math.floor(Date.parse(x.firstPool.createdAt) / 1000) : null } : null,
    mint_authority_disabled: a.mintAuthorityDisabled ?? null, freeze_authority_disabled: a.freezeAuthorityDisabled ?? null, top_holders_pct: num(a.topHoldersPercentage), is_sus: a.isSus ?? null, organic: num(x.organicScore), keys: Object.keys(x) };
}
/** a routed quote, exact size. side "buy": spend `amount` lamports of SOL for the token; "sell": sell `amount` raw token units for SOL.
 *  Returns { ok, req, t, in, out (raw units), router, route: [labels], feeBps, impact, keys } or { ok: false, why } — a missing route stays visible. */
export async function quote(mint, side, amountRaw, { urgent = true } = {}) {
  const [i, o] = side === "buy" ? [WSOL, mint] : [mint, WSOL];
  try {
    const { j, req, resp } = await get("order", `/swap/v2/order?inputMint=${i}&outputMint=${o}&amount=${BigInt(Math.floor(amountRaw))}`, { paced: true, prio: urgent ? 0 : 1 }); // lane 0 only for an order that is DUE; a routine valuation shares lane 1 with price samples
    const out = num(j?.outAmount), inn = num(j?.inAmount) ?? Number(amountRaw);
    if (!(out > 0)) return { ok: false, req, t: resp, why: (j?.errorMessage || j?.error || "no route / no output amount").toString().slice(0, 160), keys: Object.keys(j || {}) };
    const route = Array.isArray(j.routePlan) ? j.routePlan.map((r) => r?.swapInfo?.label ?? r?.label ?? "?") : null;
    return { ok: true, req, t: resp, in: inn, out, router: j.router ?? null, route, feeBps: num(j.feeBps), platformFee: j.platformFee ?? null, impact: num(j.priceImpactPct ?? j.priceImpact), keys: Object.keys(j) };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, t: Date.now() / 1000, why: String(e.message).slice(0, 160), status: e.status ?? null }; }
}

// main.mjs — older-coin paper project (spec older-2). PAPER ONLY: nothing here can sign or send a transaction.
// Env (values are never printed): OLDER_ENV file (default /root/older/.env), then /root/tracker/.env for keys already on the server.
//   RPC_WS / RPC_HTTP (any Solana RPC with WebSockets) — or HELIUS_API_KEY, from which both are built; GMGN_API_KEY;
//   OLDER_DB (default /root/older/older.db), OLDER_PORT (8793), OLDER_SITE_KEY (optional), OLDER_RES_MODE (pre|post), cost figures.
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
for (const f of [process.env.OLDER_ENV || "/root/older/.env", "/root/tracker/.env"]) if (existsSync(f)) for (const l of readFileSync(f, "utf8").split("\n")) { const m = l.match(/^\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*(.*)\s*$/); if (m && process.env[m[1]] === undefined) process.env[m[1]] = m[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, ""); }

// the diagnostic account read applies to entries priced from a reconstructed pool state; routed-quote entries carry their quote evidence instead

{ const { addClockBook } = await import("./migrate.mjs"); for (const f of ["HH-BREAK-4H"]) { const r = addClockBook(store, f, now0); if (r.added) log(`[older] ${f}: ${r.added} existing ${r.base} buys carried into the new book (${r.exact} versions unchanged, ${r.estimated} clock sales estimated from minute bars, ${r.live} still inside the clock)`); } }
engine.rebuy = process.env.OLDER_REBUY !== "0"; // buying again (older-9) — OLDER_REBUY=0 switches it off for a run

if (process.env.OLDER_GATES) { if (process.env.OLDER_GATES !== "older-11") throw new Error(`unknown OLDER_GATES "${process.env.OLDER_GATES}"`);
  engine.gates = GATES_11; engine.readGate = (mint) => tokenInfoFirst(mint, Number(process.env.GMGN_FIRST_WAIT_MS ?? 300)); gm.reserve = Number(process.env.GMGN_RESERVE ?? 2);
  // LABEL (default) or CANCEL: OLDER_GATE_MODE=label → every signal is bought as before and labelled with the verdict (the
  // baseline stays intact and the checks are compared on the same buys); =cancel → a buy that fails or has no reading is cancelled.
  const gmode = (process.env.OLDER_GATE_MODE || "").trim() || "label"; if (gmode !== "label" && gmode !== "cancel") throw new Error(`unknown OLDER_GATE_MODE "${gmode}"`);
  engine.gateMode = gmode; if (gmode === "label") engine.readGateLate = (mint) => tokenInfoLate(mint);

store.since(now0); // "counting since" is fixed at START-UP, when a book or stop first exists on this database — not at the first page view
store.restore(engine);
// WARM RESTART (warm.mjs): after a planned stop the price memory is read back, so the hour-scale books do not start empty.
// The time away stays an unobserved gap. Not used by a bounded validation run.
const { saveWarm, loadWarm } = await import("./warm.mjs"); const WARM_FILE = (process.env.OLDER_DB || "/root/older/older.db") + ".warm";
// older-11, A NEW RUN ON A NEW DATABASE: the price memory the previous run wrote at its stop can be carried in once
// (OLDER_WARM_FROM = that run's snapshot file), so the hour-scale books do not wait hours for history. Only on the very first
// start of this database (it has never been alive) and only when it has no snapshot of its own. What the other run decided
// (its one-buy marks) is NOT carried — warm.mjs, `fresh`. The same 60-second limit applies; older than that = cold start.
const carry = !existsSync(WARM_FILE) && process.env.OLDER_WARM_FROM && existsSync(process.env.OLDER_WARM_FROM) && store.lastAlive() == null;
const warm = process.env.OLDER_RUN_MINUTES || process.env.OLDER_WARM === "0" ? { loaded: false, why: "switched off" } : carry ? loadWarm(process.env.OLDER_WARM_FROM, engine, Date.now() / 1000, { fresh: true }) : loadWarm(WARM_FILE, engine);
log(warm.loaded ? `[older] warm ${carry ? "start of a new run (price memory carried from the previous run; none of its buys or one-buy marks)" : "restart"}: price memory of ${warm.coins} coins read back, ${warm.age_s} s after the stop${warm.skipped ? ` (${warm.skipped} skipped: observation method changed)` : ""}` : `[older] cold start: ${warm.why}`);
engine.restartGap(store.lastAlive(), now0, { warm: warm.loaded });
// planned stop (pm2 restart/stop sends SIGINT): every open position's peak is saved and the last-alive time is written first,
// so a planned restart loses nothing and its gap is measured from the real stop
let stopping = false; for (const sig of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM"]) process.on(sig, () => { if (stopping) return; stopping = true; const tStop = Date.now() / 1000; try { const n = store.tx(() => engine.flush()); store.heartbeat(tStop); log(`[older] ${sig}: ${n} open positions saved, stopping`); } catch (e) { log("[older] save on stop failed: " + e.message); }
  if (!process.env.OLDER_RUN_MINUTES && process.env.OLDER_WARM !== "0") try { const w = saveWarm(WARM_FILE, engine, tStop); log(`[older] price memory saved for the next start: ${w.coins} coins, ${(w.bytes / 1e6).toFixed(1)} MB, ${w.ms} ms`); } catch (e) { log("[older] price memory NOT saved (the next start will be cold): " + e.message); }
  process.exit(0); });
// the entry window: OLDER_ENTRIES_UNTIL (unix seconds, or an ISO date) — after it nothing new is opened; open positions, their
// exits and the 24-hour runner tracking carry on until every included position is complete
if (process.env.OLDER_ENTRIES_UNTIL && process.env.OLDER_ENTRIES_UNTIL.trim()) { const v = process.env.OLDER_ENTRIES_UNTIL; engine.entriesUntil = /^\d+$/.test(v) ? Number(v) : Date.parse(v) / 1000; log(`[older] entry window closes ${new Date(engine.entriesUntil * 1000).toISOString()}`); }
let COMMIT = process.env.OLDER_COMMIT || null; try { const { execSync } = await import("node:child_process"); COMMIT = COMMIT || execSync("git rev-parse HEAD", { cwd: new URL(".", import.meta.url).pathname, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] }).toString().trim(); } catch {}
store.db.prepare("INSERT INTO feed (t, ev, detail) VALUES (?,?,?)").run(now0, "start", `commit ${COMMIT || "unknown"}`);
await startSolUsd();
const J = await import("./jupiter.mjs");
// HISTORY REPAIR CAN BE SWITCHED OFF WITHOUT A RESTART (second opinion, 4: an isolated measurement of the provider's allowance
// needs the only other consumer of it out of the way): OLDER_REPAIR=0, or a file named repair.off in $OLDER_OUT — create it to
// pause, remove it to resume. While paused nothing is asked of GMGN for history; the hour-scale books simply know less.
let repOffAt = 0, repOff = false; const repairPaused = () => { if (process.env.OLDER_REPAIR === "0") return true; const n = Date.now(); if (n - repOffAt > 5000) { repOffAt = n; const was = repOff; repOff = !!process.env.OLDER_OUT && existsSync(process.env.OLDER_OUT + "/repair.off"); if (was !== repOff) log(`[backfill] history repair ${repOff ? "PAUSED (repair.off present)" : "resumed"}`); } return repOff; };
const backfill = startBackfill({ engine, store, jup: J, log, paused: repairPaused });
engine.needCreation = (mint) => backfill.want(mint, 2);
const pools = store.loadPools();
const feed = startFeed({ engine, store, backfill, pools, log });
// DIAGNOSTICS ONLY (no decision reads this): where the memory is. Recomputed at most once a minute.
let sizesAt = 0, sizesV = null;
const sizes = () => { const now = Date.now(); if (sizesV && now - sizesAt < 60000) return sizesV; sizesAt = now;
  let raw = 0, bars = 0, bfb = 0, wallets = 0, withData = 0, anchors = 0, withBreaks = 0, ownGaps = 0; for (const c of engine.coins.values()) { const sr = c.series; raw += sr.raw.length; bars += sr.bars.size; bfb += sr.bf.size; if (sr.raw.length || sr.bars.size) withData++; if (sr.anchor) anchors++; if (sr.own.length) { withBreaks++; ownGaps += sr.own.length; } for (const b of sr.bars.values()) wallets += b.buyers.size; }
  const m = process.memoryUsage(), mb = (x) => Math.round(x / 2 ** 20);
  return (sizesV = { rss_mb: mb(m.rss), heap_used_mb: mb(m.heapUsed), heap_total_mb: mb(m.heapTotal), external_mb: mb(m.external), coins: engine.coins.size, coins_with_data: withData, raw_obs: raw, live_bars: bars, backfill_bars: bfb, buyer_wallets: wallets, anchors, coins_with_price_breaks: withBreaks, price_breaks_on_record: ownGaps, entries_in_memory: engine.entries.size, dirty_bars: engine.dirtyBars?.size ?? null, ...feed.sizes() }); };

// initial discovery of existing pools: on first start, then once a day

  try { const { watch } = await inventory({ store, solUsd, log }); for (const w of watch.sort((a, b) => Math.abs(Math.log(a.mc / 150000)) - Math.abs(Math.log(b.mc / 150000)))) { engine.coin(w.mint, { pool: w.pool }); backfill.want(w.mint, w.mc >= 20000 && w.mc <= 1e6 ? 1 : 3); } }
  catch (e) { log("[inventory] failed: " + e.message); }
}
const inv0 = store.db.prepare("SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k='inventory'").get();
if (!inv0 || Date.now() - Date.parse(JSON.parse(inv0.v).at) > 86400e3) runInventory();

// EVENT-LOOP DELAY and CPU (validation protocol; Codex's review of 161d545): measured inside the process by Node's own
// monitor (10 ms resolution), kept as one line per minute — mean, 99th percentile, maximum — for the last 3 hours. One
// HTTP probe a minute from outside cannot establish the maximum. Counters restart with the process: every status carries
// the process start time and its own uptime so results are worked out per process lifetime, never across a restart.
const { monitorEventLoopDelay } = await import("node:perf_hooks"); const eld = monitorEventLoopDelay({ resolution: 10 }); eld.enable();
const loopMin = []; let cpu0 = process.cpuUsage(), cpuT0 = Date.now();
setInterval(() => { const c = process.cpuUsage(cpu0), dt = Date.now() - cpuT0; loopMin.push({ t: Math.round(Date.now() / 1000), mean_ms: +(eld.mean / 1e6).toFixed(2), p99_ms: +(eld.percentile(99) / 1e6).toFixed(1), max_ms: +(eld.max / 1e6).toFixed(1), cpu_pct: dt ? +(((c.user + c.system) / 1000 / dt) * 100).toFixed(1) : null });
  if (loopMin.length > 180) loopMin.shift(); eld.reset(); cpu0 = process.cpuUsage(); cpuT0 = Date.now(); }, 60000).unref?.();
const loopStatus = () => ({ resolution_ms: 10, minutes: loopMin.length, worst_max_ms: loopMin.length ? Math.max(...loopMin.map((x) => x.max_ms)) : null, worst_p99_ms: loopMin.length ? Math.max(...loopMin.map((x) => x.p99_ms)) : null, last: loopMin.slice(-60), current_minute_max_ms: +(eld.max / 1e6).toFixed(1) });
// clock: scheduled fills/exits every 100 ms (1 s and 0.7 s delays need a fine clock); bars, memory, heartbeat each minute
setInterval(() => engine.tick(Date.now() / 1000), 100);
let gapsSeen = engine.cov.gaps.length; // SIGNAL-coverage gaps (reconnects, late spells, silences): the list hour-scale windows are checked against — see backfill.gapRequests
setInterval(() => {
  const t = Date.now() / 1000; engine.prune(t); store.heartbeat(t);
  { const gr = gapRequests(engine.cov.gaps, gapsSeen); gapsSeen = gr.seen;
    if (gr.requests.length) { const mints = [...engine.byMint.keys()].filter((m) => engine.byMint.get(m).size); for (const [m, c] of engine.coins) if (c.series.lastPx && c.series.lastPx * c.supply >= 20000 && c.series.lastPx * c.supply <= 1e6) mints.push(m); const list = [...new Set(mints)]; for (const [a, b] of gr.requests) backfill.patchGap(a, b, list); } }
}, 60000);
setInterval(() => { const n = store.retention(Date.now() / 1000); if (n) log(`[retention] ${n} old bars removed`); }, 3600e3);

// ---- Solana-wide adapter (launchpad- and exchange-neutral): sampled prices + routed quotes through Jupiter. Off unless OLDER_SOLANA_WIDE=1.

====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/migrate.mjs
====================================================================================================
// migrate.mjs — carrying a base book's EXISTING buys into a newly added clock book (owner, 21 Nov 2026: "add the current data
// to it"). Runs once at start-up, before the positions are restored; an entry that already carries the family is left alone.
//
// For every stored buy of the base book, the clock book gets its own exit versions, copied from the plain ones:
//   • the plain version had already stopped out BEFORE the clock time  → the same real result (exact);
//   • the clock time is still in the future                            → a copy of the live position; the engine runs its clock;
//   • the clock time has passed and the plain version was still open then (or closed later) → the clock sale is ESTIMATED:
//       price at the clock = the close of the last one-minute bar COMPLETED before it (no such bar since the buy = unchanged),
//       as a ratio to the signal price; the pool at the clock is the constant-product pool with the buy pool's k at that price;
//       the tokens held then (half already banked if the half-sale happened before the clock) are sold on it, fee and
//       transaction cost charged. Marked exit.est = true and exec "estimated-minute-bar"; shown as "est." on the page.
// LIMITS of an estimated sale: a minute close is not the state 0.7 s after the clock; the bar ratio is in USD (SOL/USD movement
// over the hold is not removed); k is assumed unchanged since the buy. It is an estimate on REAL buys, never a decision input.
import * as S from "./spec.mjs";
import { newVersion, COST } from "./book.mjs";

export function addClockBook(store, family, now) {
  const ck = S.CLOCK[family], out = { base: ck?.base, added: 0, exact: 0, estimated: 0, live: 0 }; if (!ck) return out;
  const db = store.db, lastBar = db.prepare("SELECT m, c, src FROM bars WHERE mint = ? AND m >= ? AND m < ? AND src != 'backfill-rejected' ORDER BY m DESC LIMIT 1"); // minutes COMPLETED before the clock (the minute containing it closes after it), and never a rejected back-fill row
  const dec = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO decisions VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)");
  for (const row of db.prepare("SELECT json FROM entries ORDER BY id").all()) {
    const e = JSON.parse(row.json); if (!e.families?.includes(ck.base) || e.families.includes(family)) continue;
    e.families.push(family); const bc = (e.cands || []).find((c) => c.family === ck.base); if (bc) e.cands.push({ family, detail: bc.detail });
    const T = e.fill ? e.fill.t + ck.at : null;
    for (const p of e.versions.filter((v) => v.stall === false)) {
      if (e.status !== "filled") { e.versions.push(newVersion(p.stop, p.policy, ck.tag)); continue; }
      const c = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)); c.stall = ck.tag; c.stallChecked = false;
      if (p.status === "closed" && p.exit && p.exit.t <= T) { c.stallChecked = true; out.exact++; }
      else if (T > now) out.live++;
      else if (!(e.fill.quote_sol > 0) || !(e.fill.base > 0)) { c.stallChecked = true; c.flag = "bought on a routed quote: the clock sale cannot be estimated; this row keeps the plain result"; out.exact++; }
      else {
        const m0 = Math.floor(e.fill.t / 60), bar = lastBar.get(e.mint, m0, Math.floor(T / 60)), r = bar && e.signalPx > 0 ? bar.c / e.signalPx : 1;
        const banked = p.half && p.half.t <= T ? p.half : null, tok = banked ? e.fill.tok * (1 - S.SCALE_FRACTION) : e.fill.tok, got = banked ? banked.sol : 0;
        const k = e.fill.quote_sol * e.fill.base, px = (e.fill.quote_sol / e.fill.base) * r, q = Math.sqrt(k * px), b = Math.sqrt(k / px), fee = e.fill.fee_buy ?? 0.003;
        const sol = (q * tok) / (b + tok) * (1 - fee) - S.TX_COST_SOL, total = got + sol;
        Object.assign(c, { status: "closed", tok: 0, got, half: banked, reason: ck.reason, trigT: T, trigValue: null, schedT: null, stallChecked: true, lastX: total / COST, lastT: T,
          exit: { t: T + S.SELL_DELAY_MS / 1000, sol: total, last_sol: sol, ret: total / COST - 1, delay_s: 0, hold_s: Math.round(ck.at + S.SELL_DELAY_MS / 1000), trigger_value: null, fee_src: "assumed (estimate)",
            exec: "estimated-minute-bar", est: true, est_from: bar ? { minute: bar.m * 60, close: bar.c, src: bar.src, ratio_to_signal: +r.toFixed(4) } : "no trade between the buy and the clock: price unchanged", pool: { q, b, f: fee } } });
        delete c.unresolvedSince; delete c.unresolvedWhy; out.estimated++;
      }
      e.versions.push(c);
    }
    store.tx(() => { store.saveEntry(e); dec.run(e.mint, e.ag, family, e.decisionT, e.id, JSON.stringify(bc?.detail ?? null)); }); out.added++; // the entry and its one-buy flag in one commit
  }
  if (out.added) store.feedEvent?.(now, "migration: clock book added", `${family}: ${out.added} ${ck.base} buys carried in (${out.estimated} clock sales estimated)`);
  return out;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/page.mjs
====================================================================================================
// page.mjs — the books page in the ORIGINAL site's design (owner, 20 Nov 2026: "just copy the website that we had before, the
// format, it was perfect"): purple gradient, a ranked strip best-first, one card per book with today / last 6 h / last 24 h /
// total, what is open now, and the latest trades with fill in → out, timing and the high. Refreshes every 10 s.
// A "book" here is one entry family under the selected stop, exit policy, age group and population (selectors in the header);
// the dense table (families × 4 ages × 5 stops × 2 exits) is at /table.
// STAKE VIEW: the stake selector re-prices every pool-priced trade at 0.5 / 1 / 2 / 5 SOL (book.mjs atStake) — display only.
import * as S from "./spec.mjs";
import { versionsFor, atStake, COST } from "./book.mjs";

export const RULES = {
  [S.FEE_BOOK]: "every buy of a coin that charges a transfer fee (5% at most), whichever rule bought it, from the coin's first hours on (the other books start at 8 hours); kept out of the other books. The fee is inside the prices it is filled at (measured 22 Nov; not yet confirmed by the reviewer)",
  "AGE-DOUBLE": "market cap crosses 2× what it was at the start of the coin's age group (8 / 16 / 24 / 36 hours old)",
  "LOW4H": "price crosses 2× its lowest of the last 4 hours",
  "CONT": "price 30%+ above 15 minutes ago and within 5% of its 1-hour high",
  "SLOW-BUILD": "price crosses 2× its 4-hour low, only if the climb took 30+ minutes with no single 1-minute candle above +20%",
  "HH-BREAK": "new 60-minute high with the last 15 completed minutes' volume at 2× the 4-hour norm",
  "HH30-5M": "new 30-minute high with the last 5 completed minutes' volume at 2× the 2-hour norm",
  "HH30-10M": "new 30-minute high with the last 10 completed minutes' volume at 2× the 2-hour norm",
  "HH15-5M": "new 15-minute high with the last 5 completed minutes' volume at 2× the 1-hour norm",
  "HH-BREAK-4H": "the same buys as HH-BREAK, but sold at 4 hours if the position is still open",
  "VOL100K": "any age after migration: $100K or more traded in the last 5 minutes (coin worth $40K–$2M) — added in older-11; buys only in a run whose book list includes it",
  "VOL300K": "any age after migration: $300K or more traded in the last 5 minutes (coin worth $40K–$2M)",
  "DIP15K": "any age after migration: a coin that has been to $100K or more comes down through $15K (and is still at $5K or more) — bought at once; added in older-11; buys only in a run whose book list includes it",
  "VOL300K-UP": "any age after migration: $300K or more traded in the last 5 minutes and the price higher than 5 minutes ago ($40K–$2M)",
  "VOL500K": "any age after migration: $500K or more traded in the last 5 minutes (coin worth $40K–$2M)",
  "VOL500K-UP": "any age after migration: $500K or more traded in the last 5 minutes and the price higher than 5 minutes ago ($40K–$2M)",
  "RUG450": "any age after migration: peaked at $450K or more, bought the moment it is 80% below that peak",
  "RUG450-BOUNCE": "any age after migration: peaked at $450K or more, fell 80%, bought when it bounces 30% off its low (within 24 hours)",
  "RUG950": "any age after migration: peaked at $950K or more, bought the moment it is 80% below that peak",
  "RUG950-BOUNCE": "any age after migration: peaked at $950K or more, fell 80%, bought when it bounces 30% off its low (within 24 hours)",
  "CONT-STALL": "the same buys as CONT, but sold at 60 minutes if the position never reached 1.15×",
  "LOW4H-LIQ25K": "price crosses 2× its 4-hour low, only with $25K+ pool liquidity and under 5% price impact in and out",
  "FAST-1M": "price doubles inside 1 minute", "FAST-3M": "price doubles inside 3 minutes", "FAST-5M": "price doubles inside 5 minutes",
  "FAST-10M": "price doubles inside 10 minutes", "FAST-30M": "price doubles inside 30 minutes",
  "FAST150-1M": "price up 1.5× inside 1 minute", "FAST150-3M": "price up 1.5× inside 3 minutes", "FAST150-5M": "price up 1.5× inside 5 minutes", "FAST150-10M": "price up 1.5× inside 10 minutes",
  "SECOND-WAVE": "after a 1, 3 or 5-minute double: price falls 25%+ from its high, then breaks above that high within 60 minutes",

/** DISPLAY ONLY — the coin's market cap at the moment of our buy and of our sale, from the pool the trade was priced on
 *  (pool price × the SOL/USD rate and supply recorded with the signal). The sale pool is the one saved with the exit, or, for
 *  older trades, the pool rebuilt from the sale itself (as in book.atStake). Quote-priced (sampled) trades have no pool: the
 *  signal's market cap, and for the sale the signal's market cap × the result. The SALE figure is always shown as approximate
 *  (≈): it uses the SOL/USD rate and supply recorded at the signal, not at the sale. */
const usdMc = (e, q, b) => { const tr = e.evidence?.trigger; return tr?.sol_usd > 0 && q > 0 && b > 0 ? (q / b) * tr.sol_usd * (tr.supply ?? 1e9) : null; };
function saleMc(e, v) {
  const x = v.exit, f = e.fill; if (!x || !f) return null;
  if (x.pool) return usdMc(e, x.pool.q, x.pool.b);
  if (x.exec !== "reconstructed-pool-state" || !(f.quote_sol > 0) || !(f.base > 0)) return null;
  const fee = f.fee_buy ?? 0.003, k = f.quote_sol * f.base, t0 = v.half ? f.tok * (1 - S.SCALE_FRACTION) : f.tok, g = (x.last_sol + S.TX_COST_SOL) / (1 - fee);
  if (!(g > 0) || !(t0 > 0)) return null; const b = (-t0 + Math.sqrt(t0 * t0 + 4 * k * t0 / g)) / 2; return usdMc(e, k / b, b);
}
/** every trade of every family under one stop / policy (+ optional age group and population) — the page computes the windows.
 *  stake ≠ 0.2: each filled trade is shown re-priced at that stake (atStake). A trade that cannot be re-priced (a routed-quote
 *  trade; an open position not yet valued since a restart) is NOT left out — leaving it out changed which trades a book held
 *  from one stake button to the next (owner, 21 Nov 14:25 UK: the 0.5 SOL total was more than 2.5 × the 0.2 SOL total because
 *  two losing sampled trades had dropped out). It keeps its 0.2 SOL percentage result on the larger stake, with no extra price
 *  impact taken off, and is flagged `flat` and counted. Every stake button therefore shows the same set of trades. */
/** older-11 — what the page shows of the provider reading a buy was judged on (fractions 0–1; fees in SOL) */
const gateRow = (g) => ({ v: g.verdict ?? null, ms: g.ms ?? null, late: g.late || undefined, ...(g.reading ? { top10: g.reading.top10, bund: g.reading.bundlers, ins: g.reading.insiders, snip: g.reading.snipers, fees: g.reading.fees_sol, tw: g.reading.twitter == null ? null : !!g.reading.twitter, ath: g.reading.ath_usd == null ? null : Math.round(g.reading.ath_usd), holders: g.reading.holders ?? null, opt: g.options ?? undefined } : {}) });
/** older-11, label mode — one book's label on one buy: "pass" / "fail" (the reading was there at the buy), "pass-late" / "fail-late"
 *  (it arrived afterwards: exploratory only), "none" (no reading). A buy made in cancel mode that was filled had passed in time. */
export const checkOf = (e, fam) => { const g = e.gate; if (!g) return null; if (g.by) { const b = g.by[fam]; return !b ? "none" : (b.pass ? "pass" : "fail") + (g.late ? "-late" : ""); } return g.verdict === "pass" ? "pass" : g.verdict === "fail" ? "fail" : "none"; };
/** `run` (older-11): { name, books, gates, rebuy, min_age_h, sampled } of a run that chose its books — the page then shows ONLY
 *  those books (and FEE-COINS only if it holds a buy), with the run's own shared rules. Absent: every book, as before. */
export function booksApi(entries, { stop = 0.25, policy = "trail", ag = "", pop = "", stake = S.STAKE_SOL, since = null, build = null, first = false, run = null, checks = "all" } = {}) {
  // checks (a gated run only): "all" = every buy (the baseline); "pass" = only buys whose reading was there at the buy and passed
  // that book's checks; "late" = those plus buys whose reading passed but arrived after the buy (exploratory)
  const view = run?.gates && (checks === "pass" || checks === "late") ? checks : "all", keep = (c) => view === "all" || c === "pass" || (view === "late" && c === "pass-late");
  const shown = run?.books?.length ? run.books.filter((f) => S.FAMILIES.includes(f)) : S.FAMILIES;
  const books = Object.fromEntries([...shown, S.FEE_BOOK].map((f) => [f, []])), stakeView = stake !== S.STAKE_SOL, cost = stakeView ? stake + S.TX_COST_SOL : COST;
  for (const e of entries) {
    if (ag && e.ag !== ag) continue; if (pop && (e.pop || "pumpswap-canonical") !== pop) continue;
    const feeCoin = e.token?.xfer_bps > 0; let feeDone = false; // a transfer-fee coin: ONE row per buy, in the separate book only
    for (const fam of e.families) {
      const v = versionsFor(e, fam, policy).find((x) => x.stop === stop); if (!v || !books[fam] || feeDone) continue; // a re-buy carries only the rows its book had free: on the other rows it simply is not there
      if (first && e.again?.[fam]) continue; // "first buys only": the older-8 comparison, untouched by re-buys
      const chk = checkOf(e, fam); if (!keep(chk)) continue;
      const f = feeCoin ? S.FEE_BOOK : fam; feeDone = feeCoin;
      const live = v.status === "open" || v.status === "selling" || v.status === "scaling";
      books[f].push({ id: e.id, mint: e.mint, sym: e.token?.symbol ?? null, fee: e.token?.xfer_bps || 0, warm: e.warm_gap_s || 0, again: !!e.again?.[fam], via: feeCoin ? e.families.join(" + ") : null, ag: e.ag, mode: e.mode || "live", origin: e.token?.origin ?? (e.pop === "solana-wide" ? null : "pump.fun"), route: e.fill?.quote?.route ?? null,
        t: e.decisionT, mc: Math.round(e.signalMc), status: e.status === "filled" ? (live ? "open" : v.status) : e.status, why: e.status === "cancelled" ? e.flags.join("; ") : null,
        fill_t: e.fill?.t ?? null, buy_delay: e.fill?.delay_s ?? null, hi: e.runner?.maxX ?? null, hi_held: v.maxEq ?? (v.half ? null : v.maxX ?? null), // what the row was really worth at its best; a half-sold row from before this was recorded shows none rather than the full-position figure
         now_x: live ? (v.lastX ?? null) : null, half: v.half ? +(v.half.sol / COST).toFixed(3) : null,
        exit_t: v.exit?.t ?? null, ret: v.exit?.ret ?? null, net: v.exit ? v.exit.sol - COST : null, reason: v.reason ?? null, sell_delay: v.exit?.delay_s ?? null, hold_s: v.exit?.hold_s ?? null,
        startup: !!e.startup, shared: e.families.length > 1 ? e.families.filter((x) => x !== fam) : null, check: e.verify ? (e.verify.error ? "error" : e.verify.suspect ? "SUSPECT" : +(e.verify.diff_quote * 100).toFixed(2)) : null, flags: e.flags?.length ? e.flags : null, est_exit: v.exit?.est ? true : undefined,
        mc_buy: e.fill ? Math.round(usdMc(e, e.fill.quote_sol, e.fill.base) ?? e.signalMc) : null, mc_sold: v.exit ? (() => { const m = saleMc(e, v); return Math.round(m ?? e.signalMc * (1 + v.exit.ret)); })() : null, mc_approx: e.fill && usdMc(e, e.fill.quote_sol, e.fill.base) == null ? true : undefined, gate: e.gate ? gateRow(e.gate) : undefined, chk: chk ?? undefined, chk_why: e.gate?.by?.[fam]?.why ?? (chk === "fail" ? e.gate?.fails : undefined) ?? undefined });
      if (stakeView && e.status === "filled") { // the same trade at another stake; the high stays the 0.2 SOL multiple (labelled on the page)
        const r = books[f][books[f].length - 1], z = atStake(e, v, stake);
        if (!z) Object.assign(r, { flat: true, net: v.exit ? v.exit.ret * cost : null }); // same % as at 0.2 SOL: ret, now_x and the half multiple stay as recorded
        else Object.assign(r, { est: z.est || undefined, half: z.half != null ? +(z.half / z.cost).toFixed(3) : null, ret: z.closed ? z.ret : null, net: z.closed ? z.net : null, now_x: z.closed ? null : (live ? z.now_x : null) });
      }
    }
  }
  const order = [...shown]; if (!run?.books?.length || books[S.FEE_BOOK].length) order.push(S.FEE_BOOK); else delete books[S.FEE_BOOK];
  const rules = run?.books?.length ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(RULES).map(([k, v]) => [k, v.replace(/ — added in older-11; buys only in a run whose book list includes it|; added in older-11; buys only in a run whose book list includes it/, "")])) : RULES;
  return { at: Date.now() / 1000, build, spec: S.SPEC_VER, stop, policy, ag, pop, stake, first, since: since ? { first: since.first, families: since.families, stop: since.stops[stop] ?? since.first } : null, stakes: S.STAKE_VIEWS, cost, rows_total: shown.reduce((n, f) => n + S.agesOf(f).length, 0) * S.STOPS.length * S.POLICIES.length, rules, order, books,
    run: run?.books?.length ? { name: run.name ?? null, books: shown, gates: !!run.gates, gate_mode: run.gates ? run.gate_mode ?? "cancel" : null, checks: view, rebuy: run.rebuy !== false, min_age_h: run.min_age_h ?? S.MIN_AGE_S / 3600, sampled: !!run.sampled } : null };
}

<script>
var Q=new URLSearchParams(location.search),key=Q.get('k')||Q.get('key')||'';document.getElementById('tbl').href='/table'+(key?'?k='+encodeURIComponent(key):'');
var SEL={stop:'0.25',policy:'trail',ag:'',pop:'',stake:'0.2'};try{var sv=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('olderSel')||'null');if(sv)SEL=sv}catch(e){}if(!SEL.stake)SEL.stake='0.2';if(!SEL.win)SEL.win='tot';if(!SEL.buys)SEL.buys='all';if(!SEL.chk)SEL.chk='all';
function UKT(ts){return new Date(ts*1000).toLocaleString('en-GB',{timeZone:'Europe/London',hour12:false,day:'2-digit',month:'short',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}
function HM(ts){return new Date(ts*1000).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB',{timeZone:'Europe/London',hour12:false,hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit'})}
function DT(ts){return new Date(ts*1000).toLocaleString('en-GB',{timeZone:'Europe/London',hour12:false,day:'2-digit',month:'short',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit'})}
function ago(ts){var s=Math.floor(Date.now()/1000-ts);return s<60?s+' s':s<3600?Math.floor(s/60)+' min':(s/3600).toFixed(1)+' h'}
function sl(v){return (v>=0?'+':'')+v.toFixed(3)+' SOL'}function pc(v){return (v>=0?'+':'')+(v*100).toFixed(0)+'%'}function esc(s){return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[<>&"]/g,function(c){return {'<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','&':'&amp;','"':'&quot;'}[c]})}
function ukDayStart(){var d=new Date();var p=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB',{timeZone:'Europe/London',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hour12:false}).formatToParts(d);var o={};p.forEach(function(x){o[x.type]=x.value});return Math.floor(d.getTime()/1000)-((+o.hour)*3600+(+o.minute)*60+(+o.second))}
function sumR(rows){var n=0,s=0,w=0;rows.forEach(function(t){n++;s+=t.net;if(t.ret>0)w++});return {n:n,net:s,won:w}}
function coin(t){return '<a class="coin" href="https://gmgn.ai/sol/token/'+t.mint+'" target="_blank">'+esc(t.sym||t.mint.slice(0,8))+'</a>'+(t.fee?'<b style="color:var(--amb)" title="transfer fee '+(t.fee/100)+'% on the buy and on the sale, already taken off the result">*</b>':'')+(t.sym?' <span class="mut small" title="'+esc(t.mint)+'">'+esc(t.mint.slice(0,6))+'…</span>':'')+(t.again?' <span class="small" style="color:#5fd7ff" title="this book had bought this coin before, sold it on this stop, and bought it again when its rule fired again">re-buy</span>':'')+(t.warm?' <span class="small" style="color:var(--amb)" title="this buy relied on a price window that contains a restart: '+t.warm+' seconds were not watched">restart '+t.warm+'s</span>':'')+(t.via?' <span class="small" style="color:var(--amb)">'+(t.fee/100)+'% fee · bought by '+esc(t.via)+'</span>':'')}
function HI(t){var h=t.hi_held!=null?t.hi_held:(t.status==='closed'?null:t.hi);if(h==null)return '<span class="mut">—</span>'+(t.hi!=null?' <span class="mut">24 h '+t.hi.toFixed(2)+'x</span>':'');var later=t.status==='closed'&&t.hi!=null&&t.hi>h*1.02;return '<b>'+h.toFixed(2)+'x</b>'+(later?' <span class="mut" title="highest value of the position in the 24 hours after the buy, including after this sale">later '+t.hi.toFixed(2)+'x</span>':'')}
function openNet(rows,cost){var n=0,s=0;rows.forEach(function(t){if(t.now_x!=null){n++;s+=(t.now_x-1)*cost}});return {n:n,net:s}}
function MC(v,ap){return v==null?'—':(ap?'≈ ':'')+'$'+Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-GB')}
var FIND='';function hit(t){if(!FIND)return true;var f=FIND.toLowerCase();return t.mint.toLowerCase().indexOf(f)>=0||String(t.sym||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(f)>=0}
function how(t){return '<span class="mut">'+t.ag+' · '+(t.mode==='polled'?'sampled'+(t.route?' · '+esc(t.route.join('→')):''):'live')+(t.origin&&t.origin!=='pump.fun'?' · '+esc(t.origin):'')+(t.startup?' · startup':'')+'</span>'}
function P1(x){return x==null?'?':(x*100).toFixed(x*100<10&&x!==0?1:0)+'%'}
var CK={'pass':['win','passed'],'fail':['loss','failed'],'pass-late':['hold','passed late'],'fail-late':['na','failed late'],'none':['na','no reading']};
function CP(t){var k=CK[t.chk];return k?'<span class="pill '+k[0]+'" style="font-size:11px" title="'+esc((t.chk_why||[]).join('; ')||(t.chk==='pass'?'the reading was there at the buy and passes the checks of this book':t.chk==='none'?'no GMGN reading arrived for this buy':'the reading arrived after the buy: for interest only, it can reflect what happened after the entry'))+'">'+k[1]+'</span> ':''}
function GT(t){var g=t.gate;if(!g)return '<span class="mut">—</span>';if(g.top10===undefined)return CP(t)+'<span class="mut">'+(t.chk?'':(g.v==='no reading'?'no reading in time':'—'))+'</span>';
 var o=g.opt||{},tag=function(m,s2){return m===true&&s2===true?'':m===true?' <span class="mut">main</span>':s2===true?' <span class="mut">2nd</span>':''};
 return CP(t)+'<span title="read from GMGN '+(g.ms!=null?g.ms+' ms after the signal':'at the signal')+(g.holders!=null?' · '+g.holders.toLocaleString('en-GB')+' holders':'')+(g.ath!=null?' · all-time high $'+g.ath.toLocaleString('en-GB'):'')+'">top10 <b>'+P1(g.top10)+'</b> · bund <b>'+P1(g.bund)+'</b>'+tag(o.bundlers_main,o.bundlers_second)+' · ins <b>'+P1(g.ins)+'</b>'+tag(o.insiders_main,o.insiders_second)+' · snip <b>'+P1(g.snip)+'</b> · fees <b>'+(g.fees==null?'?':g.fees.toFixed(1))+'</b> SOL</span>'}
var PREV={},LAST=null,ST=null;
function draw(j){
 var now=Math.floor(Date.now()/1000),day0=ukDayStart(),B={},SK=(j.stake||0.2)+' SOL',VIEW=j.stake&&j.stake!==0.2,UNS=0,PRICED=0,EST=0,RUN=j.run||null,GA=!!(RUN&&RUN.gates),LAB=!!(GA&&RUN.gate_mode==='label');
 var ce=document.getElementById('chk'),cl=document.getElementById('chkLab');if(ce){ce.style.display=LAB?'':'none';cl.style.display=LAB?'':'none'}

  var meas=done.filter(function(t){return !t.est_exit}),estd=done.filter(function(t){return t.est_exit});
  var w=function(s){return sumR(meas.filter(function(t){return t.fill_t>=s}))};var tot=sumR(meas),h12=w(now-43200);
  var byMint={};meas.forEach(function(t){byMint[t.mint]=(byMint[t.mint]||0)+t.net});var top3=Object.keys(byMint).sort(function(a,b){return byMint[b]-byMint[a]}).slice(0,3);var ex=sumR(meas.filter(function(t){return top3.indexOf(t.mint)<0}));
  var uns=all.filter(function(t){return t.flat});UNS+=done.filter(function(t){return t.flat}).length;PRICED+=done.length;EST+=done.filter(function(t){return t.est}).length;
  B[k]={all:all,done:done,open:open,other:other,uns:uns,est:sumR(estd),day:w(day0),h6:w(now-21600),h24:w(now-86400),h12:h12,tot:tot,ex3:ex,perTrade:h12.n?h12.net/h12.n:(tot.n?tot.net/tot.n:null)}});

 var num=function(o){return o.n?'<span class="'+(o.net>0?'up':o.net<0?'dn':'mut')+'">'+sl(o.net)+'</span> <span class="mut">('+o.n+')</span>':'<span class="mut">—</span>'};
 document.getElementById('rank').innerHTML='<div class="rank"><b>Every book · '+(LAB&&RUN.checks!=='all'?(RUN.checks==='pass'?'ONLY BUYS THAT PASSED THE CHECKS IN TIME':'ONLY BUYS THAT PASSED THE CHECKS (INCL. LATE)')+' · ':'')+WL+' · '+Math.round(j.stop*100)+'% stop · '+(VIEW?'re-priced at '+SK+' a trade':'simulated fills at 0.2 SOL')+' · most profitable first</b>'+keys.map(function(k,i){var b=B[k];return '<span>'+(i+1)+'. <a class="coin" href="#bk-'+k+'"><b>'+k+'</b></a> '+num(b[W])+(W==='tot'?(b.h6.n?' <span class="mut">6h</span> <span class="'+(b.h6.net>0?'up':'dn')+'">'+sl(b.h6.net)+'</span>':''):(b.tot.n?' <span class="mut">total '+sl(b.tot.net)+'</span>':''))+'</span>'}).join('')+'</div>';

The filtered views are first-signal views of the same buys: they cannot show a version that skips a first signal and buys a later one':'')+(RUN.gates?' · <b>GMGN checks</b>: top 10 holders 18–30% (rug books: under 12%), bundlers 5–50%, insiders 2.5–40%, snipers under 5%, total fees 5 SOL or more, a Twitter link, has reached $100K (not asked of the rug books) — read once at the signal; '+(LAB?'a missing reading is "no reading", never zero':'a reading that is missing, failed or late cancels the buy and is never counted as zero')+'; "main" / "2nd" beside a figure says which of the two ranges it is inside when it is not inside both':''):'every book also follows the shared rules: coins 8 hours to 90 days old (the VOL and RUG books: any age after migration), $40K–$1M market cap at the buy (VOL books up to $2M), bought 1 s after the rule fires, sold by the selected trailing stop (or at 24 hours), one buy per coin per age group')+' · profit in SOL on closed trades, number of trades in brackets · tap a book to jump to its trades · <b>counting since</b> = when that book, on the stop selected above, began (amber = added after the first start, so its totals cover a shorter time than the others) · the same coin and buy can sit in several books</div>';
 var sz=document.getElementById('size');
 if(VIEW){sz.style.display='';sz.innerHTML='<b style="color:var(--amb)">Stake view: '+SK+' a trade.</b> <span class="mut">Each trade is re-priced on the pool sizes saved at its own buy and sale, so the extra price impact of a bigger trade is included — the result is <b>not</b> the 0.2 SOL profit multiplied up. It uses the same buy and sell moments as the 0.2 SOL paper trade: a real '+SK+' position would hit its stop slightly earlier, and nobody can know how other traders would have reacted to a bigger buy. It is on the <b>cautious</b> side: it assumes the price falls straight back after your buy, as if you had never bought. Treat it as an estimate. The "high" column is still the 0.2 SOL multiple. <b>'+PRICED+'</b> closed book entries, the same ones at every stake'+(EST?' · <b>'+EST+'</b> are older trades whose pool size at the sale was not saved and is worked out from the sale itself (a rougher estimate)':'')+(UNS?' · <b>'+UNS+'</b> could not be re-priced (coins priced through Jupiter quotes): they keep their 0.2 SOL percentage result, with no extra price impact taken off, so they look slightly better than they would be':'')+'.</span>'}
 else sz.style.display='none';
 var fd=document.getElementById('found');

  var seen={},list=[];keys.forEach(function(k){B[k].all.filter(hit).forEach(function(t){if(t.status==='cancelled'||t.fill_t==null)return;var id=t.id+'|'+(t.exit_t||'')+'|'+(t.ret==null?'':t.ret.toFixed(4));if(!seen[id]){seen[id]={t:t,books:[]};list.push(seen[id])}seen[id].books.push(k)})});
  list.sort(function(a,b){return a.t.fill_t-b.t.fill_t});
  var tbl=list.length?'<div class="tbl" style="margin-top:8px"><table><thead><tr><th>bought</th><th>picked up by</th><th>bought at (market cap)</th><th>sold</th><th>sold at (market cap)</th><th>result</th><th>high while held</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+list.map(function(o){var t=o.t,cl=t.status==='closed'&&t.ret!=null;return '<tr><td style="white-space:nowrap">'+DT(t.fill_t)+'</td><td style="white-space:normal"><b>'+o.books.join(', ')+'</b> <span class="mut">'+t.ag+'</span></td><td><b>'+MC(t.mc_buy!=null?t.mc_buy:t.mc,t.mc_approx)+'</b></td><td style="white-space:nowrap">'+(cl?DT(t.exit_t)+' <span class="mut">'+esc(t.reason||'')+'</span>':'<span class="mut">still open</span>')+'</td><td>'+(cl?MC(t.mc_sold,true):(t.now_x!=null?'<span class="mut">now '+t.now_x.toFixed(2)+'x</span>':'—'))+'</td><td>'+(cl?'<span class="pill '+(t.ret>0?'win':'loss')+'">'+pc(t.ret)+'</span> <span class="mut">'+sl(t.net)+'</span>'+(t.est_exit?' <span style="color:var(--amb)">est.</span>':''):'<span class="pill hold">open</span>')+'</td><td style="white-space:nowrap">'+HI(t)+'</td></tr>'}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div><div class="mut" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:4px">one line per buy at the stop and exit selected above · "picked up by" lists every book that shares that buy · market caps are what the coin was worth at the moment of our buy and of our sale (the sale figure is approximate: it uses the SOL price of the buy) · "high while held" is the best the position reached before it was sold; "later" is its best in the 24 hours after the buy, including after the sale · a buy made before a stop existed (the 60% stop began 21 Nov 14:19) has no line under that stop</div>':'';
  fd.style.display='';fd.innerHTML=n?'<b>Showing only '+esc(Object.keys(names).slice(0,4).join(', '))+'</b> <span class="mut">· '+n+' book entries at this stop and exit · '+cl+' closed, '+sl(net)+' in total across the books'+(on?' · <b>'+on+' still open</b>, worth '+sl(onet)+' if sold now (open positions are never in the totals — a wide stop keeps them open longer)':'')+' (the same buy can sit in several books) · all dates, not just the last 24 hours · the today / 6 h / 24 h / total boxes of each book still show the WHOLE book, not this coin · clear the search box to see everything</span>'+tbl:'<b>No paper buy found for "'+esc(FIND)+'"</b> <span class="mut">at this stop, exit, age group and coin filter.   var nfee=b.all.filter(function(t){return t.fee}).length,feeline=(k==='FEE-COINS'?'<div class="small" style="color:var(--amb);margin:2px 0 4px"><b>Fee handling, measured 22 Nov:</b> the prices this book is filled at already include the coin fee (8 of 8 fee coins checked, on 8 different routes), so no further fee is taken off. Buys made before that change had the fee taken off twice and are too low. Not yet confirmed by the reviewer.</div>':'')+(nfee?'<div class="small mut" style="margin:2px 0 4px"><b style="color:var(--amb)">*</b> marks a coin that charges a transfer fee (5% at most): '+nfee+' here.</div>':'');
  var stats='<div class="grid4">'+cell('today (UK)',b.day)+cell('last 6 hours',b.h6)+cell('last 24 hours',b.h24)+cell('total',b.tot,b.tot.n>3?' · <span class="mut">without top 3 coins: '+sl(b.ex3.net)+'</span>':'')+'</div>';

  var recent=rows.length?'<div class="lab" style="margin-top:8px">'+(FIND?'this coin · all dates':'last 24 hours')+' · '+rows.length+' trades · newest first</div><div class="tbl scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>coin</th><th>bought → sold</th><th>bought at (market cap)</th><th>sold at (market cap)</th><th>result</th><th>high while held</th>'+(GA?'<th>GMGN at the buy</th>':'')+'<th>buy → sell</th><th>timing</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map(function(t){var x=1+t.ret;return '<tr><td>'+coin(t)+' '+how(t)+'</td><td style="white-space:nowrap">'+DT(t.fill_t)+(t.exit_t?' <span class="mut">→</span> '+HM(t.exit_t):'')+'</td><td style="white-space:nowrap"><b>'+MC(t.mc_buy!=null?t.mc_buy:t.mc,t.mc_approx)+'</b></td><td style="white-space:nowrap">'+MC(t.mc_sold,true)+'</td><td style="white-space:nowrap"><span class="pill '+(t.ret>0?'win':'loss')+'">'+pc(t.ret)+'</span> <span class="mut">'+sl(t.net)+'</span>'+(t.est_exit?' <span style="color:var(--amb)" title="sale estimated from one-minute prices">est.</span>':'')+'</td><td class="'+(((t.hi_held!=null?t.hi_held:t.hi)||1)>=1.5?'up':'')+'" style="white-space:nowrap">'+HI(t)+'</td>'+(GA?'<td style="white-space:nowrap">'+GT(t)+'</td>':'')+'<td>1.000x <span class="mut">→</span> '+x.toFixed(3)+'x <span class="mut">'+esc(t.reason||'')+(t.half!=null?' · half at '+t.half.toFixed(2)+'x':'')+'</span></td><td class="mut">'+(t.buy_delay!=null?'buy +'+(1+t.buy_delay).toFixed(1)+' s':'')+(t.sell_delay!=null?' · sold '+(0.7+t.sell_delay).toFixed(1)+' s after trigger':'')+(t.hold_s!=null?' · held '+(t.hold_s<3600?Math.round(t.hold_s/60)+' min':(t.hold_s/3600).toFixed(1)+' h'):'')+'</td></tr>'}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div>':'<div class="small mut" style="margin-top:6px">no closed trades in the last 24 hours</div>';
  var nf=b.other.length?'<div class="small mut" style="margin-top:6px">not filled / unresolved: '+b.other.slice(GA?-8:-4).map(function(t){var w=t.why?esc(t.why).slice(0,GA?150:70):'';return esc(t.sym||t.mint.slice(0,6))+' ('+(GA&&w.indexOf('cancelled')===0?w:esc(t.status)+(w?': '+w:''))+')'}).join(' · ')+'</div>':'';
  h+='<div class="card" id="bk-'+k+'" style="scroll-margin-top:150px">'+head+stats+estline+rbline+feeline+open+recent+nf+'</div>'});
 document.getElementById('books').innerHTML=h;
 document.getElementById('foot').textContent='older-coins paper project · '+j.spec+' · full '+(j.rows_total||j.order.length*40)+'-row table at /table';
}
function health(s){var st=s.status,f=st.feed,d=st.decoder,p=st.poller,age=f.lastMsg?Math.round(Date.now()/1000-f.lastMsg):null;
