// report.mjs — result rows (family × age group × stop × exit policy) and the shared-entry overlap, from stored entries.
// families × 4 ages × 5 stops pure-trailing rows, and the same under the scale-out policy (21 families × 4 ages + 10 any-age families × 5 ages → 670 + 670 = 1,340 comparisons since older-11 added VOL100K and DIP15K).
// Results are after costs (pool fees, impact, declared transaction cost). Returns are per entry on COST (stake + buy transaction).
import * as S from "./spec.mjs";
import { versionsFor, COST } from "./book.mjs";

const q = (a, p) => { if (!a.length) return null; const s = [...a].sort((x, y) => x - y); const i = (s.length - 1) * p, lo = Math.floor(i), hi = Math.ceil(i); return s[lo] + (s[hi] - s[lo]) * (i - lo); };
const r4 = (x) => (x == null || !isFinite(x) ? null : +x.toFixed(4));
const sum = (a) => a.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0);

export function bookRows(entries, noteCounts = []) {
  const rows = new Map();
  const mk = (f, ag, stop, policy) => { const r = { family: f, ag, stop, policy, entries: 0, cancelled: 0, pending: 0, filled: 0, open: 0, closed: 0, unresolved: 0, mints: new Set(), trades: [], holds: [], delays: [], runners: { n: 0, partial: 0, x2: 0, x3: 0, x5: 0, x10: 0 }, outage: 0, estimated: 0, estimatedNet: 0, rebuys: 0, rebuyNet: 0, rebuyOpen: 0, feeAssumed: 0, halves: 0, startup: 0, startupNet: 0, unsupported: 0, unsupportedNet: 0, unsupportedRebuys: 0, unsupportedRebuyNet: 0 }; rows.set(`${f}|${ag}|${stop}|${policy}`, r); return r; };
  for (const policy of S.POLICIES) for (const f of S.FAMILIES) for (const [ag] of S.agesOf(f)) for (const stop of S.STOPS) mk(f, ag, stop, policy);
  for (const e of entries) { if (e.token?.xfer_bps > 0) continue; // transfer-fee coins are reported in their own book (spec FEE_BOOK), not in the rule rows
   for (const f of e.families) for (const policy of S.POLICIES) for (const v of versionsFor(e, f, policy)) {
    // older-11: a run may buy for an 8-hour book from 6 hours on (spec MIN_AGE_11_S). Such a buy is in age group A0, for which
    // these books have no fixed row: the row is added when the first such buy exists, so no buy is ever left out of the table.
    const r = rows.get(`${f}|${e.ag}|${v.stop}|${policy}`) ?? (e.ag === "A0" && S.FAMILIES.includes(f) && S.STOPS.includes(v.stop) ? mk(f, "A0", v.stop, policy) : null); if (!r) continue;
    // BUYING AGAIN (older-9): a row's figures are its FIRST buys, exactly as in older-8. Re-buys are counted and summed apart.
    // A re-buy of a coin later found fee-bearing or restricted is separated exactly like a first buy (Codex's review of
    // 161d545: the re-buy branch used to return before that check, so those fee-free results stayed in the re-buy P&L).
    if (e.again?.[f]) { if (e.status === "filled") { if (v.status === "closed") { if (v.exit.est) { /* an estimated sale is not a result */ } else if (e.riskAfter && e.exec !== "routed-quote") { r.unsupportedRebuys++; r.unsupportedRebuyNet += v.exit.sol - COST; } else { r.rebuys++; r.rebuyNet += v.exit.sol - COST; } } else r.rebuyOpen++; } continue; }
    r.entries++; r.mints.add(e.mint);
    if (e.status === "cancelled" || e.status === "failed") { r.cancelled++; continue; } if (e.status === "pending") { r.pending++; continue; }
    r.filled++; if (e.fill.fee_src !== "event") r.feeAssumed++; if (e.startup) r.startup++;
    if (e.runner.horizonDone) { r.runners.n++; if (e.runner.partial) r.runners.partial++; for (const k of S.RUNNER_X) if (e.runner.reached[k]) r.runners["x" + k]++; }
    if (v.half) r.halves++;
    if (v.status === "open" || v.status === "selling" || v.status === "scaling" || v.status === "unresolved") { r.open++; if (v.unresolvedSince || v.status === "unresolved") r.unresolved++; }
    else if (v.status === "closed" && v.exit.est) { r.estimated++; r.estimatedNet += v.exit.sol - COST; } // an ESTIMATED sale is not a result: counted apart, in no total, quantile or win rate (eighth review, A)
    // A coin found fee-bearing or restricted AFTER a live buy (Codex review of 3537ada): the live method models no transfer fee,
    // so this row's fee-free exit is not a supported result. The position and its ledger stay; its result is counted APART and
    // is in no primary total, quantile or win rate — never deleted, never zeroed.
    else if (v.status === "closed" && e.riskAfter && e.exec !== "routed-quote") { r.unsupported++; r.unsupportedNet += v.exit.sol - COST; }
    else if (v.status === "closed") { r.closed++; const net = v.exit.sol - COST; r.trades.push({ mint: e.mint, t: e.fill.t, ret: v.exit.ret, net, reason: v.reason }); r.holds.push(v.exit.hold_s); r.delays.push(v.exit.delay_s); if (v.exit.outage_during) r.outage++; if (e.startup) r.startupNet += net; }
  } }
  const notes = {}; for (const n of noteCounts) (notes[n.family] ||= {})[n.reason] = n.n;
  return [...rows.values()].map((r) => {
    const t = r.trades.sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t), rets = t.map((x) => x.ret), n = t.length;
    const byMint = new Map(); for (const x of t) byMint.set(x.mint, (byMint.get(x.mint) || 0) + x.net);
    const top3 = new Set([...byMint].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 3).map((x) => x[0])), rest = t.filter((x) => !top3.has(x.mint));
    const h1 = t.slice(0, Math.floor(n / 2)), h2 = t.slice(Math.floor(n / 2));
    const reasons = {}; for (const x of t) reasons[x.reason] = (reasons[x.reason] || 0) + 1;
    return {
      book: `${r.family} · ${r.ag} · ${Math.round(r.stop * 100)}% · ${r.policy}`, family: r.family, ag: r.ag, stop: r.stop, policy: r.policy,
      mints: r.mints.size, entries: r.entries, filled: r.filled, open: r.open, closed: n, cancelled: r.cancelled, pending: r.pending, unresolved: r.unresolved,
      net_sol: r4(sum(t.map((x) => x.net))), avg_ret: n ? r4(sum(rets) / n) : null, win_rate: n ? r4(rets.filter((x) => x > 0).length / n) : null,
      q10: r4(q(rets, 0.1)), q25: r4(q(rets, 0.25)), median: r4(q(rets, 0.5)), q75: r4(q(rets, 0.75)), q90: r4(q(rets, 0.9)),
      loss40: rets.filter((x) => x <= -0.4).length, loss50: rets.filter((x) => x <= -0.5).length, loss60: rets.filter((x) => x <= -0.6).length,
      ex_top3_net: r4(sum(rest.map((x) => x.net))), ex_top3_avg: rest.length ? r4(sum(rest.map((x) => x.ret)) / rest.length) : null, ex_top3_n: rest.length,
      h1_net: r4(sum(h1.map((x) => x.net))), h1_avg: h1.length ? r4(sum(h1.map((x) => x.ret)) / h1.length) : null, h2_net: r4(sum(h2.map((x) => x.net))), h2_avg: h2.length ? r4(sum(h2.map((x) => x.ret)) / h2.length) : null,
      hold_median_min: r.holds.length ? Math.round(q(r.holds, 0.5) / 60) : null, stake_committed_sol: r4(r.filled * COST), sell_delay_max_s: r.delays.length ? r4(Math.max(...r.delays)) : null,
      runners: r.runners, exit_reasons: reasons, exits_during_outage: r.outage, fee_not_from_event: r.feeAssumed, half_sales: r.halves,
      estimated_exits: r.estimated, estimated_net_sol: r4(r.estimatedNet), // shown apart: never part of closed / net_sol / win_rate above
      unsupported_fee_exits: r.unsupported, unsupported_fee_net_sol: r4(r.unsupportedNet), // live buys of coins later found fee-bearing or restricted: shown apart, never inside net_sol
      unsupported_fee_rebuys: r.unsupportedRebuys, unsupported_fee_rebuys_net_sol: r4(r.unsupportedRebuyNet), // the same for re-buys: never inside rebuys_net_sol
      rebuys_closed: r.rebuys, rebuys_net_sol: r4(r.rebuyNet), rebuys_open: r.rebuyOpen, // older-9 re-buys: beside the row, never inside it
      startup_entries: r.startup, startup_net_sol: r4(r.startupNet), notes: notes[r.family] || {},
    };
  });
}

/** families sharing coins / entries (correlated comparisons, not independent discoveries) */
export function overlap(entries) {
  const mints = {};
  const ok = (e) => e.status !== "cancelled" && e.status !== "failed";
  for (const e of entries) { if (!ok(e)) continue; for (const f of e.families) (mints[f] ||= new Set()).add(e.mint + "|" + e.ag); }
  const fam = S.FAMILIES, out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < fam.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < fam.length; j++) {
    const a = fam[i], b = fam[j]; let sm = 0; for (const m of mints[a] || []) if (mints[b]?.has(m)) sm++;
    const se = entries.filter((e) => ok(e) && e.families.includes(a) && e.families.includes(b)).length;
    out.push({ a, b, shared_mint_ages: sm, shared_entries: se, a_n: mints[a]?.size || 0, b_n: mints[b]?.size || 0 });
  }
  return out;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/risk.mjs
====================================================================================================
// risk.mjs — token risk evidence for broad Solana coverage. Purpose: stop entries whose EXECUTION ASSUMPTION is unsupported
// (the paper book assumes a plain buy and a plain sell). It is not a quality filter and adds no strategy condition.
//   status "restricted" : a known transfer restriction → not entered, counted
//   status "clean"      : checked, none of the restrictions below
//   status "flagged"    : tradeable under our assumption, but with recorded warnings (mint/freeze authority still active, "sus" marker…)
//                         — includes a TRANSFER FEE of at most XFER_FEE_MAX_BPS: entered, fee deducted on the buy and on every sale (fee_bps)
//   status "missing"    : no risk data — recorded as missing, NOT treated as clean. From older-10 a coin whose token program /
//                         Token-2022 extensions are not known is NOT ENTERED on either method (feeKnown); the reader retries it
// Risk flags are evidence, not proof a later sale succeeds.
import { XFER_FEE_MAX_BPS } from "./spec.mjs";
export const TOKEN_2022 = "TokenzQdBNbLqP5VEhdkAS6EPFLC1PHnBqCXEpPxuEb", SPL_TOKEN = "TokenkegQfeZyiNwAJbNbGKPFXCWuBvf9Ss623VQ5DA";
/** IS THE COIN'S TRANSFER FEE KNOWN? (eighth review, B1: "unknown" must never silently mean "no fee".) Known = a classic SPL
 *  token (the program has no transfer fee), or a mint whose extensions were read from the chain. Anything else — a Token-2022
 *  mint not read yet, an unknown token program — is NOT known, and a coin in that state is not entered — on the sampled method (eighth review) and, from older-10, on the live
 *  method too (ninth review, A: 225 live buys of Token-2022 coins were made with the extensions never read). */
export const feeKnown = (info) => info?.token_program === SPL_TOKEN || info?.risk?.src === "rpc mint account";
/** WHICH MINT ACCOUNTS TO READ NEXT (one RPC call for the whole batch), in this order:
 *    1. coins with a pending or open position whose mint account has not been read in this run   (a buy is waiting, or we hold it)
 *    2. coins whose fee is not known: a SAMPLED coin that is being priced, most recently priced first; a LIVE coin only once
 *       a decision has asked for it (engine._decide marks it: feeWanted), most recently asked first — not every pool ever seen
 *    3. coins with a pending or open position last read over refreshS ago     (a fee can be raised while we hold)
 *  Both observation methods (ninth review, A: until 24 Nov 2026 live coins were never read). A classic SPL token needs no
 *  read (the program has no extensions); a read that failed is retried after a minute without holding up the others. */
const LAT_EDGES = [1, 2, 5, 10, 30, 60, 300, Infinity]; // seconds from the first decision that needed a coin to its classification
export function riskQueue(engine, now, refreshS = 600, max = 50) {
  const a = [], b = [], c = [];
  for (const x of engine.coins.values()) { if (x.info?.token_program === SPL_TOKEN || (x.riskRetryAt ?? 0) > now) continue;
    const held = !!engine.byMint.get(x.mint)?.size, live = x.mode !== "polled";
    if (!x.riskRead) { if (held) { a.push(x); continue; } const k = live ? (feeKnown(x.info) ? null : x.feeWanted ?? null) : x.series.lastT; if (k != null) b.push([k, x]); } // sampled: only coins that are actually being priced (a coin off the watch list is read when it comes back)
    else if (held && now - (x.riskAt ?? 0) > refreshS) c.push(x); }
  b.sort((p, q) => q[0] - p[0]); c.sort((p, q) => (p.riskAt ?? 0) - (q.riskAt ?? 0));
  return [...a, ...b.map((p) => p[1]), ...c].slice(0, max);
}
/** the single next coin (the head of riskQueue), or null */
export const nextRiskRead = (engine, now, refreshS = 600) => riskQueue(engine, now, refreshS, 1)[0] ?? null;
/** reader counters for the status report (bounded): how many accounts were asked for, what came back, and how long a coin
 *  waited between the first decision that needed its classification and the classification arriving */
export const rk = { rounds: 0, asked: 0, classified: 0, transport_fail_rounds: 0, null_account: 0, unparsed: 0, malformed: 0, short_response: 0, retries: 0, max_batch: 0, backoff_max_s: 0,
  rpc_ms_sum: 0, rpc_ms_max: 0, latency_n: 0, latency_sum_s: 0, latency_max_s: 0, latency_hist: LAT_EDGES.map(() => 0), refreshes: 0, refresh_overdue_sum_s: 0, refresh_overdue_max_s: 0 };
/** the reader's part of the status report: the counters above plus the queue as it stands (length, oldest waiting request) */
export const riskStatus = (engine, now, refreshS = 600) => { const q = riskQueue(engine, now, refreshS, Infinity), w = q.map((c) => c.feeWantedFirst).filter((x) => x != null);
  return { ...rk, latency_hist: Object.fromEntries(LAT_EDGES.map((x, i) => [x === Infinity ? ">300" : "<=" + x, rk.latency_hist[i]])), latency_mean_s: rk.latency_n ? +(rk.latency_sum_s / rk.latency_n).toFixed(2) : null, rpc_ms_mean: rk.rounds ? Math.round(rk.rpc_ms_sum / rk.rounds) : null,
    refresh_overdue_mean_s: rk.refreshes ? +(rk.refresh_overdue_sum_s / rk.refreshes).toFixed(1) : null, queue: q.length, queue_oldest_wait_s: w.length ? +(now - Math.min(...w)).toFixed(1) : null, backing_off: [...engine.coins.values()].filter((c) => (c.riskRetryAt ?? 0) > now).length }; };
const WHY = { transport: "mint account read failed", null_account: "the provider returned no account for this mint", unparsed: "mint account not parsed", short_response: "the provider's answer was shorter than the request", malformed: "mint account malformed (a field had an unexpected shape)" };
/** ONE ROUND of the reader: reads the queued mint accounts in one call and records what they say. Returns how many were asked.
 *  rpc(method, params) is injected (feed.rpc in production). No dependency on the sampled adapter.
 *  FAILURES (Codex B5) — a whole-batch failure (transport) and a per-account failure (no account, an account that does not
 *  parse, an answer shorter than the request) are told apart and counted. Either way the coin is tried again with a bounded
 *  back-off (1 min, doubling, at most 1 h), what was known about it before stays, and one bad account never strands the others
 *  in its batch. A coin whose account could not be read stays "fee not known" and is therefore not entered — but it is no
 *  longer dropped from the queue for good. engine.riskUpdated(mint) lets the engine record a restriction found on a coin it holds. */
export async function riskReadOnce(engine, store, rpc, now, refreshS = 600, max = 50) {
  const q = riskQueue(engine, now, refreshS, max); if (!q.length) return 0;
  rk.rounds++; rk.asked += q.length; if (q.length > rk.max_batch) rk.max_batch = q.length;
  for (const c of q) { if (c.riskFails) rk.retries++; else if (c.riskRead && c.riskAt != null) { const late = Math.max(0, now - c.riskAt - refreshS); rk.refreshes++; rk.refresh_overdue_sum_s += late; if (late > rk.refresh_overdue_max_s) rk.refresh_overdue_max_s = +late.toFixed(1); } c.riskRead = true; c.riskAt = now; }
  const p0 = performance.now(); let vals = null; try { vals = (await rpc("getMultipleAccounts", [q.map((c) => c.mint), { encoding: "jsonParsed" }]))?.value ?? null; } catch { vals = null; }
  const took = (performance.now() - p0) / 1000, batchFail = !Array.isArray(vals); if (batchFail) rk.transport_fail_rounds++; rk.rpc_ms_sum += took * 1000; if (took * 1000 > rk.rpc_ms_max) rk.rpc_ms_max = Math.round(took * 1000);
  // back-off: 1 min, doubling, at most 1 h — and for a coin we hold never longer than its normal refresh interval
  const fail = (c, why) => { if (why !== "transport") rk[why]++; c.riskFails = (c.riskFails || 0) + 1; c.riskRead = false; const wait = Math.min(engine.byMint.get(c.mint)?.size ? refreshS : 3600, 60 * 2 ** Math.min(c.riskFails - 1, 10)); c.riskRetryAt = now + wait; if (wait > rk.backoff_max_s) rk.backoff_max_s = wait;
    c.riskFailWhy = why; if (!c.info.risk) c.info.risk = { status: "missing", why: WHY[why] }; };
  // EACH account is handled on its own (Codex's review of 161d545): an answer with an unexpected shape used to throw out of
  // this loop and leave the WHOLE batch marked as read for good. Now it fails that one coin ("malformed"), which is retried
  // like any other failure, and the rest of the batch is classified.
  q.forEach((c, i) => { try {
    if (batchFail) return fail(c, "transport"); if (i >= vals.length) return fail(c, "short_response"); if (vals[i] == null) return fail(c, "null_account");
    const k = classifyMintAccount(vals[i]); if (k.status === "missing") return fail(c, k.malformed ? "malformed" : "unparsed");
    k.read_at = Math.round(now + took); c.info.risk = k; if (k.program) c.info.token_program = k.program; c.riskFails = 0; c.riskFailWhy = null; rk.classified++;
    if (c.feeWantedFirst != null) { const w = Math.max(0, now + took - c.feeWantedFirst); rk.latency_n++; rk.latency_sum_s += w; rk.latency_hist[LAT_EDGES.findIndex((x) => w <= x)]++; if (w > rk.latency_max_s) rk.latency_max_s = +w.toFixed(2); c.feeWantedFirst = null; }
    store?.saveCoin(c.mint, { info: c.info }); engine.riskUpdated?.(c.mint);
  } catch { fail(c, "malformed"); } });
  return q.length;
}
const BLOCKING = new Set(["transferHook", "nonTransferable", "permanentDelegate", "pausableConfig", "confidentialTransferMint"]);

/** from an RPC getAccountInfo(mint, jsonParsed) value — the authoritative check for Token-2022 extensions */
export function classifyMintAccount(value) {
  const info = value?.data?.parsed?.info; if (!info) return { status: "missing", why: "mint account not parsed" };
  // FAIL CLOSED on an unexpected shape: a mint whose extension list, decimals or owner cannot be read is "missing", never "no extensions"
  const bad = (why) => ({ status: "missing", malformed: true, why: "mint account malformed: " + why });
  if (typeof info !== "object") return bad("info is not an object");
  if (info.extensions != null && (!Array.isArray(info.extensions) || info.extensions.some((x) => !x || typeof x !== "object" || typeof x.extension !== "string"))) return bad("extensions is not a list of extensions");
  if (!Number.isInteger(info.decimals) || info.decimals < 0 || info.decimals > 18) return bad("decimals"); if (typeof value.owner !== "string") return bad("owner");
  if (!(Number(info.supply) >= 0)) return bad("supply");
  const program = value.owner, ext = (info.extensions || []).map((x) => x.extension), warn = [], block = []; let fee = 0;
  for (const x of info.extensions || []) {
    if (BLOCKING.has(x.extension)) { if (x.extension === "transferHook" && !x.state?.programId) continue; block.push(x.extension); }
    if (x.extension === "transferFeeConfig") { const bps = Math.max(Number(x.state?.newerTransferFee?.transferFeeBasisPoints || 0), Number(x.state?.olderTransferFee?.transferFeeBasisPoints || 0)); fee = bps; // the HIGHER of the fee in force and the one already scheduled; the per-transfer cap (maximumFee) is ignored, so the fee is never understated
      if (bps > XFER_FEE_MAX_BPS) block.push(`transferFee ${bps} bps`); else if (bps > 0) warn.push(`transfer fee ${bps} bps: taken off the buy and off every sale`); }
    if (x.extension === "defaultAccountState" && x.state?.accountState === "frozen") block.push("defaultAccountState frozen");
  }
  if (info.freezeAuthority) warn.push("freeze authority active"); if (info.mintAuthority) warn.push("mint authority active");
  return { status: block.length ? "restricted" : warn.length ? "flagged" : "clean", program, extensions: ext, blocking: block, warnings: warn, fee_bps: fee, supply: Number(info.supply) / 10 ** info.decimals, decimals: info.decimals, src: "rpc mint account" };
}
/** from Jupiter token metadata — cannot see Token-2022 extensions, so a Token-2022 mint is "missing" until the RPC check has run */
export function classifyJupToken(t) {
  if (!t) return { status: "missing", why: "no provider record" };
  const warn = []; if (t.freeze_authority_disabled === false) warn.push("freeze authority active"); if (t.mint_authority_disabled === false) warn.push("mint authority active"); if (t.is_sus) warn.push("provider 'suspicious' marker");
  if (t.token_program === TOKEN_2022) return { status: "missing", why: "Token-2022 mint: extensions not yet read from the chain", warnings: warn, src: "jupiter tokens" };
  if (t.mint_authority_disabled == null && t.freeze_authority_disabled == null) return { status: "missing", why: "provider gave no audit fields", src: "jupiter tokens" };
  return { status: warn.length ? "flagged" : "clean", warnings: warn, src: "jupiter tokens" };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/series.mjs
====================================================================================================
// series.mjs — one coin's price history in memory: exact recent observations + minute bars + coverage.
// Time is in seconds (float, from the feed's receive clock). Prices are USD per token. Volume is USD.
// Coverage answers "do we really know what happened in [x, t]?" and separates four cases:
//   verified quiet (feed up, coin covered, simply no trades)  → the last price stands;
//   stale (the coin's own data stops but the feed was up)     → same as quiet, reported with its age;
//   feed outage (a global gap overlapping the window)         → window NOT covered unless that gap was back-filled for this coin;
//   missing history (window starts before the coin's history) → NOT covered.
import { RAW_KEEP_S, RAW_CAP, BAR_KEEP_S, BUYERS_KEEP_S, BUYER_RAW_KEEP_S, BUYERS_PER_BAR_CAP, BACKFILL_MAX_HOLE_S, WARM_MAX_GAP_S } from "./spec.mjs";

const EMPTY = new Set(); // shared: most minute bars never record a buyer wallet
export class Coverage { // global data-quality state, shared by all coins. Down while ANY reason is active (feed outage, SOL/USD stale…).
  constructor() { this.gaps = []; this.upSince = null; this.down = true; this.reasons = new Set(["feed"]); this.lastDown = null; this.warm = false; }
  /** WARM RESTART: the previous run stopped observing at `from` and its history was read back. The gap is closed by the next up():
   *  marked "warm" (tolerated by hour-scale windows) only if observation resumes within WARM_MAX_GAP_S — otherwise a plain outage. */
  resume(from) { this.lastDown = from; this.warm = true; }
  up(t, reason = "feed") {
    this.reasons.delete(reason); if (this.reasons.size || !this.down) return;
    if (this.lastDown != null) { const g = [this.lastDown, t]; if (this.warm && t - this.lastDown <= WARM_MAX_GAP_S) g[2] = "warm"; this.gaps.push(g); } this.warm = false;
    this.upSince = this.upSince ?? t; this.down = false; this.lastDown = null;
  }
  drop(t, reason = "feed") { this.reasons.add(reason); if (!this.down) { this.down = true; this.lastDown = t; } }
  /** gaps overlapping [x, t] (an outage still in progress counts up to t) */
  gapsIn(x, t) { const g = this.gaps.filter(([a, b]) => b > x && a < t); if (this.down && this.lastDown != null && this.lastDown < t) g.push([this.lastDown, t]); return g; }
}

export class Series {
  constructor(mint, cov) {
    this.mint = mint; this.cov = cov;
    this.raw = [];          // [t, px, volUsd, isBuy] in time order, last RAW_KEEP_S
    this.bars = new Map();  // minute → {o,h,l,c,v,nb,ns,buyers:Set,lowT} — LIVE observations only
    this.bf = new Map();    // minute → {o,h,l,c,v,…} — ACCEPTED back-fill only, kept apart: never read by an exact (seconds-scale) decision
    this.histFrom = null;   // earliest second from which this coin's history is continuous (back-fill or live)
    this.obsFrom = null;    // earliest second from which EXACT observations are continuous (live only: back-fill never moves it)
    this.lastIsBf = false;  // lastPx/lastT came from back-fill (no exact observation yet)
    this.compT = null;      // last time exact observations were merged within a second (RAW_CAP reached): measurements over it are flagged
    this.anchor = null;     // [t, px] the last exact observation that aged out of `raw`: the price still standing for a coin that went quiet
    this.patched = [];      // global gaps re-filled for this coin from back-fill bars: [a, b]
    this.pend = false;      // the coin's data was lost and no valid observation has arrived since: history restarts at the NEXT observation, not at the loss
    this.mrg = new WeakMap(); // raw entry → { lo, hi, t1, p0 } (p0 = its own first price): an entry that absorbed later observations of its own second (RAW_CAP) keeps their extremes and the last time
    this.own = [];          // THIS coin's own unusable-price intervals [a, b|null]: e.g. a boost quarantine, from the estimated state to the confirming swap
    this.ownCut = null;     // the end of the latest own break that was pruned from `own`: any stretch starting before it overlapped a break that is no longer on record
    this.lastPx = null; this.lastT = null; this.prevPx = null; this.prevT = null;
  }
  startLive(t) { if (this.histFrom === null || this.histFrom > t) this.histFrom = t; if (this.obsFrom === null || this.obsFrom > t) this.obsFrom = t; }
  /** this coin's own data was lost at t (lost events, a hole in the samples, a new pool): nothing before t is continuous — and
   *  nothing is known from t until the next valid observation either. LOSS and RECOVERY are different moments (sixth review, A):
   *  history restarts provisionally at t and is moved to the first observation that actually arrives (see add). */
  restartAt(t) { this.histFrom = t; this.obsFrom = t; this.anchor = null; this.own = []; this.pend = true; }
  /** this coin's price is unusable from t (its state is an estimate — a boost not yet confirmed by a swap): recorded as a break */
  breakOpen(t) { const g = this.own[this.own.length - 1]; if (g && g[1] === null) return false; this.own.push([t, null]); return true; }
  /** …and usable again from t (the confirming swap). The interval stays on record: no window or carried price may span it. */
  breakClose(t) { const g = this.own[this.own.length - 1]; if (!g || g[1] !== null) return false; g[1] = Math.max(t, g[0]); return true; }
  ownGapsIn(x, t) { const g = this.own.filter(([a, b]) => (b === null ? Infinity : b) > x && a < t); if (this.ownCut !== null && x < this.ownCut) g.unshift([null, this.ownCut]); return g; } // a pruned break still counts: provenance that was discarded is not provenance
  /** THE continuity test for exact data: were this coin's exact observations unbroken over [a, b]? True only if exact observation
   *  was already running at a, no feed or SOL/USD outage touches [a, b], and none of the coin's own breaks does. Everything that
   *  needs exact data uses this one test: seconds-scale windows, fresh crossings, candle completeness, and — new — every price
   *  CARRIED from an earlier moment to a later one (the carry must be continuous from the price's own time to where it is used). */
  continuous(a, b) { return this.obsFrom !== null && this.obsFrom <= a && this.cov.gapsIn(a, b).length === 0 && this.ownGapsIn(a, b).length === 0; }
  /** continuous EXCEPT for tolerated warm-restart gaps (spec WARM_MAX_GAP_S): the test for state that is carried for hours (the
   *  RUG books' peak and armed setup). Fresh crossings and exact windows keep using continuous(): they never span a restart. */
  steady(a, b) { return this.obsFrom !== null && this.obsFrom <= a && this.cov.gapsIn(a, b).every((g) => g[2] === "warm") && this.ownGapsIn(a, b).length === 0; }
  /** seconds of tolerated warm-restart gap inside [x, t] (0 = none): recorded on every decision whose window contains one */
  warmIn(x, t) { let s = 0; for (const [a, b, k] of this.cov.gapsIn(x, t)) if (k === "warm") s += Math.min(b, t) - Math.max(a, x); return +s.toFixed(1); }
  obsCovered(x, t) { return this.continuous(x, t); }
  /** an EXACT WINDOW [x, t] (FAST, SECOND-WAVE, fresh crossings): unbroken exact observation over it AND a price already standing
   *  at x — an exact observation at or before x, carried to x without a break. Observations that only begin somewhere inside the
   *  window (after a loss, a reconnection, a start-up) do not make it exact: its starting price, and so its low, would be unknown.
   *  A start that falls inside a second whose observations were merged (RAW_CAP) is not exact either (priceAt answers "not known"). */
  exactWindow(x, t) { return this.continuous(x, t) && this.priceAt(x, true) !== null; }
  /** back-fill minute bars (e.g. GMGN 1-minute kline): [{m, o, h, l, c, v}] requested for [from, to).
   *  Coverage follows the EVIDENCE, not the request: an empty reply extends nothing and repairs nothing (it cannot be told
   *  apart from "the provider has no data"). SPARSE replies prove nothing about the minutes they leave out (a missing minute
   *  may be "no trade" or missing data): bars count as continuous evidence only where consecutive bars are
   *  ≤ BACKFILL_MAX_HOLE_S apart — a DECLARED APPROXIMATION for hour-scale windows, not proof of continuity.
   *  History is extended back over the unbroken run of bars that reaches the start of the known history, and no further; an
   *  outage is repaired only when bars reach both of its ends (within 2 minutes) with no such hole between them.
   *  VALIDATION COMES FIRST: bars of a rejected reply, and bars outside the accepted run, are NOT kept — nothing a decision
   *  can read ever comes from data that was not accepted. Accepted bars live in `bf`, apart from live bars, and are read only
   *  by hour-scale windows: never by a fresh crossing or a seconds-scale window (see covered(…, strict) and the exact paths). */
  addBars(bars, { from, to, patchGap } = {}) {
    if (from == null || to == null) return { accepted: false, from: null, why: "no requested range: nothing can be claimed" };
    if (!bars.length) return { accepted: false, from: null, why: "provider returned no bars" };
    const ms = [...new Set(bars.map((b) => b.m))].sort((a, b) => a - b), H = BACKFILL_MAX_HOLE_S;
    const first = ms[0] * 60, last = ms[ms.length - 1] * 60 + 59;
    let keepFrom = ms[0], start = from;
    if (patchGap) {
      if (first > from + 120) return { accepted: false, from: first, why: "bars do not reach back to the start of the outage" };
      if (last < to - 120) return { accepted: false, from: first, why: "bars stop before the end of the outage" };
      for (let i = 1; i < ms.length; i++) if ((ms[i] - ms[i - 1]) * 60 > H) return { accepted: false, from: first, why: `a hole of ${ms[i] - ms[i - 1]} minutes inside the outage` };
    } else {
      if (last < to - H) return { accepted: false, from: null, why: "bars do not reach the start of the known history" };
      let k = ms.length - 1; while (k > 0 && (ms[k] - ms[k - 1]) * 60 <= H) k--; // the unbroken run that reaches the known history
      keepFrom = ms[k]; start = Math.max(from, ms[k] * 60);
    }
    for (const b of bars) if (b.m >= keepFrom) this.bf.set(b.m, { o: b.o, h: b.h, l: b.l, c: b.c, v: b.v || 0, nb: b.nb || 0, ns: b.ns || 0, buyers: EMPTY, lowT: b.m * 60, bf: true });
    if (patchGap) { this.patched.push([from, to]); return { accepted: true, from, resolution: "1-minute bars" }; }
    if (this.histFrom === null || (start < this.histFrom && to >= this.histFrom)) this.histFrom = start;
    if (this.lastT === null) { this.lastPx = this.bf.get(ms[ms.length - 1]).c; this.lastT = last; this.lastIsBf = true; }
    return { accepted: true, from: start, resolution: "1-minute bars" };
  }
  add(t, px, volUsd = 0, isBuy = null, user = null) {
    if (!(px > 0)) return;
    this.prevPx = this.lastPx; this.prevT = this.lastT;
    this.lastPx = px; this.lastT = t; this.lastIsBf = false;
    if (this.pend) { this.pend = false; if (t > this.histFrom) this.histFrom = t; if (t > this.obsFrom) this.obsFrom = t; } // recovery: history starts at the first observation after the loss
    const r = this.raw, n = r.length, sec = Math.floor(t), fresh = isBuy === true && user ? [t, px, volUsd, isBuy, user] : [t, px, volUsd, isBuy]; // user: one wallet, or [[wallet, usd]…] when a transaction has several buyers
    if (n >= RAW_CAP && n >= 2 && Math.floor(r[n - 1][0]) === sec && Math.floor(r[n - 2][0]) === sec) {
      // COMPRESSION (RAW_CAP reached; sixth review, C). The NEWEST observation always stays its own exact entry. What is merged
      // is the one before it, into the earlier entry of the same second — and that entry keeps the PRICE PATH a decision needs:
      // the lowest and highest price it absorbed, its first time (entry[0]) and its last time (t1), with the last price as its
      // price. Volume is summed and every buyer kept with its own volume (also through a buy → sell → buy second). What is lost
      // is the order of prices inside that second and the exact transaction counts: measurement windows over it are flagged
      // `compressed`, and an exact window that would START inside it is refused (exactWindow). At most two entries per second
      // are kept beyond the cap, so memory stays bounded.
      const y = r[n - 2], z = r[n - 1], m = this.mrg.get(y) || { lo: y[1], hi: y[1], t1: y[0], p0: y[1] };
      const buyers = (o) => (Array.isArray(o[4]) ? o[4] : (o[3] === true && typeof o[4] === "string" ? [[o[4], o[2]]] : [])), all = [...buyers(y), ...buyers(z)]; // buyers already merged stay, whatever sides followed
      if (z[1] < m.lo) m.lo = z[1]; if (z[1] > m.hi) m.hi = z[1]; m.t1 = z[0]; this.mrg.set(y, m);
      y[1] = z[1]; y[2] += z[2]; if (y[3] !== z[3]) y[3] = null; y[4] = all.length ? all : null; y[5] = true; this.compT = t;
      r[n - 1] = fresh;
    } else r.push(fresh);
    const m = Math.floor(t / 60); let b = this.bars.get(m);
    if (!b) { b = { o: px, h: px, l: px, c: px, v: 0, nb: 0, ns: 0, buyers: EMPTY, lowT: t }; this.bars.set(m, b); }
    if (px > b.h) b.h = px; if (px < b.l) { b.l = px; b.lowT = t; } b.c = px; b.v += volUsd;
    if (isBuy === true) { b.nb++; if (user) for (const u of typeof user === "string" ? [user] : user.map((x) => x[0])) { if (b.buyers.size >= BUYERS_PER_BAR_CAP) break; if (b.buyers === EMPTY) b.buyers = new Set(); b.buyers.add(u); } } else if (isBuy === false) b.ns++;
  }
  prune(now) {
    const cut = now - RAW_KEEP_S; let i = 0; while (i < this.raw.length && this.raw[i][0] < cut) i++;
    // the newest observation leaving the buffer is kept as the ANCHOR: for a coin that then goes quiet for hours (its bars pruned
    // too) it is still the exact price standing, so the wake-up trade is measured against it and not against nothing
    if (i) { const a = this.raw[i - 1]; this.anchor = [this.mrg.get(a)?.t1 ?? a[0], a[1]]; this.raw.splice(0, i); } // a merged entry's price is its LAST one: the anchor carries that time
    const ucut = now - BUYER_RAW_KEEP_S; for (const o of this.raw) { if (o[0] >= ucut) break; if (o.length > 4) o.length = 4; } // buyer wallets are only read for the last 5 minutes
    const mc = Math.floor((now - BAR_KEEP_S) / 60), bc = Math.floor((now - BUYERS_KEEP_S) / 60);
    // buyer wallets are only read for the last 15 minutes (features): older bars keep the COUNT and release the wallet list (memory)
    for (const [m, b] of this.bars) { if (m < mc) this.bars.delete(m); else if (m < bc && b.buyers !== EMPTY) { b.nbuyers = b.buyers.size; b.buyers = EMPTY; } }
    for (const m of this.bf.keys()) if (m < mc) this.bf.delete(m);
    if (this.own.length && this.own[0][1] !== null && this.own[0][1] < now - BAR_KEEP_S - 3600) { const cut = now - BAR_KEEP_S - 3600; for (const [, b] of this.own) if (b !== null && b < cut && (this.ownCut === null || b > this.ownCut)) this.ownCut = b; this.own = this.own.filter(([, b]) => b === null || b >= cut); }
  }
  /** is [x, t] fully known for this coin?
   *  strict = EXACT observations only: continuous since before x (back-fill does not count, even at start-up) and no outage,
   *  repaired or not. Used for fresh crossings and the seconds-scale FAST / SECOND-WAVE windows.
   *  non-strict = hour-scale: history (live or accepted back-fill) reaches back to x, and every outage inside was repaired. */
  covered(x, t, strict = false) {
    if (strict) return this.exactWindow(x, t);
    if (this.histFrom === null || this.histFrom > x) return false;
    for (const [a, b, k] of this.cov.gapsIn(x, t)) if (k !== "warm" && !this.patched.some(([pa, pb]) => pa <= a && pb >= b)) return false; // a warm-restart gap (≤ WARM_MAX_GAP_S, history read back) is tolerated here and recorded on the decision (warmIn)
    if (this.ownGapsIn(x, t).length) return false; // the coin's own breaks are never repaired by back-fill
    return true;
  }
  /** a PRICE window [x, t] for hour-scale use (lows, highs, a price a while ago): covered history AND a price known at x.
   *  Coverage alone says no trade was missed; it does not say what the price WAS before the first observation inside the
   *  window (after a start-up, a reconnection or a long silence). Without a starting price the window's low and high are
   *  unknown, so nothing is decided on it (seventh review, 3). Volume windows keep using covered(): no trade missed = volume known. */
  priced(x, t) { return this.covered(x, t) && this.priceAt(x) !== null; }
  /** last price at or before t. Exact sources first: an exact observation; else the later of the last completed LIVE bar's close
   *  and the anchor (the last exact observation that aged out of the buffer); else the last exact price if older. exact = false (hour-scale use) also considers accepted back-fill: a back-filled close of a later
   *  minute is newer information (a repaired outage, or history before the live start) and wins — an older observation is never
   *  preferred over a newer known close. exact = true never returns anything that came from back-fill. */
  priceAt(t, exact = false) {
    // s0 = the earliest moment the candidate's price can date from (an observation: its own time; a bar: the START of its minute)
    let r = null; for (let i = this.raw.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (this.raw[i][0] <= t) { const o = this.raw[i], m = this.mrg.get(o);
      // a merged entry holds prices from o[0] to m.t1; asked for a moment INSIDE it, its last price would be a price from later than t:
      // exact use answers "not known"; hour-scale use accepts the sub-second imprecision
      // exact use answers its FIRST price at exactly its first time, "not known" in between; hour-scale use accepts the sub-second imprecision
      if (m && m.t1 > t && exact) { if (t !== o[0]) return null; r = { px: m.p0, t: o[0], src: "obs", s0: o[0] }; break; }
      r = { px: o[1], t: m ? Math.min(m.t1, t) : o[0], src: "obs", s0: o[0] }; break; }
    if (!r) { let best = null; for (const m of this.bars.keys()) if ((m + 1) * 60 <= t && (best === null || m > best)) best = m; if (best !== null) r = { px: this.bars.get(best).c, t: best * 60 + 59, src: "bar", s0: best * 60 };
      if (this.anchor && this.anchor[0] <= t && (!r || this.anchor[0] > r.t)) r = { px: this.anchor[1], t: this.anchor[0], src: "anchor", s0: this.anchor[0] }; }
    if (!r && this.lastT !== null && this.lastT <= t && !this.lastIsBf) r = { px: this.lastPx, t: this.lastT, src: "last", s0: this.lastT };
    if (!exact && this.bf.size) {
      let bb = null; for (const m of this.bf.keys()) if ((m + 1) * 60 <= t && (bb === null || m > bb)) bb = m;
      // a completed back-filled minute that ENDS after the exact observation holds that minute's last trade: newer or equal information
      if (bb !== null && (!r || (bb + 1) * 60 > r.t)) r = { px: this.bf.get(bb).c, t: bb * 60 + 59, src: "backfill", s0: bb * 60 };
    }
    if (!r && !exact && this.lastT !== null && this.lastT <= t) r = { px: this.lastPx, t: this.lastT, src: "last", s0: this.lastT };
    if (!r) return null;
    // PROVENANCE: a price is carried forward from its own time to t only across a stretch we really know — for exact use, an
    // unbroken stretch of exact observation (no outage, no loss of the coin's data, no quarantine); for hour-scale use, covered
    // history (an outage must have been repaired). An older candidate would span the same break, so there is no fallback: the
    // answer is "not known". Age alone never disqualifies a price: verified silence can be arbitrarily long.
    if (!(exact ? this.continuous(r.s0, t) : this.covered(r.s0, t))) return null;
    return { px: r.px, t: r.t, src: r.src };
  }
  /** lowest / highest price in [x, t), plus the price carried in at x.
   *  exact = true (FAST, SECOND-WAVE): exact observations inside the window and the exact price standing at its start — nothing
   *  from back-fill and no minute bar (a bar's extreme may lie outside the window).
   *  exact = false (hour-scale): also live bar extremes for minutes older than the exact buffer — including its boundary minute,
   *  up to 59 s before x — and accepted back-filled bars that lie WHOLLY inside the window. */
  lowIn(x, t, exact = false) { return this._ext(x, t, (a, b) => a < b, "l", exact); }
  highIn(x, t, exact = false) { return this._ext(x, t, (a, b) => a > b, "h", exact); }
  _ext(x, t, better, k, exact = false) {
    let best = null; const rawFrom = this.raw.length ? this.raw[0][0] : Infinity;
    const carried = this.priceAt(x, exact); if (carried) best = { px: carried.px, t: x };
    if (!exact) {
      for (const [m, b] of this.bars) { const s = m * 60; if (s + 60 > x && s < Math.min(t, rawFrom)) { if (!best || better(b[k], best.px)) best = { px: b[k], t: k === "l" ? b.lowT : s }; } }
      for (const [m, b] of this.bf) { const s = m * 60; if (s >= x && s + 60 <= t) { if (!best || better(b[k], best.px)) best = { px: b[k], t: s }; } }
    }
    for (const o of this.raw) { const ts = o[0]; if (ts < x) continue; if (ts >= t) break; const m = this.mrg.get(o), px = m ? (k === "l" ? m.lo : m.hi) : o[1]; // a merged entry answers with the extreme it absorbed
      if (!best || better(px, best.px)) best = { px, t: ts }; }
    return best;
  }
  /** RECORDED MEASUREMENTS (never filters): trading in (x, t] from the exact observations — USD volume, buy and sell
   *  transactions, different buying wallets, and the largest single buyer's share of the buy volume.
   *  One observation is one transaction's committed state; a transaction with both buys and sells counts as neither.
   *  compressed: true when observations inside the window were merged within a second (RAW_CAP): volume and buyers are still
   *  complete, but the buy/sell transaction counts are understated. */
  winStats(x, t) {
    let vol = 0, buys = 0, sells = 0, buyVol = 0, compressed = false; const by = new Map();
    for (const o of this.raw) { const ts = o[0]; if (ts <= x) continue; if (ts > t) break; vol += o[2]; if (o[5]) compressed = true;
      const u = o[4]; let bv = 0; if (typeof u === "string") { by.set(u, (by.get(u) || 0) + o[2]); bv = o[2]; } else if (u) for (const [w, usd] of u) { by.set(w, (by.get(w) || 0) + usd); bv += usd; }
      if (o[3] === true) { buys++; buyVol += u ? bv : o[2]; } else { if (o[3] === false) sells++; buyVol += bv; } }
    let top = 0; for (const v of by.values()) if (v > top) top = v;
    return { vol_usd: Math.round(vol), buys, sells, buyers: by.size, top_buyer_share: buyVol > 0 && by.size ? +(top / buyVol).toFixed(4) : null, ...(compressed ? { compressed: true } : {}) };
  }
  /** exact USD volume in (x, t] from raw observations */
  /** USD volume of the exact observations after x (walks back from the newest: cheap on a busy coin).
   *  null = NOT KNOWN: x falls inside a second whose observations were merged (RAW_CAP) — that entry's volume lies on both
   *  sides of x and can no longer be split, so the caller must not decide on it (seventh review, 4). */
  volSince(x) { let v = 0; for (let i = this.raw.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const o = this.raw[i]; if (o[0] > x) { v += o[2]; continue; } const m = this.mrg.get(o); return m && m.t1 > x ? null : v; } return v; }
  volIn(x, t) { let v = 0; for (const [ts, , vol] of this.raw) if (ts > x && ts <= t) v += vol; return v; }
  /** was the WHOLE of minute m observed exactly? (exact observations already running at its start, and no outage inside it) */
  liveComplete(m) { return this.continuous(m * 60, m * 60 + 60); } // the same continuity test as everything else: outages, the coin's own breaks (quarantine), loss of its data
  /** THE authoritative candle of minute m, chosen by coverage — one rule for every candle reader (hour-scale use only):
   *    a fully observed live minute            → the live candle (back-fill never replaces complete live data)
   *    a PARTIAL live minute (cut by an outage, or begun before exact observation started) with an accepted back-filled candle
   *                                            → the back-filled candle: it covers the whole minute
   *    a partial live minute with no back-fill → the live fragment (all that is known)
   *    no live candle                          → the accepted back-filled candle, if any
   *  The two are never added together: a repaired whole-minute candle already contains the trades of the live fragment. */
  barAt(m) { const lv = this.bars.get(m), bf = this.bf.get(m); if (!lv) return bf ?? null; if (!bf) return lv; return this.liveComplete(m) ? lv : bf; }
  /** authoritative candles with minute index in [m0, m1), in time order */
  barsIn(m0, m1) { const out = [], seen = new Set(); for (const m of this.bars.keys()) if (m >= m0 && m < m1) { out.push([m, this.barAt(m)]); seen.add(m); } for (const [m, b] of this.bf) if (m >= m0 && m < m1 && !seen.has(m)) out.push([m, b]); return out.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]); }
  buyersIn(m0, m1) { const s = new Set(); for (const [, b] of this.barsIn(m0, m1)) for (const u of b.buyers) s.add(u); return s.size; }
  /** WARM RESTART: everything a decision reads, as plain data (see warm.mjs). Nothing is rounded or summarised. */
  dump() {
    const bar = (b) => { const o = { o: b.o, h: b.h, l: b.l, c: b.c, v: b.v, nb: b.nb, ns: b.ns, lowT: b.lowT }; if (b.nbuyers != null) o.nbuyers = b.nbuyers; if (b.buyers && b.buyers.size) o.buyers = [...b.buyers]; return o; };
    const mrg = []; this.raw.forEach((o, i) => { const m = this.mrg.get(o); if (m) mrg.push([i, m]); });
    return { raw: this.raw, mrg, bars: [...this.bars].map(([m, b]) => [m, bar(b)]), bf: [...this.bf].map(([m, b]) => [m, bar(b)]), histFrom: this.histFrom, obsFrom: this.obsFrom, lastIsBf: this.lastIsBf, compT: this.compT,
      anchor: this.anchor, patched: this.patched, pend: this.pend, own: this.own, ownCut: this.ownCut, lastPx: this.lastPx, lastT: this.lastT, prevPx: this.prevPx, prevT: this.prevT };
  }
  static load(mint, cov, d) {
    const s = new Series(mint, cov); s.raw = d.raw || []; for (const [i, m] of d.mrg || []) if (s.raw[i]) s.mrg.set(s.raw[i], m);
    const bar = (b, bf) => ({ o: b.o, h: b.h, l: b.l, c: b.c, v: b.v || 0, nb: b.nb || 0, ns: b.ns || 0, buyers: b.buyers ? new Set(b.buyers) : EMPTY, lowT: b.lowT, ...(b.nbuyers != null ? { nbuyers: b.nbuyers } : {}), ...(bf ? { bf: true } : {}) });
    for (const [m, b] of d.bars || []) s.bars.set(m, bar(b, false)); for (const [m, b] of d.bf || []) s.bf.set(m, bar(b, true));
    for (const k of ["histFrom", "obsFrom", "lastIsBf", "compT", "anchor", "pend", "ownCut", "lastPx", "lastT", "prevPx", "prevT"]) s[k] = d[k] ?? (k === "lastIsBf" || k === "pend" ? false : null);
    s.patched = d.patched || []; s.own = d.own || []; return s;
  }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/signals.mjs
====================================================================================================
// signals.mjs — the seven families' entry decisions for one coin observation. Pure: no I/O, no clock.
// Called AFTER the observation has been added to the coin's Series. Every window excludes the current observation
// (windows are [x, t) on time; the current observation sits at t) unless stated otherwise.
// Returns { cands: [...entry candidates], notes: [...rejections / missing-reference records] }.
import * as S from "./spec.mjs";

const NONE = new Set();
export function newCoin(mint, createTs, series, supply = 1e9) { return { mint, createTs, series, supply, groups: {}, sw: null }; }
const grp = (c, ag) => (c.groups[ag] ||= { ref: null, done: {}, logged: {} });
const median = (a) => { const s = [...a].sort((x, y) => x - y), n = s.length; return n === 0 ? 0 : n % 2 ? s[(n - 1) / 2] : (s[n / 2 - 1] + s[n / 2]) / 2; };

/** age-group reference market cap (AGE-DOUBLE): the price prevailing at the group's start, only if that is known */
export function ageRef(coin, ag) {
  const b = coin.createTs + S.ageStartOf(ag), sr = coin.series;
  const p = sr.priceAt(b);
  if (!p) return { status: "MISSING", why: "no observation at or before the age-group start", boundary: b };
  if (!sr.covered(p.t, b)) return { status: "MISSING", why: "history between the last trade and the age-group start is not covered", boundary: b, lastT: p.t };
  return { status: "OK", mc: p.px * coin.supply, px: p.px, t: p.t, quietS: Math.round(b - p.t), src: p.src, boundary: b };
}

/** research features at a decision (recorded, never used as filters) */
export function features(coin, o, low, hi1h, p15) {
  const sr = coin.series, t = o.t, m = Math.floor(t / 60);
  const last60 = sr.barsIn(m - 60, m); let bigCandle = 0; for (const [, b] of last60) if (b.o > 0) bigCandle = Math.max(bigCandle, b.c / b.o - 1);
  const last15 = sr.barsIn(m - 15, m); let nb = 0, ns = 0; const seen = new Map(); for (const [, b] of last15) { nb += b.nb; ns += b.ns; for (const u of b.buyers) seen.set(u, (seen.get(u) || 0) + 1); }
  const hi4 = sr.highIn(t - 4 * 3600, t);
  // short-window trading at the decision, from decoded trades (live method). Sampled coins have prices only → null; their
  // 5-minute figures come from the provider snapshot recorded on the entry (entry.snap).
  // A window is reported only when EXACT observations cover all of it (no back-fill, no outage): otherwise it is empty, never a partial count.
  const live = coin.mode !== "polled", win = (s) => (live && sr.obsCovered(t - s, t) ? sr.winStats(t - s, t) : null), w1 = win(60), w3 = win(180), w5 = win(300);
  const full60 = live && sr.obsCovered(t - 3600, t), vol60 = full60 ? sr.volIn(t - 3600, t) : null, prior = full60 && w5 ? (vol60 - w5.vol_usd) / 11 : null;
  return {
    w1, w3, w5, vol60_usd: vol60 != null ? Math.round(vol60) : null, vol_surge5: prior > 0 ? +(w5.vol_usd / prior).toFixed(2) : null, // last 5 min ÷ the average 5 min of the 55 min before
    age_s: Math.round(t - coin.createTs), mc: Math.round(o.mc), band: o.mc < 100e3 ? "40-100K" : o.mc < 250e3 ? "100-250K" : o.mc < 500e3 ? "250-500K" : "500K-1M",
    rise15: p15 ? +(o.px / p15.px).toFixed(4) : null, big_candle_60m: +bigCandle.toFixed(4),
    vol15_usd: Math.round(sr.volIn(t - 900, t)), buys15: nb, sells15: ns, buyers15: seen.size, repeat_buyers15: [...seen.values()].filter((n) => n > 1).length,
    liq_usd: o.liqUsd != null ? Math.round(o.liqUsd) : null, rt_impact: o.rtImpact != null ? +o.rtImpact.toFixed(4) : null,
    from_hi1h: hi1h ? +(o.px / hi1h.px - 1).toFixed(4) : null,
    drawdown4h: hi4 && low ? +(low.px / hi4.px - 1).toFixed(4) : null, recovery4h: low ? +(o.px / low.px).toFixed(4) : null,
  };
}

export function evaluate(coin, o, opts = {}) {
  const skip = opts.skip || NONE; // families the observation mode cannot support (recorded by the caller, never silently entered)
  const out = { cands: [], notes: [] }, sr = coin.series, t = o.t;
  const ag = S.ageGroupAny(t - coin.createTs); if (!ag) return out;
  const young = ag === "A0"; // migration to 8 hours old: only the any-age books are evaluated
  const g = grp(coin, ag), inBand = o.mc >= S.BAND_LO_USD && o.mc <= S.BAND_HI_USD;
  const note = (family, reason, detail = {}, once = true) => { const k = family + "|" + reason; if (once && g.logged[k]) return; g.logged[k] = 1; out.notes.push({ family, ag, t, reason, detail }); };
  // the previous observation counts for a "fresh crossing" only if nothing between it and now is unknown
  const prevOk = sr.prevT !== null && sr.prevT < t && sr.covered(sr.prevT, t, true); // strict: never across an outage, even a repaired one
  const cross = (line) => prevOk && sr.prevPx < line && o.px >= line;
  // `from` = the start of the longest stretch this decision relied on. If a tolerated warm-restart gap lies inside it, the seconds
  // that were not observed are recorded on the decision (restart_gap_s) — exact windows pass no `from`: they can never span one.
  // …and "the stretch it relied on" starts at the observation that SUPPLIED the price standing at the window's start, not at the
  // window's nominal start: a quiet coin's reference can date from before a restart while its window begins after it (eighth review, C3)
  const srcT = (x) => { const p = sr.priceAt(x); return p ? Math.min(x, p.src === "obs" || p.src === "anchor" || p.src === "last" ? p.t : p.t - 60) : x; };
  // BUYING AGAIN (spec REBUY): g.done[family] still means "this book has had its first attempt in this age group", exactly as in
  // older-8. opts.again(family) says whether the book may buy this coin AGAIN now (it holds no position on at least one row).
  // With opts.again absent every decision below is the older-8 decision. A candidate from a second or later attempt is marked `again`.
  const again = opts.again || (() => false), can = (f) => !g.done[f] || again(f);
  const cand = (family, detail, from = null, re = null) => { const a = re ?? !!g.done[family]; g.done[family] = 1; const w = from != null ? sr.warmIn(from, t) : 0; out.cands.push({ family, ag, t, mc: o.mc, px: o.px, detail: w ? { ...detail, restart_gap_s: w } : detail, ...(a ? { again: true } : {}) }); };
  const prevRec = sr.prevT !== null ? { t: sr.prevT, px: sr.prevPx, covered_to_now: prevOk } : null;

  // 0. ANY-AGE BOOKS (older-8) — evaluated from migration on. See spec VOL / RUG.
  // 0a. VOL300K / VOL500K (± price up): rolling 5-minute USD volume from exact observations. The window must be fully observed
  //     (a feed outage or a loss of this coin's data inside it would understate the volume, so nothing is decided over one).
  { const todo = S.VOL.filter(([f]) => can(f) && !skip.has(f));
    if (todo.length && o.mc >= S.BAND_LO_USD && o.mc <= S.VOL_BAND_HI_USD) {
      if (!sr.obsCovered(t - S.VOL_WIN_S, t)) note(todo[0][0], "window not covered");
      else { const v5 = sr.volSince(t - S.VOL_WIN_S);
        if (v5 === null) note(todo[0][0], "5-minute boundary inside a merged second: volume not known, not decided");
        else if (v5 >= Math.min(...todo.map((x) => x[1]))) { const p5 = sr.priceAt(t - S.VOL_WIN_S, true);
          for (const [family, X, up] of todo) { if (v5 < X) continue; if (up && !(p5 && o.px > p5.px)) continue;
            cand(family, { vol5_usd: Math.round(v5), threshold_usd: X, vol_window_s: S.VOL_WIN_S, px_5m_ago: p5 ? p5.px : null, change_5m: p5 ? +(o.px / p5.px - 1).toFixed(4) : null }); } } } } }
  // 0b. RUG450 / RUG950 (knife, bounce). The peak is the highest market cap OBSERVED WITHOUT A BREAK up to now: the state lives
  //     in memory only and is dropped the moment observation between the peak and now is not continuous (a feed outage, a loss
  //     of this coin's data, a quarantine, a change of observation method, a restart — except a WARM restart of at most
  //     WARM_MAX_GAP_S, where this exact state is read back and the unobserved seconds are recorded on the decision). After a break the true peak and the true
  //     low are unknown, so a fall or a bounce measured against the old values could be late or false (seventh review, 1 and 2):
  //     the coin starts again and is bought only if a new peak AND its collapse are both watched from start to finish.
  //     Nothing is persisted. An observation mode that cannot support these books (price polling) builds no state at all.
  if (S.RUG.some(([f]) => !skip.has(f))) {
    const rg = (coin.rug ??= { pk: null, arm: null });
    if (rg.pk && !sr.steady(rg.pk.t, t)) { /* steady = unbroken, or broken only by a tolerated warm restart */ if (rg.arm) note("RUG450-BOUNCE", "abandoned: observation broke during the setup", { armed_t: rg.arm.t }, false); rg.pk = null; rg.arm = null; }
    const prevMc = sr.prevPx != null ? sr.prevPx * coin.supply : null, inRug = (f) => can(f) && !skip.has(f);
    if (!rg.pk || o.mc > rg.pk.mc) rg.pk = { mc: o.mc, t };
    else {
      const line = rg.pk.mc * (1 - S.RUG_FALL);
      if (rg.pk.mc >= S.RUG_MIN_PEAK_USD && prevOk && prevMc > line && o.mc <= line) { // armed (again): the bounce books follow the low from here
        rg.arm = { t, peak: rg.pk.mc, peak_t: rg.pk.t, low: o.mc, low_t: t };
        for (const [family, P, kind] of S.RUG) if (kind === "knife" && rg.arm.peak >= P && inRug(family) && inBand)
          cand(family, { peak_mc: Math.round(rg.pk.mc), peak_t: rg.pk.t, line_mc: Math.round(line), fall: +(1 - o.mc / rg.pk.mc).toFixed(4), fall_s: Math.round(t - rg.pk.t) }, rg.pk.t);
      } else if (rg.arm) {
        if (t - rg.arm.t > S.RUG_BOUNCE_EXPIRE_S) rg.arm = null;
        else if (o.mc < rg.arm.low) { rg.arm.low = o.mc; rg.arm.low_t = t; }
        else { const up = rg.arm.low * S.RUG_BOUNCE_X;
          if (prevOk && prevMc < up && o.mc >= up) { // the bounce happened: bought if in the band, and the setup is spent either way
            for (const [family, P, kind] of S.RUG) if (kind === "bounce" && rg.arm.peak >= P && inRug(family) && inBand)
              cand(family, { peak_mc: Math.round(rg.arm.peak), peak_t: rg.arm.peak_t, armed_t: rg.arm.t, low_mc: Math.round(rg.arm.low), low_t: rg.arm.low_t, bounce: +(o.mc / rg.arm.low - 1).toFixed(4), from_peak: +(1 - o.mc / rg.arm.peak).toFixed(4), fall_s: Math.round(rg.arm.t - rg.arm.peak_t), wait_s: Math.round(t - rg.arm.t) }, rg.arm.peak_t);
            rg.arm = null; } }
      }
    }
  }

  // 0c. DIP15K (older-11) — has been to $100K or more, comes down into $5K–$15K: bought at the fresh crossing down through $15K.
  //     Its own band. The "$100K" evidence is the watched peak (coin.rug.pk, kept above while any RUG book can be evaluated) or,
  //     in a run with the owner's gates, the provider's all-time high tested at the buy (opts.peakByGate). See spec DIP.
  if (can(S.DIP) && !skip.has(S.DIP)) {
    const prevMc = sr.prevPx != null ? sr.prevPx * coin.supply : null;
    if (prevOk && prevMc > S.DIP_HI_USD && o.mc <= S.DIP_HI_USD) {
      const pk = coin.rug?.pk ?? null, watched = !!pk && pk.mc >= S.DIP_PEAK_USD, d = { line_mc: S.DIP_HI_USD, prev_mc: Math.round(prevMc), peak_watched_mc: pk ? Math.round(pk.mc) : null, peak_watched_t: pk ? pk.t : null, peak_evidence: watched ? "watched" : opts.peakByGate ? "provider all-time high (tested at the buy)" : null };
      if (o.mc < S.DIP_LO_USD) note(S.DIP, "fell through the band in one step (not entered)", { ...d, mc: Math.round(o.mc) }, false);
      else if (!watched && !opts.peakByGate) note(S.DIP, "crossing down into the band, but no $100K peak was watched (not entered)", { ...d, mc: Math.round(o.mc) }, false);
      else cand(S.DIP, { ...d, fall: watched ? +(1 - o.mc / pk.mc).toFixed(4) : null, fall_s: watched ? Math.round(t - pk.t) : null }, watched ? pk.t : null);
    }
  }

  let low = null, hi1h = null, p15 = null; // shared by the books below and by the measurements at the end
  older: { if (young && !opts.youngAll) break older; // every book below starts at 8 hours old — except for a transfer-fee coin (owner, 22 Nov 2026: fee coins are bought in their first 8 hours too; they are reported in FEE-COINS only)
  // 1. AGE-DOUBLE — first fresh crossing of 2 × the market cap at the age-group start
  if (!young && can("AGE-DOUBLE")) { // needs an age-group start: there is none under 8 hours
    if (!g.ref || g.ref.status !== "OK") { g.ref = ageRef(coin, ag); if (g.ref.status !== "OK") note("AGE-DOUBLE", "reference_status=MISSING", g.ref); } // re-tried while missing (a later back-fill may supply it); logged once
    if (g.ref.status === "OK") {
      const linePx = (S.AGE_DOUBLE_X * g.ref.mc) / coin.supply;
      if (cross(linePx)) { if (inBand) cand("AGE-DOUBLE", { ref_mc: Math.round(g.ref.mc), ref_t: g.ref.t, ref_quiet_s: g.ref.quietS, ref_src: g.ref.src }, g.ref.t); else note("AGE-DOUBLE", "crossing outside entry band", { mc: Math.round(o.mc) }, false); }
    }
  }

  // 2. LOW4H (+ 4. SLOW-BUILD and 7. LOW4H-LIQ25K on LOW4H's first eligible candidate)
  { const first = !g.done["LOW4H"], aL = again("LOW4H"), aS = again("SLOW-BUILD"), aQ = again("LOW4H-LIQ25K"); // first attempt: all three, as in older-8; later: each book that may buy again
  if (first || aL || aS || aQ) {
    if (!sr.priced(t - S.LOW4H_WIN_S, t)) note("LOW4H", "insufficient 4 h history");
    else {
      low = sr.lowIn(t - S.LOW4H_WIN_S, t);
      if (low && cross(S.LOW4H_X * low.px)) {
        if (!inBand) { if (first || aL) note("LOW4H", "crossing outside entry band", { mc: Math.round(o.mc) }, false); }
        else {
          const d = { low_px: low.px, low_t: low.t, low_mc: Math.round(low.px * coin.supply) };
          const w4 = srcT(t - S.LOW4H_WIN_S); if (first || aL) cand("LOW4H", d, w4, !first);
          // SLOW-BUILD: ≥ 30 min from the low, and no completed 1-min candle from the low's minute up to (not incl.) the trigger's minute gains > 20%
          const elapsed = t - low.t, bars = sr.barsIn(Math.floor(low.t / 60), Math.floor(t / 60));
          const worst = bars.reduce((w, [m, b]) => { const gn = b.o > 0 ? b.c / b.o - 1 : 0; return gn > w.g ? { g: gn, m } : w; }, { g: -Infinity, m: null });
          // flag: the candle that contains the low may have OPENED before the low, so its full open→close gain is not all "climb after the low"
          const lowBar = sr.barAt(Math.floor(low.t / 60));
          const sd = { ...d, elapsed_s: Math.round(elapsed), candles: bars.length, max_candle_gain: bars.length ? +worst.g.toFixed(4) : null, low_candle_opened_before_low: !!(lowBar && lowBar.o > low.px), worst_is_low_candle: worst.m === Math.floor(low.t / 60) };
          if (!(first || aS)) { /* this book holds the coin on every row: not evaluated */ }
          else if (elapsed < S.SLOW_MIN_ELAPSED_S) note("SLOW-BUILD", "rejected: under 30 min from low", sd, false);
          else if (bars.length && worst.g > S.SLOW_MAX_CANDLE_GAIN + 1e-12) note("SLOW-BUILD", "rejected: a candle above +20%", { ...sd, at_minute: worst.m }, false);
          else cand("SLOW-BUILD", sd, w4, !first);
          if (first || aS) g.done["SLOW-BUILD"] = 1;
          // LOW4H-LIQ25K: liquidity ≥ $25K and 0.2 SOL round-trip price impact ≤ 5% (fees excluded)
          // the two tests are recorded separately so we can see whether the impact test ever binds on its own
          const lq = { ...d, liq_usd: o.liqUsd == null ? null : Math.round(o.liqUsd), static_impact: o.rtImpact ?? null, liq_pass: o.liqUsd != null && o.liqUsd >= S.LIQ_MIN_USD, impact_pass: o.rtImpact != null && o.rtImpact <= S.LIQ_MAX_RT_IMPACT };
          if (!(first || aQ)) { /* holds the coin on every row */ }
          else if (o.liqUsd == null || o.rtImpact == null) note("LOW4H-LIQ25K", "rejected: liquidity/impact evidence missing", lq, false);
          else if (!lq.liq_pass) note("LOW4H-LIQ25K", lq.impact_pass ? "rejected: liquidity under $25K" : "rejected: liquidity under $25K and static impact over 5%", lq, false);
          else if (!lq.impact_pass) note("LOW4H-LIQ25K", "rejected: static impact over 5% (liquidity passed)", lq, false);
          else cand("LOW4H-LIQ25K", lq, w4, !first);
          if (first || aQ) g.done["LOW4H-LIQ25K"] = 1;
        }
      }
    }
  } }

  // 3. CONT (+ 6. CONT-STALL shares its entry) — first eligible observation; not a crossing
  { const first = !g.done["CONT"], aC = again("CONT"), aX = again("CONT-STALL");
  if (first || aC || aX) {
    if (!sr.priced(t - S.CONT_HI_WIN_S, t)) note("CONT", "insufficient 1 h history");
    else {
      p15 = sr.priceAt(t - S.CONT_LOOKBACK_S); hi1h = sr.highIn(t - S.CONT_HI_WIN_S, t);
      if (p15 && hi1h && o.px >= S.CONT_RISE * p15.px && o.px >= S.CONT_NEAR_HI * hi1h.px && inBand) {
        const d = { px15: p15.px, hi1h: hi1h.px }, w1 = srcT(t - S.CONT_HI_WIN_S); if (first || aC) cand("CONT", d, w1, !first); if (first || aX) cand("CONT-STALL", d, w1, !first);
      }
    }
  } }

  // 5. HH-BREAK (amended in older-3) — price strictly above the preceding 60-min high, and volume of the last 15 COMPLETED minutes
  // ≥ 2 × the median of the sixteen 15-minute buckets before them. B = the last completed minute boundary (floor(t / 60 s)).
  //   signal volume : [B − 15 min, B)            baseline : 16 adjacent buckets covering [B − 255 min, B − 15 min)
  // Half-open intervals; no overlap and no missing minute. Needs a volume source (not available from price polling alone).
  { const first = !g.done["HH-BREAK"], aH = again("HH-BREAK"), aK = again("HH-BREAK-4H");
  if ((first || aH || aK) && !skip.has("HH-BREAK")) {
    const mB = Math.floor(t / 60), histFrom = (mB - 15 - 15 * S.HH_BASE_WINDOWS) * 60;
    if (!sr.covered(histFrom, t) || !sr.priced(t - S.HH_HI_WIN_S, t)) note("HH-BREAK", "insufficient 4 h 15 min history"); // volume windows: no trade missed; the 60-minute high: a known starting price too
    else {
      const hi = hi1h ?? sr.highIn(t - S.HH_HI_WIN_S, t);
      if (hi && o.px > hi.px && inBand) {
        const vsum = (m0) => sr.barsIn(m0, m0 + 15).reduce((x, [, b]) => x + b.v, 0);
        const sums = []; for (let k = 1; k <= S.HH_BASE_WINDOWS; k++) sums.push(vsum(mB - 15 - 15 * k));
        const base = median(sums), v15 = vsum(mB - 15);
        const d = { hi1h: hi.px, B: mB * 60, signal_window: [(mB - 15) * 60, mB * 60], baseline_window: [(mB - 255) * 60, (mB - 15) * 60], vol15: Math.round(v15), base_median: Math.round(base), windows: sums.map(Math.round) };
        if (!(base > 0)) note("HH-BREAK", "zero volume baseline (not entered)", d, false);
        else if (v15 >= S.HH_VOL_X * base) { const f0 = Math.min(histFrom, srcT(t - S.HH_HI_WIN_S)); if (first || aH) cand("HH-BREAK", d, f0, !first); if (first || aK) cand("HH-BREAK-4H", d, f0, !first); } // the 4-hour clock book shares the buy (as CONT-STALL shares CONT's)
      }
    }
  } }

  // 5b. HH30-5M / HH30-10M / HH15-5M (older-6) — the same rule on shorter clocks: price strictly above the preceding W-second high,
  // and volume of the last n COMPLETED minutes ≥ 2 × the median of the K adjacent n-minute buckets before them.
  //   signal volume : [B − n min, B)            baseline : K buckets covering [B − (K + 1)·n min, B − n min)
  // Same half-open windows, same completed-minute boundary B, same candle selection (barsIn) and the same zero-baseline refusal as
  // HH-BREAK. No dollar floor. Needs a volume source, so it is not evaluated from price polling.
  for (const [family, W, n, K] of S.HH_FAST) {
    if (!can(family) || skip.has(family)) continue;
    const mB = Math.floor(t / 60), histFrom = Math.min((mB - n - n * K) * 60, t - W);
    if (!sr.covered(histFrom, t) || !sr.priced(t - W, t)) { note(family, "insufficient history"); continue; }
    const hi = sr.highIn(t - W, t);
    if (!(hi && o.px > hi.px && inBand)) continue;
    const vsum = (m0) => sr.barsIn(m0, m0 + n).reduce((x, [, b]) => x + b.v, 0);
    const sums = []; for (let k = 1; k <= K; k++) sums.push(vsum(mB - n - n * k));
    const base = median(sums), vn = vsum(mB - n);
    const d = { high: hi.px, high_window_s: W, B: mB * 60, signal_window: [(mB - n) * 60, mB * 60], baseline_window: [(mB - n - n * K) * 60, (mB - n) * 60], vol: Math.round(vn), base_median: Math.round(base), windows: sums.map(Math.round) };
    if (!(base > 0)) note(family, "zero volume baseline (not entered)", d, false);
    else if (vn >= S.HH_VOL_X * base) cand(family, d, Math.min(histFrom, srcT(t - W)));
  }

  // FAST / FAST150 — fresh crossing of X × the lowest pool spot price in the preceding window (1, 3, 5, 10 or 30 minutes; see S.FAST).
  // Window = exact observations on the receive clock, [t − W, t), including the price standing at the window's start.
  let fastSeed = false, fastSeedFirst = false; // …First: a seed from a FIRST FAST buy — the only kind that can seed SECOND-WAVE's first attempt (older-8)
  for (const [family, W, X, seeds] of S.FAST) {
    if (!can(family) || skip.has(family)) continue;
    if (!sr.covered(t - W, t, true)) { note(family, "window not covered"); continue; }
    const lo = sr.lowIn(t - W, t, true); if (!lo) continue; // EXACT: observations inside the window + the exact price standing at its start; never back-fill, never a minute bar
    if (!cross(X * lo.px)) continue;
    if (!inBand) { note(family, "crossing outside entry band", { mc: Math.round(o.mc) }, false); continue; }
    const obs = sr.raw.filter((r) => r[0] >= t - W && r[0] < t);
    cand(family, { window_s: W, x: X, low_px: lo.px, low_t: lo.t, threshold_px: X * lo.px, low_support: obs.filter((r) => r[1] <= lo.px * 1.02).length, window_obs: obs.length, low_carried_in: lo.t <= t - W,
      move_s: +(t - Math.max(lo.t, t - W)).toFixed(1) }); // RECORDED: seconds from the window's low to this trigger (= the whole window when the low was the price standing at its start)
    if (seeds) { fastSeed = true; if (!out.cands[out.cands.length - 1].again) fastSeedFirst = true; }
  }
  // 14. SECOND-WAVE (amended in older-3) — seeded by the first 2× FAST trigger. ONE active setup per mint, kept alive until its
  // own 60-minute expiry even across an age boundary. The seed uses up the SEED age group's attempt; the trade is classified by
  // the age group AT ENTRY, whose slot must be free and is then used. Seed age is recorded separately.
  // BUYING AGAIN and SECOND-WAVE. The FIRST setup per age group is seeded exactly as in older-8: by a first FAST buy, when the
  // slot is unused. A setup seeded AGAIN (slot already used, book holds nothing on some row) is marked, never uses up a first
  // slot, and gives way to a first setup: so the first buys of this book are the same with or without buying again.
  let w = coin.sw; const firstSW = !g.done["SECOND-WAVE"];
  if (w && w.again && firstSW && fastSeedFirst) { coin.sw = null; note("SECOND-WAVE", "re-buy setup replaced by a first setup", { seed_t: w.seedT, seed_ag: w.seedAg }, false); w = null; }
  if (!w) {
    if (firstSW ? fastSeedFirst : fastSeed && again("SECOND-WAVE")) {
      const re = !firstSW; g.done["SECOND-WAVE"] = 1; note("SECOND-WAVE", "seeded", { seed_t: t, seed_ag: ag, ...(re ? { again: true } : {}) }, false);
      coin.sw = { seedT: t, seedPx: o.px, seedAg: ag, seedAgeS: Math.round(t - coin.createTs), windows: out.cands.filter((c) => (re || !c.again) && S.FAST.some(([f, , , sd]) => sd && f === c.family)).map((c) => c.family), H: o.px, HT: t, frozen: null, pullT: null, again: re };
    }
  } else {
    const end = (reason, detail = {}) => { coin.sw = null; note("SECOND-WAVE", reason, { seed_t: w.seedT, seed_ag: w.seedAg, windows: w.windows, ...detail }, false); };
    if (t > w.seedT + S.SW_EXPIRE_S) end(w.frozen ? "expired: no reclaim within 60 min" : "expired: no 25% pullback within 60 min");
    else if (!sr.covered(w.seedT, t, true)) end("abandoned: coverage broke during the setup");
    else if (w.frozen === null) { if (o.px > w.H) { w.H = o.px; w.HT = t; } else if (o.px <= S.SW_PULLBACK * w.H) { w.frozen = w.H; w.pullT = t; w.pullPx = o.px; } }
    else if (o.px > w.frozen) {
      const d = { seed_t: w.seedT, seed_px: w.seedPx, seed_ag: w.seedAg, seed_age_s: w.seedAgeS, entry_ag: ag, seed_windows: w.windows, frozen_high: w.frozen, high_t: w.HT, pullback_t: w.pullT, pullback_px: w.pullPx, since_seed_s: Math.round(t - w.seedT) };
      if (w.again) { // a re-buy setup: bought only if the book may buy again now; it never takes a first slot
        if (!inBand) end("reclaim outside entry band", { ...d, mc: Math.round(o.mc) });
        else if (g.done["SECOND-WAVE"] && !again("SECOND-WAVE")) end("reclaim, but the book holds this coin on every row", d);
        else { const had = g.done["SECOND-WAVE"]; coin.sw = null; cand("SECOND-WAVE", d, null, true); if (!had) delete g.done["SECOND-WAVE"]; }
      }
      else if (ag !== w.seedAg && g.done["SECOND-WAVE"]) end("reclaim, but the entry-age group's SECOND-WAVE slot is already used", d);
      else if (!inBand) { g.done["SECOND-WAVE"] = 1; end("reclaim outside entry band (attempt used)", { ...d, mc: Math.round(o.mc) }); }
      else { coin.sw = null; cand("SECOND-WAVE", d, null, false); }
    }
  }

  } // end of the 8-hours-and-older books

  if (out.cands.length) {
    // evidence stored ONCE per entry: the previous observation that established the fresh crossing, and — for the short-window
    // families — every observation of the longest window involved
    const W = Math.max(0, ...out.cands.map((c) => c.detail.window_s || (c.family === "SECOND-WAVE" ? t - c.detail.seed_t : 0)));
    out.evidence = { prev: prevRec, window_s: W || null, obs: W ? sr.raw.filter((r) => r[0] >= t - W && r[0] <= t).slice(-S.EVIDENCE_OBS_CAP).map((r) => [+r[0].toFixed(3), r[1], Math.round(r[2]), r[3]]) : null };
  }
  if (out.cands.length) { low ??= sr.priced(t - S.LOW4H_WIN_S, t) ? sr.lowIn(t - S.LOW4H_WIN_S, t) : null; hi1h ??= sr.priced(t - 3600, t) ? sr.highIn(t - 3600, t) : null; p15 ??= sr.priceAt(t - 900); const f = features(coin, o, low, hi1h, p15); for (const c of out.cands) c.features = f; }
  return out;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/solusd.mjs
