// solusd.mjs — SOL/USD for converting pool prices to USD. Polls Coinbase's public spot price every 30 s; Kraken as fallback.
// Engine calls solUsd(t): the latest price if it is under 5 minutes old, else null (swaps are then not evaluated — counted, never guessed).
export const sp = { px: null, at: 0, src: null, fails: 0 };
async function get() {
  try { const j = await (await fetch("https://api.coinbase.com/v2/prices/SOL-USD/spot", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) })).json(); const v = Number(j?.data?.amount); if (v > 0) return [v, "coinbase"]; } catch {}
  try { const j = await (await fetch("https://api.kraken.com/0/public/Ticker?pair=SOLUSD", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) })).json(); const v = Number(Object.values(j?.result || {})[0]?.c?.[0]); if (v > 0) return [v, "kraken"]; } catch {}
  return null;
}
export async function startSolUsd() { const tick = async () => { const r = await get(); if (r) { sp.px = r[0]; sp.at = Date.now() / 1000; sp.src = r[1]; } else sp.fails++; }; await tick(); setInterval(tick, 30000); }
export const solUsd = () => (sp.px && Date.now() / 1000 - sp.at < 300 ? sp.px : null);


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/spec.mjs
====================================================================================================
// spec.mjs — the FROZEN rule constants for the older-coin paper project (older-1, 20 Nov 2026 UK).
// Every number a rule depends on lives here and nowhere else. Changing one is a new spec version (SPEC_VER), never a silent edit.
// The written specification that matches this code: older/docs/SPEC.md.
export const SPEC_VER = "older-11"; // 25 Nov 2026 (owner's decisions 24–25 Nov): NOTHING changes for a run that sets none of the new settings — it decides exactly as older-10 did. New, each switched on per run (main.mjs): the run's book list (OLDER_BOOKS; + VOL100K and DIP15K), the owner's gates on one provider reading per buy signal (OLDER_GATES), and the youngest age at which the 8-hour books are evaluated (OLDER_MIN_AGE_H). See the older-11 sections at the end of this file.
// older-10: 24 Nov 2026 (ninth review, Codex on the full export): EXECUTION TRUST only, no strategy number changed — (A) a coin whose token program / Token-2022 extensions have not been read from the chain is not entered on EITHER method (was: sampled coins only), the mint-account reader serves live coins and runs without the sampled adapter, and a pending live buy is checked again at the fill; (B) FEED_MAX_LAG_S: no signal and no pricing while the stream runs more than 5 s behind the chain or its delay is unknown. Rules, thresholds, stops, exits: unchanged.
// older-9: 22 Nov 2026 17:40 UK: + BUYING AGAIN (owner's decision) — a book that has bought a coin may buy it again, on any stop on which it no longer holds it (see REBUY below). The first buy per book, coin and age group is unchanged.
// older-8: 21 Nov 2026 21:15 UK: + eight any-age books (owner's decision): VOL300K / VOL500K (5-minute volume, with and without a price-up condition, $40K–$2M) and RUG450 / RUG950 (80% below a peak, bought at the level and on a 30% bounce); evaluated from migration on, incl. the first 8 hours (age group A0). 29 families, 1,240 rows. Previous: older-7 // 21 Nov 2026 16:40 UK: + HH-BREAK-4H (owner's decision: HH-BREAK's buys, sold at 4 hours if still open; 21 families, 840 rows; the existing HH-BREAK buys are carried into it, sales past 4 h estimated from minute bars and marked). Previous: older-6 // 21 Nov 2026 16:00 UK: + HH30-5M, HH30-10M, HH15-5M (owner's decision: faster volume breakouts, no dollar floor; 20 families, 800 comparison rows). Previous: older-5 // 21 Nov 2026 (afternoon): + a 60% trailing stop (owner's decision; 5 stops, 680 comparison rows) and website-only stake views. Previous: older-4 // 21 Nov 2026: + FAST-10M, FAST-30M, FAST150-10M (owner's decision: fill the gap between the 5-minute and the 4-hour books); SECOND-WAVE seeds unchanged (1/3/5-minute doubles only). Previous: older-3 // 20 Nov 2026 pm: Solana-wide scope (launchpad/exchange-neutral), HH-BREAK completed-minute windows, SECOND-WAVE across age boundaries. Previous: older-2 // 20 Nov 2026: + FAST, FAST150, SECOND-WAVE, scale-out policy, 90-day ceiling, scheduled-time fills (owner + ChatGPT consensus round)

export const STAKE_SOL = 0.2;                 // paper stake per entry
export const TX_COST_SOL = 0.0005;            // declared network/priority cost per transaction side (assumption, not measured for this project)
export const BAND_LO_USD = 40_000, BAND_HI_USD = 1_000_000; // entry market-cap band, inclusive lo, inclusive hi
export const MIN_AGE_S = 8 * 3600;            // since TOKEN creation (not pool creation)

// Age groups: [id, from (incl), to (excl)] in seconds of token age. A4 ends at 90 days (entry ceiling; open positions are not closed by it).
export const AGES = [["A1", 8 * 3600, 16 * 3600], ["A2", 16 * 3600, 24 * 3600], ["A3", 24 * 3600, 36 * 3600], ["A4", 36 * 3600, 90 * 86400]];
export const ageGroupOf = (ageS) => { for (const [id, a, b] of AGES) if (ageS >= a && ageS < b) return id; return null; };
export const ageStartOf = (id) => AGES.find((g) => g[0] === id)[1];
// A0 = from migration to 8 hours old. Only the ANY-AGE books (older-8) are evaluated there; every other book still starts at 8 h.
export const AGE_YOUNG = ["A0", 0, MIN_AGE_S], ALL_AGES = [AGE_YOUNG, ...AGES];
export const ageGroupAny = (ageS) => (ageS >= 0 && ageS < MIN_AGE_S ? "A0" : ageGroupOf(ageS));

export const STOPS = [0.20, 0.25, 0.30, 0.40, 0.60]; // trailing stops on after-cost liquidation value (60% added in older-5)
// STAKE VIEWS (website only, never a decision input): the page can re-price every pool-priced trade at these stakes from the pool
// reserves saved at its buy, its half-sale and its exit. The paper trade itself is always STAKE_SOL.
export const STAKE_VIEWS = [0.2, 0.5, 1, 2, 5];
export const MAX_HOLD_S = 24 * 3600;          // from the simulated FILL
export const BUY_DELAY_MS = 1000;             // buy scheduled 1 s after the signal
export const SELL_DELAY_MS = 700;             // sell scheduled 0.7 s after an exit trigger
export const BUY_PRICING_TIMEOUT_S = 5;       // no trustworthy pool state within 5 s after the scheduled buy → pricing/data timeout, entry cancelled (NOT "untradeable")
export const SELL_UNRESOLVED_S = 24 * 3600;   // a triggered sale that cannot be priced for 24 h is shown as unresolved exposure (never silently closed)

// Families. 21 families × 4 ages + 10 any-age families × 5 ages = 134 book-ages; × 5 stops × 2 exit policies = 1,340 comparisons (VOL100K and DIP15K added in older-11).
export const FAMILIES = ["AGE-DOUBLE", "LOW4H", "CONT", "SLOW-BUILD", "HH-BREAK", "CONT-STALL", "LOW4H-LIQ25K",
  "FAST-1M", "FAST-3M", "FAST-5M", "FAST-10M", "FAST-30M", "FAST150-1M", "FAST150-3M", "FAST150-5M", "FAST150-10M", "SECOND-WAVE", "HH30-5M", "HH30-10M", "HH15-5M", "HH-BREAK-4H",
  "VOL100K", "VOL300K", "VOL300K-UP", "VOL500K", "VOL500K-UP", "RUG450", "RUG450-BOUNCE", "RUG950", "RUG950-BOUNCE", "DIP15K"];
// ANY-AGE BOOKS (older-8, owner's decisions 21 Nov 2026 21:11 UK). Migrated coins only (this feed sees nothing else), any age after
// migration: the first 8 hours (A0) and the four age groups. One entry per family per coin per age group, as everywhere.
// VOL: USD volume over the last VOL_WIN_S seconds (exact observations, rolling) ≥ the threshold; the -UP version also needs the
//      price above the exact price VOL_WIN_S ago. Market cap $40K–$2M at the buy (the owner's upper limit for these books).
// [family, threshold USD, needs price up]
export const VOL = [["VOL100K", 100_000, false], ["VOL300K", 300_000, false], ["VOL300K-UP", 300_000, true], ["VOL500K", 500_000, false], ["VOL500K-UP", 500_000, true]];
export const VOL_WIN_S = 300, VOL_BAND_HI_USD = 2_000_000;
// RUG: the coin's highest observed market cap ≥ the peak threshold, and the market cap FRESHLY crosses down to (1 − RUG_FALL) × that
//      peak, with observation unbroken from the peak to the crossing (the state is in memory only and starts again after any
//      break or restart). "knife" buys at that crossing. "bounce" is armed by it, follows the low, and buys at the first fresh crossing up
//      through RUG_BOUNCE_X × the low within RUG_BOUNCE_EXPIRE_S. Usual band ($40K–$1M) at the buy. The books nest: a $950K peak
//      also qualifies for the $450K books.
// [family, peak threshold USD, kind]
export const RUG = [["RUG450", 450_000, "knife"], ["RUG450-BOUNCE", 450_000, "bounce"], ["RUG950", 950_000, "knife"], ["RUG950-BOUNCE", 950_000, "bounce"]];
export const RUG_FALL = 0.80, RUG_BOUNCE_X = 1.30, RUG_BOUNCE_EXPIRE_S = 24 * 3600, RUG_MIN_PEAK_USD = 450_000;
// DIP15K (older-11, owner 25 Nov 2026 09:32 and 10:43): a coin that HAS BEEN to $100,000 or more and comes down into $5,000–$15,000
//   is bought when its market cap freshly crosses down through DIP_HI_USD (the observation before it above the line, this one at
//   or under it, nothing unobserved in between) and is still at or above DIP_LO_USD — at once, however long the fall took. Any
//   age after migration. It has its OWN band: the $40K floor of the other books does not apply to it.
//   "Has been to $100K": the watched peak (the highest market cap observed without a break — the same state the RUG books keep)
//   is at least DIP_PEAK_USD, OR the run uses the owner's gates, where the provider's all-time high is tested at the buy (gates.mjs).
//   A coin that is already inside the band when it is first seen is not bought there: nothing was watched arriving.
export const DIP = "DIP15K", DIP_HI_USD = 15_000, DIP_LO_USD = 5_000, DIP_PEAK_USD = 100_000;
export const ANY_AGE = new Set([...VOL.map((x) => x[0]), ...RUG.map((x) => x[0]), DIP]);
export const agesOf = (family) => (ANY_AGE.has(family) ? ALL_AGES : AGES);
export const POLICIES = ["trail", "scale"];   // trail = the original pure trailing stop; scale = sell half at 2× cost, trail the rest
export const SCALE_AT_X = 2, SCALE_FRACTION = 0.5;
// FAST: fresh crossing of X × the lowest pool spot price in the preceding window (receive-time clock, exact observations)
// [family, window seconds, multiple, seeds SECOND-WAVE]. The 10- and 30-minute books (older-4) use the same exact-observation rules;
// they do NOT seed SECOND-WAVE, which stays "after a 1/3/5-minute double" as in older-3. The longest window (30 min) is inside RAW_KEEP_S.
export const FAST = [["FAST-1M", 60, 2, true], ["FAST-3M", 180, 2, true], ["FAST-5M", 300, 2, true], ["FAST-10M", 600, 2, false], ["FAST-30M", 1800, 2, false],
  ["FAST150-1M", 60, 1.5, false], ["FAST150-3M", 180, 1.5, false], ["FAST150-5M", 300, 1.5, false], ["FAST150-10M", 600, 1.5, false]];
// SECOND-WAVE: seeded by the first 2× FAST trigger in the age group; pullback to ≤ 0.75 × running high freezes that high; buy strictly above it; expires 60 min after the seed
export const SW_PULLBACK = 0.75, SW_EXPIRE_S = 3600;
export const STARTUP_TAG_S = 24 * 3600;       // entries in the first 24 h after the project's first start are tagged "startup" (kept in totals, shown separately)
export const EVIDENCE_OBS_CAP = 1500;         // observations saved with a FAST / SECOND-WAVE trigger (stored once per entry)
export const AGE_DOUBLE_X = 2;
export const LOW4H_WIN_S = 4 * 3600, LOW4H_X = 2;
export const CONT_LOOKBACK_S = 15 * 60, CONT_RISE = 1.30, CONT_HI_WIN_S = 3600, CONT_NEAR_HI = 0.95;
export const SLOW_MIN_ELAPSED_S = 30 * 60, SLOW_MAX_CANDLE_GAIN = 0.20; // gain ABOVE 0.20 rejects; exactly 0.20 allowed
export const HH_HI_WIN_S = 3600, HH_VOL_WIN_S = 15 * 60, HH_BASE_WINDOWS = 16, HH_VOL_X = 2; // 16 × 15 min = the preceding 4 h
// Faster volume breakouts (older-6, owner's decision 21 Nov 2026): the HH-BREAK rule on shorter clocks.
// [family, high window seconds, signal minutes n, baseline buckets K]: price strictly above the preceding high-window high, and
// the volume of the last n COMPLETED minutes ≥ HH_VOL_X × the median of the K adjacent n-minute buckets before them.
// No dollar floor (owner's decision). As in HH-BREAK, a zero baseline (the median bucket had no volume) is recorded and not entered.
export const HH_FAST = [["HH30-5M", 1800, 5, 24], ["HH30-10M", 1800, 10, 12], ["HH15-5M", 900, 5, 12]]; // baselines: 2 h, 2 h, 1 h
export const STALL_AT_S = 3600, STALL_MIN_PEAK_X = 1.15;
// CLOCK BOOKS: a book that shares another book's buys (base) and adds a timed sale to its own exit versions.
//   tag     = the value those versions carry in v.stall (plain versions carry false)
//   minPeak = sold at `at` seconds only if the position never reached this multiple of cost; null = sold at `at` if still open
// CONT-STALL (older-1): CONT's buys, sold at 60 min if never 1.15×.  HH-BREAK-4H (older-7): HH-BREAK's buys, sold at 4 h.
export const CLOCK = {
  "CONT-STALL": { base: "CONT", tag: true, at: STALL_AT_S, minPeak: STALL_MIN_PEAK_X, reason: "stall" },
  "HH-BREAK-4H": { base: "HH-BREAK", tag: "4h", at: 4 * 3600, minPeak: null, reason: "clock" },
};
export const clockTag = (family) => CLOCK[family]?.tag ?? false;
export const clockOf = (tag) => Object.values(CLOCK).find((c) => c.tag === tag) ?? null;
export const LIQ_MIN_USD = 25_000, LIQ_MAX_RT_IMPACT = 0.05;

// Runner outcome tracking (separate from realised profit): liquidation value / stake over 24 h after the fill.
export const RUNNER_X = [2, 3, 5, 10], RUNNER_HORIZON_S = 24 * 3600;
// Severe-loss thresholds shown on the page (−40% is the project's existing severe-loss measure).
export const LOSS_X = [0.40, 0.50, 0.60];

// Memory / storage bounds (not rules; operating limits).
export const RAW_KEEP_S = 65 * 60;            // exact observations kept in memory per coin (covers the 15-min and 60-min windows)
export const RAW_CAP = 6000;                  // per coin; beyond it, older observations within the same second are merged — the merged entry keeps their lowest, highest and last price and its first and last time; the newest observation always stays exact
export const BAR_KEEP_S = 4 * 3600 + 30 * 60; // in-memory minute bars (covers LOW4H's 4 h and HH-BREAK's 4 h 15 min)
export const BAR_RETAIN_DAYS = 10;            // minute bars kept in the database
export const BUYERS_PER_BAR_CAP = 300;
export const QUOTE_CHAIN_TOL = 1e-3;           // quote-side chain check: a mismatch above 0.1% of effective reserves is a discontinuity (smaller = fee dust, counted)
export const FEE_MAX = 0.10;                  // a measured pool fee outside 0–10% is a failed measurement: counted, NOT installed
export const BACKFILL_MAX_HOLE_S = 300;       // back-filled minute bars prove a window only where consecutive bars are ≤ 5 min apart
export const OMIT_DROP_AFTER = 10;             // a sampled token the price provider omits this many times in a row (no position, no setup) leaves the watch list
export const SCALE_QUOTE_MAX_SPREAD_S = 5;    // the routed quotes of one half-sale must have been requested within 5 s of each other
// STREAM FRESHNESS (ninth review, B) — an operating limit for execution trust, NOT a strategy filter and not tuned on results.
// Our clock is receive time; each transaction also carries its block time. Normal delay is about 1.0–1.3 s. Above this limit
// (or with no measurement in the last FEED_MAX_LAG_S seconds, or none since the last connect) the stream is not CURRENT:
// no signal, no peak or stop, no order priced. The same number is the time measurements must stay within the limit before a
// late spell is over. See Engine.noteLag / freshAt.
export const FEED_MAX_LAG_S = 5;
export const CHECKPOINT_S = 1;                // open positions' peaks are saved at most this long after they change
export const BUYER_RAW_KEEP_S = 6 * 60;       // exact per-trade buyer wallets kept this long (the 1/3/5-minute entry measurements)
export const BUYERS_KEEP_S = 20 * 60;          // per-bar buyer wallet lists kept this long (the features read 15 minutes); older bars keep the count only
// TRANSFER-FEE COINS (owner's decision, 22 Nov 2026): a Token-2022 coin whose creator takes a fee on every transfer may be entered
// when that fee is at most this (5%); above it the coin stays blocked. The fee is taken off the paper result twice — off the
// tokens received at the buy and off the proceeds of every sale — and every such trade is marked on the page. Sampled method
// only (pool-state fills do not model the fee). Other transfer restrictions (hooks, freeze, permanent delegate…) stay blocked.
export const XFER_FEE_MAX_BPS = 500;
// THE INCLUSION ASSUMPTION (eighth review, B2): does a routed quote's output already have the coin's transfer fee taken off?
// true  = the quote is net: nothing more is deducted here.  false = the quote is gross: the fee is deducted here, once per transfer.
// MEASURED 22 Nov 2026 21:13 UK on the server (scripts/fee-probe.mjs, quotes only): 8 fee coins (3% and 4.2%) over 8 different
// routes — FluxBeam, Raydium CP, Raydium CLMM, Whirlpool, Meteora DLMM, BisonFi, Kipseli and mixed routes. A 0.2 SOL round trip
// lost 7.9%–10.2% against 0.2%–2.8% for 4 control coins (median 2.4%): the extra loss was 5.5%–7.8%, i.e. about 2 × the coin's fee
// (6.0% / 8.4%) in every case. So Jupiter's quotes ALREADY INCLUDE the transfer fee on both the buy and the sale → true.
// The raw quoted amounts, the fee and this value are still stored on every fill and sale, and the probe can be re-run at any time.
export const XFER_FEE_IN_QUOTE = true;
export const RISK_REFRESH_S = 600; // a sampled coin with a pending or open position has its transfer restrictions read again this often
// Reporting only: every buy of a transfer-fee coin is shown in ONE separate book and left out of the rule books' own totals
// and rows (owner, 22 Nov 2026: "a separate book so we can analyse those separately"). The entry still records the rule(s) that bought it.
export const FEE_BOOK = "FEE-COINS";
// WARM RESTART (owner's decision, 22 Nov 2026): a planned stop writes each coin's price memory to disk and the next start reads
// it back, so a restart no longer empties the hour-scale windows. The time the program was away stays UNOBSERVED: it is kept
// as a gap, and that gap is TOLERATED — for hour-scale windows only — when it is at most this long. Exact (seconds-scale)
// windows and fresh crossings never span it. Longer away than this: the gap is an ordinary outage and nothing spans it.
export const WARM_MAX_GAP_S = 60;
// BUYING AGAIN (older-9, owner's decisions 22 Nov 2026 17:37–17:45 UK). Until now a book bought a coin once per age group, sold or
// not. From older-9 the FIRST buy is exactly as before, and after it the book may buy the coin again whenever its rule fires and
// it no longer holds the coin — judged SEPARATELY ON EACH STOP AND EXIT (the 20% row can be back in while the 60% row still holds
// its first buy). No waiting time (owner: "no wait"). A re-buy carries only the rows that are free, is marked (`again`) and is
// reported apart from first buys everywhere, so the older-8 comparison (first buys only) stays intact.
// REBUY_MAX_PER_DAY is a safety limit for the server, not a strategy rule: one book cannot re-buy one coin more often than this in 24 hours.
export const REBUY_MAX_PER_DAY = 50;

// ───────────────────────── older-11: WHICH BOOKS BUY (owner's decisions 24–25 Nov 2026) ─────────────────────────
// A run chooses its books (main.mjs, OLDER_BOOKS):
//   unset or "legacy" : the 29 books of older-10, exactly as before. VOL100K and DIP15K, the books added in older-11, stay off.
//   "older-11"        : BOOKS_11 below — the owner's list for the scored week (the pool books; the launch-curve books are not
//                       in it because this feed cannot observe or price a coin before it migrates).
//   "A,B,C"           : exactly those books.
// A book that is not active makes NO new buy. Nothing else changes for it: its open positions are managed to their exits by the
// same rules, its history stays in the database and in the reports. Every other book decides exactly as it would have decided
// with all books active (the list is applied to the finished candidates, after every book has been evaluated).
// VOL100K: the VOL rule at $100,000 of volume in 5 minutes, any age after migration, $40K–$2M at the buy (owner, 24 Nov).
export const NEW_IN_11 = ["VOL100K", "DIP15K"];
export const BOOKS_11 = ["HH-BREAK", "HH-BREAK-4H", "VOL100K", "VOL300K", "VOL500K", "FAST-1M", "FAST-3M", "FAST-5M", "RUG450", "RUG950", "LOW4H", "SLOW-BUILD", "DIP15K"];
export const BOOKS_LEGACY = FAMILIES.filter((f) => !NEW_IN_11.includes(f));
/** the active books for a run name; throws on an unknown book or a clock book without the book whose buys it shares */
// THE YOUNGEST AGE OF THE "OLDER" BOOKS IN A RUN (older-11; a run sets it with OLDER_MIN_AGE_H, main.mjs). MIN_AGE_S (8 hours)
// stays the age-group boundary and the default. A run may evaluate the books that normally start at 8 hours from an earlier
// age: MIN_AGE_11_S (6 hours) is the value of the older-11 run. Between that age and 8 hours a coin is in age group A0, as it
// already is for the any-age books; AGE-DOUBLE is not evaluated there (it needs an age-group start, and A0 has none).
// The any-age books (VOL, RUG, DIP15K) are unaffected: they are evaluated from migration on in every run.
export const MIN_AGE_11_S = 6 * 3600;
// what a run name switches on (scripts/start.sh passes these to main.mjs; each can also be set on its own)
export const RUNS = { "older-11": { books: "older-11", gates: "older-11", rebuy: false, min_age_h: MIN_AGE_11_S / 3600 } };
export function booksOf(name) {
  const n = (name ?? "").trim();
  const list = !n || n === "legacy" ? BOOKS_LEGACY : n === "older-11" ? BOOKS_11 : n.split(",").map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for (const f of list) { if (!FAMILIES.includes(f)) throw new Error(`unknown book "${f}"`); const base = CLOCK[f]?.base; if (base && !list.includes(base)) throw new Error(`${f} shares the buys of ${base}, which is not in the list`); }
  return [...new Set(list)];
}

// ───────────────────────── older-11: THE OWNER'S GATES (decisions of 24–25 Nov 2026) ─────────────────────────
// At a buy signal the engine asks the token-data provider for ONE reading of the coin (gates.mjs). The pending buy is filled
// only if that reading had ARRIVED BY THE TIME OF THE BUY (decision + BUY_DELAY_MS + GATE_WAIT_S) and every gate passes for at
// least one of the books that fired. A reading that is missing, failed or late is never treated as zero: the buy is cancelled
// and the reason recorded; a late reading is still stored on the cancelled entry, marked late, so it can be seen what it said.
// Fractions are 0–1; ranges are inclusive [low, high], high = null for "no upper limit"; exclusiveHi makes the upper limit strict.
//   buy    — the range that admits a buy: the UNION of the owner's main range and his second option
//   main / second — his two options; every filled buy records which of them it satisfies, so both are reported from the same buys
// byFamily — a book's own settings replace the common ones (a clock book uses its base book's). The rug books are defined by a
//   watched peak of $450K / $950K, so "has reached $100K" is not asked of them, and the owner gave them their own top-10 rule (under 12%).
//   Fees of at least 5 SOL apply to every book, the rug books included (owner, 25 Nov 10:43).
// A run switches the gates on with OLDER_GATES=older-11 (main.mjs); without it nothing is asked and nothing is gated.
export const GATE_WAIT_S = 0; // extra seconds after the scheduled buy within which a reading still counts (0 = it must be there at the buy)
export const GATES_11 = {
  top10: { buy: [0.18, 0.30] },                                                    // owner 25 Nov 09:22: "between 18% and 30%"; never under 10% is implied
  bundlers: { buy: [0.05, 0.50], main: [0.07, 0.50], second: [0.05, 0.20] },       // owner 24 Nov 16:02 (7–50%) and 25 Nov 09:22 (second option 5–20%)
  insiders: { buy: [0.025, 0.40], main: [0.025, 0.25], second: [0.025, 0.40] },    // owner 24 Nov 16:02 (2.5–25%) and 25 Nov 09:22 (second option 2.5–40%)
  snipers: { buy: [0, 0.05], exclusiveHi: true },                                  // "snipers under 5%"
  fees_sol: { buy: [5, null] },                                                    // owner 25 Nov 09:22: "fees I want to increase to 5 SOL"
  twitter: true,                                                                   // a Twitter link of any kind (a single tweet counts)
  ath_usd: { buy: [100_000, null] },                                               // "has reached $100K before" — the provider's all-time high
  bothZeroBlocks: true,                                                            // "zero bundles, zero insiders, never buy" (already implied by the minimums)
  byFamily: { // rug books — owner 25 Nov 10:49: top 10 UNDER 12% (read at the buy, i.e. after the fall: the large wallets have sold); no floor; no "$100K" test (their own peak rule is stricter)
    RUG450: { top10: { buy: [0, 0.12], exclusiveHi: true }, ath_usd: null }, RUG950: { top10: { buy: [0, 0.12], exclusiveHi: true }, ath_usd: null },
    "RUG450-BOUNCE": { top10: { buy: [0, 0.12], exclusiveHi: true }, ath_usd: null }, "RUG950-BOUNCE": { top10: { buy: [0, 0.12], exclusiveHi: true }, ath_usd: null } },
};



====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/store.mjs
====================================================================================================
// store.mjs — the project's own SQLite database (separate from the old tracker.db). Compact by design:
// minute bars (retained BAR_RETAIN_DAYS), decisions/rejections, entries with their exits, feed events. No raw trade copy.
import Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { statSync, statfsSync } from "node:fs";
import { dirname } from "node:path";
import { BAR_RETAIN_DAYS, SPEC_VER, FAMILIES, STOPS, CLOCK, FEE_BOOK } from "./spec.mjs";
import { setNextId } from "./book.mjs";

export class Store {
  constructor(path) {
    this.path = path; const db = (this.db = new Database(path)); db.pragma("journal_mode = WAL"); db.pragma("synchronous = NORMAL"); db.pragma("busy_timeout = 5000");
    db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta (k TEXT PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coins (mint TEXT PRIMARY KEY, pool TEXT, create_ts INTEGER, create_src TEXT, pump_origin TEXT, first_seen INTEGER, last_px REAL, last_t REAL, info TEXT);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bars (mint TEXT, m INTEGER, o REAL, h REAL, l REAL, c REAL, v REAL, nb INTEGER, ns INTEGER, buyers INTEGER, src TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (mint, m)) WITHOUT ROWID;
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS bars_m ON bars(m);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS decisions (mint TEXT, ag TEXT, family TEXT, t REAL, entry_id INTEGER, detail TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (mint, ag, family)) WITHOUT ROWID;
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notes (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, mint TEXT, ag TEXT, family TEXT, t REAL, reason TEXT, detail TEXT);
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS notes_f ON notes(family, reason);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entries (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, mint TEXT, status TEXT, done INTEGER DEFAULT 0, decision_t REAL, json TEXT);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pools (pool TEXT PRIMARY KEY, mint TEXT, quote TEXT, creator TEXT) WITHOUT ROWID;
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS feed (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, t REAL, ev TEXT, detail TEXT);`);
    db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO meta VALUES ('spec', ?)").run(SPEC_VER);
    this.q = {
      bar: db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bars VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)"),
      barBf: db.prepare("INSERT INTO bars VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(mint, m) DO UPDATE SET o=excluded.o, h=excluded.h, l=excluded.l, c=excluded.c, v=excluded.v, nb=excluded.nb, ns=excluded.ns, buyers=excluded.buyers, src=excluded.src WHERE bars.src <> 'live'"),
      dec: db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO decisions VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)"),
      note: db.prepare("INSERT INTO notes (mint, ag, family, t, reason, detail) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)"),
      ent: db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO entries (id, mint, status, done, decision_t, json) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)"),
      feed: db.prepare("INSERT INTO feed (t, ev, detail) VALUES (?,?,?)"),
      coin: db.prepare("INSERT INTO coins (mint, pool, create_ts, create_src, pump_origin, first_seen, info) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(mint) DO UPDATE SET pool=COALESCE(coins.pool, excluded.pool), create_ts=COALESCE(coins.create_ts, excluded.create_ts), create_src=COALESCE(coins.create_src, excluded.create_src), pump_origin=COALESCE(coins.pump_origin, excluded.pump_origin), info=COALESCE(excluded.info, coins.info)"),
      last: db.prepare("UPDATE coins SET last_px=?, last_t=? WHERE mint=?"),
      mode: db.prepare("UPDATE coins SET pool=COALESCE(?, pool), info=? WHERE mint=?"),
    };
    this.tx = db.transaction((fn) => fn());
  }
  saveBar(mint, m, b) { this.q.bar.run(mint, m, b.o, b.h, b.l, b.c, b.v, b.nb, b.ns, b.nbuyers ?? b.buyers?.size ?? 0, b.bf ? "backfill" : "live"); }
  /** AUDIT copy of a back-filled bar with its acceptance label ('backfill' = accepted into decision history, 'backfill-rejected' =
   *  returned by the provider but NOT used). It never overwrites a live row. Nothing reads these rows back into decisions. */
  saveBackfillBar(mint, m, b, accepted) { this.q.barBf.run(mint, m, b.o, b.h, b.l, b.c, b.v || 0, b.nb || 0, b.ns || 0, 0, accepted ? "backfill" : "backfill-rejected"); }
  saveDecision(mint, ag, family, t, entryId, detail) { this.q.dec.run(mint, ag, family, t, entryId, JSON.stringify(detail ?? null)); }
  /** older-11: a buy cancelled for want of a provider reading does not use up the book's one buy (engine._giveBack): its marks go */
  dropDecisions(entryId) { return this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM decisions WHERE entry_id = ?").run(entryId).changes; }
  saveNote(mint, n) { this.q.note.run(mint, n.ag, n.family, n.t, n.reason, JSON.stringify(n.detail ?? null)); if (n.family === "SLOW-BUILD" || n.family === "LOW4H-LIQ25K" || (n.family === "SECOND-WAVE" && n.reason !== "window not covered")) this.q.dec.run(mint, n.ag, n.family, n.t, null, JSON.stringify({ rejected: n.reason, ...n.detail })); }
  saveEntry(e) { const done = e.status === "cancelled" || (e.status === "filled" && e.versions.every((v) => v.status === "closed") && e.runner.horizonDone) ? 1 : 0; this.q.ent.run(e.id, e.mint, e.status, done, e.decisionT, JSON.stringify(e)); }
  /** `pumpOrigin` column = launchpad/origin label; `info` = the launchpad-neutral record (venues, token program, sources, mode, risk…) */
  saveCoin(mint, { pool = null, createTs = null, createSrc = null, pumpOrigin = null, firstSeen = null, info = null } = {}) { this.q.coin.run(mint, pool, createTs, createSrc, pumpOrigin, firstSeen, info ? JSON.stringify(info) : null); }
  /** a coin changed observation method (sampled → live): pool and record are overwritten, not merged */
  setCoinMode(mint, pool, info) { this.q.mode.run(pool ?? null, JSON.stringify(info ?? {}), mint); }
  savePool(pool, i) { this.db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO pools VALUES (?,?,?,?)").run(pool, i.mint, i.quote, i.creator ?? null); }
  loadPools() { const m = new Map(); for (const r of this.db.prepare("SELECT pool, mint, quote, creator FROM pools").iterate()) m.set(r.pool, { mint: r.mint, quote: r.quote, creator: r.creator }); return m; }
  saveLast(mint, c) { this.q.last.run(c.series.lastPx, c.series.lastT, mint); }
  feedEvent(t, ev, detail = "") { this.q.feed.run(t, ev, String(detail).slice(0, 300)); }
  /** restart: reload the one-entry flags, unfinished entries and known coins into an engine */
  restore(engine) {
    // 1) coins with their identity and OBSERVATION MODE first — a sampled coin must come back as sampled, or its positions
    //    would be stranded without quotes (and the live adapter would wrongly claim it)
    for (const r of this.db.prepare("SELECT mint, pool, create_ts, info FROM coins").iterate()) { const info = r.info ? JSON.parse(r.info) : null; engine.coin(r.mint, { pool: r.pool, createTs: r.create_ts, info, mode: info?.monitoring_mode === "polled" ? "polled" : null }); }
    // 2) one-entry flags. First RECONCILE: every stored entry must have its flags (an entry written by a version that did not
    //    commit the two together, or carried in by a migration, may lack them) — otherwise the same book could buy the coin again.
    this.tx(() => { for (const r of this.db.prepare("SELECT id, json FROM entries").all()) { const e = JSON.parse(r.json); if (e.attempt_returned) continue; /* its marks were taken back on purpose (no provider reading) */ for (const f of e.families || []) this.q.dec.run(e.mint, e.ag, f, e.decisionT, e.id, JSON.stringify((e.cands || []).find((c) => c.family === f)?.detail ?? null)); } });
    for (const r of this.db.prepare("SELECT mint, ag, family FROM decisions").iterate()) engine.restoreDone(r.mint, r.ag, r.family);
    // 3) unfinished entries (pending, open, or with a sale still waiting for a price)
    const maxId = this.db.prepare("SELECT MAX(id) m FROM entries").get().m || 0;
    for (const r of this.db.prepare("SELECT id, json FROM entries WHERE done = 0 ORDER BY id").all()) { // .all(): corrections below write to the database
      const e = JSON.parse(r.json), want = e.exec === "routed-quote" ? "polled" : "live", c = engine.coin(e.mint);
      // 4) the entry's own execution method decides how its coin is observed (see engine.forceMode). Two unfinished entries of
      //    different methods on one coin cannot both be served: the first keeps the coin, the other is flagged, never hidden.
      if (c.mode !== want) { if (engine.byMint.get(e.mint)?.size) { const f = "observation-method conflict after restart: this position cannot be priced by the coin's current method"; if (!e.flags.includes(f)) e.flags.push(f); }
        else if (engine.forceMode(e.mint, want, e.pool)) { this.setCoinMode(e.mint, e.pool ?? null, c.info); this.feedEvent(Date.now() / 1000, "restore: coin mode corrected", `${e.mint} → ${want} (entry ${e.id})`); } }
      engine.addEntry(e); }
    setNextId(maxId + 1);
  }
  /** the project's first-ever start (for the 24 h "startup" tag); set once */
  firstStart(now) { this.db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO meta VALUES ('first_start', ?)").run(String(now)); return Number(this.db.prepare("SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k='first_start'").get().v); }
  /** WHEN EACH BOOK AND EACH STOP BEGAN COUNTING on this database (display only). Books and stops added after the first start
   *  have no earlier trades, so their totals cover a shorter period — the page shows the start next to each.
   *  Set once per book / stop and then kept. A book's start is the earliest time it was evaluated on this database: its first
   *  recorded refusal or decision (a book writes refusals from its first minute), never earlier than the first start; a book
   *  with nothing on record yet starts now. A stop's start is the first buy that carries it; a stop every buy carries started
   *  with the database. */
  since(now) {
    if (this._since) return this._since;
    const get = (k) => { const r = this.db.prepare("SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k=?").get(k); return r ? Number(r.v) : null; }, put = (k, v) => this.db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO meta VALUES (?, ?)").run(k, String(v));
    const first = get("first_start") ?? now, families = {}, stops = {};
    for (const f of FAMILIES) { if (CLOCK[f] && families[CLOCK[f].base] != null) { families[f] = families[CLOCK[f].base]; continue; } // a clock book shares its base book's buys: same start
      let t = get("since:" + f);
      if (t === null) { const a = this.db.prepare("SELECT MIN(t) t FROM notes WHERE family=?").get(f)?.t, b = this.db.prepare("SELECT MIN(t) t FROM decisions WHERE family=?").get(f)?.t;
        const seen = [a, b].filter((x) => x != null); t = Math.max(first, seen.length ? Math.min(...seen) : now); put("since:" + f, t); }
      families[f] = t; }
    { let t = get("since:" + FEE_BOOK); if (t === null) { t = now; put("since:" + FEE_BOOK, t); } families[FEE_BOOK] = t; } // fee coins are bought only from the first start with that rule
    let all = null;
    for (const s of STOPS) { let t = get("since:stop:" + s);
      if (t === null) { all ??= this.allEntries(); const has = all.filter((e) => (e.versions || []).some((v) => v.stop === s)), lacks = all.some((e) => !(e.versions || []).some((v) => v.stop === s));
        t = !lacks ? first : has.length ? Math.min(...has.map((e) => e.decisionT)) : now; put("since:stop:" + s, t); }
      stops[s] = t; }
    return (this._since = { first, families, stops });
  }
  heartbeat(t) { this.db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta VALUES ('alive', ?)").run(String(t)); }
  /** the time the previous run was last known alive: the restart gap starts there (null on a first run) */
  lastAlive() { const r = this.db.prepare("SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k='alive'").get(); return r ? Number(r.v) : null; }
  allEntries() { return this.db.prepare("SELECT json FROM entries").all().map((r) => JSON.parse(r.json)); }
  noteCounts() { return this.db.prepare("SELECT family, reason, COUNT(*) n FROM notes GROUP BY family, reason ORDER BY family, n DESC").all(); }
  retention(now) { const cut = Math.floor(now / 60) - BAR_RETAIN_DAYS * 1440; return this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM bars WHERE m < ?").run(cut).changes; }
  disk() {
    let db = 0, wal = 0; try { db = statSync(this.path).size; } catch {} try { wal = statSync(this.path + "-wal").size; } catch {}
    let freeGb = null; try { const s = statfsSync(dirname(this.path)); freeGb = +((s.bavail * s.bsize) / 2 ** 30).toFixed(2); } catch {}
    const n = (sql) => { try { return this.db.prepare(sql).get().n; } catch { return null; } };
    return { db_mb: +(db / 2 ** 20).toFixed(1), wal_mb: +(wal / 2 ** 20).toFixed(1), free_gb: freeGb, bars: n("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM bars"), coins: n("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM coins"), notes: n("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM notes"), entries: n("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM entries") };
  }
}

FILE: older/src/validate-tx.mjs

// validate-tx.mjs — transaction-level evidence for the decoder, run on the server (needs RPC_HTTP; read-only, prints no secrets):
//   node older/src/validate-tx.mjs [N=120] > older/docs/tx-validation.json
// For N recent successful PumpSwap transactions it compares each Buy/Sell event with the transaction's own token-balance
// changes on the pool's two vaults:
//   • are pool_*_token_reserves the vault balances BEFORE or AFTER the swap?   (pre / post / neither, by instruction name and side)
//   • does the constant product on EFFECTIVE reserves reproduce the traded amount, under each interpretation?
//   • does the user's actual SOL debit/credit match user_quote_amount_in/out?  (the fee measure the engine uses)
// Only transactions with exactly one swap on the pool are scored (multi-hop / several swaps in one transaction are listed separately).
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
const ENVF = process.env.OLDER_ENV || "/root/older/.env", ALT = "/root/tracker/.env";
for (const f of [ENVF, ALT]) if (existsSync(f)) for (const l of readFileSync(f, "utf8").split("\n")) { const m = l.match(/^\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*(.*)\s*$/); if (m && process.env[m[1]] === undefined) process.env[m[1]] = m[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, ""); }
if (!process.env.RPC_HTTP && process.env.HELIUS_API_KEY) process.env.RPC_HTTP = "https://mainnet.helius-rpc.com/?api-key=" + process.env.HELIUS_API_KEY;
if (!process.env.RPC_HTTP) { console.error("RPC_HTTP is not set"); process.exit(1); }
const { rpc, parsePool } = await import("./feed.mjs"); const { PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM, WSOL, eventsFromLogs, IDL_PIN } = await import("./decoder.mjs");
const N = Number(process.argv[2] || 120), nap = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const out = { at: new Date().toISOString(), idl: IDL_PIN, asked: N, scored: 0, skipped: {}, reserves: {}, curve: { pre: 0, post: 0, both: 0, neither: 0 }, curve_large_trades: { pre: 0, post: 0, both: 0, neither: 0 }, user_amount: { match: 0, differ: 0 }, multi_swap: { txs: 0, chain_pre_ok: 0, chain_post_ok: 0, chain_neither: 0 }, virtual: { zero: 0, negative: 0, positive: 0, absent: 0 }, fee_rate: { buy: [], sell: [] }, samples: [] };
const skip = (k) => (out.skipped[k] = (out.skipped[k] || 0) + 1), near = (a, b, tol = 2) => Math.abs(Number(a) - Number(b)) <= tol;
const sigs = await rpc("getSignaturesForAddress", [PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM, { limit: Math.min(1000, N * 4) }]);
const poolCache = new Map();
for (const s of sigs) {
  if (out.scored >= N) break; if (s.err) { skip("failed tx"); continue; }
  let tx; try { tx = await rpc("getTransaction", [s.signature, { encoding: "jsonParsed", maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 0, commitment: "confirmed" }]); } catch { skip("getTransaction error"); await nap(300); continue; }
  await nap(120); if (!tx?.meta) { skip("no meta"); continue; }
  const evs = eventsFromLogs(tx.meta.logMessages).filter((e) => e.type === "buy" || e.type === "sell"); if (!evs.length) { skip("no swap event"); continue; }
  const byPool = new Map(); for (const e of evs) byPool.set(e.pool, (byPool.get(e.pool) || 0) + 1);
  // several swaps on ONE pool inside one transaction: consecutive events must chain. Under the "pre" reading the next event's
  // base reserve = this one's ∓ its amount; under "post" the PREVIOUS event's reserve ± this amount = this one's.
  for (const [pool, n] of byPool) if (n > 1) { out.multi_swap.txs++; const seq = evs.filter((e) => e.pool === pool);
    for (let k = 0; k + 1 < seq.length; k++) { const a = seq[k], b = seq[k + 1], d = (x) => (x.isBuy ? -x.baseAmt : x.baseAmt);
      if (near(a.poolBase + d(a), b.poolBase)) out.multi_swap.chain_pre_ok++; else if (near(a.poolBase + d(b), b.poolBase)) out.multi_swap.chain_post_ok++; else out.multi_swap.chain_neither++; } }
  for (const e of evs) {
    if (byPool.get(e.pool) !== 1) { skip("several swaps on one pool in the tx (see multi_swap)"); continue; }
    let p = poolCache.get(e.pool); if (!p) { try { const r = await rpc("getAccountInfo", [e.pool, { encoding: "base64" }]); p = r?.value ? parsePool(r.value.data[0]) : null; poolCache.set(e.pool, p); await nap(120); } catch { skip("pool read error"); continue; } }
    if (!p) { skip("pool unreadable"); continue; } if (p.quoteMint !== WSOL) { skip("non-SOL pool"); continue; }
    const keys = tx.transaction.message.accountKeys.map((k) => k.pubkey ?? k), bi = keys.indexOf(p.poolBaseAta), qi = keys.indexOf(p.poolQuoteAta);
    const bal = (arr, i) => arr.find((x) => x.accountIndex === i)?.uiTokenAmount?.amount;
    const b0 = bal(tx.meta.preTokenBalances, bi), b1 = bal(tx.meta.postTokenBalances, bi), q0 = bal(tx.meta.preTokenBalances, qi), q1 = bal(tx.meta.postTokenBalances, qi);
    if ([b0, b1, q0, q1].some((x) => x == null)) { skip("vault balances not in tx"); continue; }
    const key = `${e.ix || "legacy"}|${e.type}`, R = (out.reserves[key] ||= { base_pre: 0, base_post: 0, base_neither: 0, quote_pre: 0, quote_post: 0, quote_neither: 0 });
    const bm = near(e.poolBase, b0) ? "pre" : near(e.poolBase, b1) ? "post" : "neither", qm = near(e.poolQuote, q0) ? "pre" : near(e.poolQuote, q1) ? "post" : "neither";
    R["base_" + bm]++; R["quote_" + qm]++;
    const vq = e.virtualQuote; if (vq == null) out.virtual.absent++; else if (vq === 0n) out.virtual.zero++; else if (vq < 0n) out.virtual.negative++; else out.virtual.positive++;
    const exactIn = e.isBuy && typeof e.ix === "string" && e.ix.startsWith("buy_exact_quote_in"); // same field rule as the decoder
    const B = Number(e.poolBase), E = Number(e.poolQuote + (vq ?? 0n)), b = Number(e.baseAmt), q = Number(exactIn ? e.userQuote : e.quoteAmt), paidAmt = Number(exactIn ? e.quoteAmt : e.userQuote);
    const preQ = e.isBuy ? (E * b) / (B - b) : (E * b) / (B + b), postQ = e.isBuy ? (E * b) / (B + b) : (E * b) / (B - b), okPre = Math.abs(preQ - q) / q < 0.003, okPost = Math.abs(postQ - q) / q < 0.003;
    const c = okPre && okPost ? "both" : okPre ? "pre" : okPost ? "post" : "neither"; out.curve[c]++; if (b / B > 0.005) out.curve_large_trades[c]++;
    // the user's own SOL movement (fee payer, native balance, net of the transaction fee) vs the event's user amount
    const ui = keys.indexOf(e.user); let userOk = null;
    if (ui >= 0) { const dl = tx.meta.postBalances[ui] - tx.meta.preBalances[ui] + (ui === 0 ? tx.meta.fee : 0); userOk = Math.abs(Math.abs(dl) - paidAmt) / paidAmt < 0.02; out.user_amount[userOk ? "match" : "differ"]++; }
    const fr = e.isBuy ? paidAmt / q - 1 : 1 - paidAmt / q; out.fee_rate[e.isBuy ? "buy" : "sell"].push(+fr.toFixed(5));
    out.scored++;
    if (out.samples.length < 12) out.samples.push({ sig: s.signature, slot: tx.slot, ix: e.ix, type: e.type, event: { pool_base: String(e.poolBase), pool_quote: String(e.poolQuote), virtual_quote: vq == null ? null : String(vq), base_amt: String(e.baseAmt), quote_amt: String(e.quoteAmt), user_quote: String(e.userQuote), lp_bps: e.lpBps, protocol_bps: e.protoBps, creator_bps: e.creatorBps, cashback_bps: e.cashbackBps, buyback_bps: e.buybackBps, holder_bps: e.holderBps }, vault: { base_pre: b0, base_post: b1, quote_pre: q0, quote_post: q1 }, reserves_are: { base: bm, quote: qm }, curve_fit: c, user_amount_matches: userOk, fee_rate: +fr.toFixed(5) });
  }
}
const stat = (a) => { if (!a.length) return null; const s = [...a].sort((x, y) => x - y); return { n: s.length, min: s[0], median: s[Math.floor(s.length / 2)], max: s[s.length - 1] }; };
out.fee_rate = { buy: stat(out.fee_rate.buy), sell: stat(out.fee_rate.sell) };
out.verdict = "reserves: see `reserves` (pre vs post per instruction and side); curve: `curve_large_trades` is the discriminating sample (small trades fit both)";
console.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));

FILE: older/src/verify.mjs
====================================================================================================
// verify.mjs — DIAGNOSTIC account read for each simulated entry (one per unique entry, never per stop version).
// After a fill, ONE batched getMultipleAccounts (pool + its two vaults) is compared with the reconstructed state the fill used.
// The read never replaces the simulated fill (a later, more favourable observation must not rewrite history). A snapshot from a
// later slot may legitimately differ, so the record keeps both slots and the request/response times; only a difference with NO
// intervening event on that pool is evidence of a decoder problem (flagged `suspect`).
import { rpc, parsePool } from "./feed.mjs";
export const vf = { done: 0, failed: 0, within1pct: 0, suspect: 0, calls: 0 };
const atas = new Map(); // pool → { base, quote }

export async function verifyEntry(e, c, { store, lastSlotOf = () => null } = {}) {
  const req = Date.now() / 1000, eventsAtFill = c.eventCount ?? null;
  try {
    let a = atas.get(e.pool);
    if (!a) { vf.calls++; const r = await rpc("getAccountInfo", [e.pool, { encoding: "base64", commitment: "confirmed" }]); const p = r?.value ? parsePool(r.value.data[0]) : null; if (!p) throw new Error("pool account unreadable"); a = { base: p.poolBaseAta, quote: p.poolQuoteAta }; atas.set(e.pool, a); }
    vf.calls++; const r = await rpc("getMultipleAccounts", [[e.pool, a.base, a.quote], { encoding: "base64", commitment: "confirmed" }]);
    const resp = Date.now() / 1000, [pa, ba, qa] = r.value; if (!pa || !ba || !qa) throw new Error("account missing");
    const p = parsePool(pa.data[0]), base = Number(Buffer.from(ba.data[0], "base64").readBigUInt64LE(64)) / 1e6, vault = Buffer.from(qa.data[0], "base64").readBigUInt64LE(64);
    const quoteEff = Number(vault + p.virtualQuote) / 1e9;
    const dBase = base / e.fill.base - 1, dQuote = quoteEff / e.fill.quote_sol - 1, moved = (c.eventCount ?? null) !== eventsAtFill;
    e.verify = { commitment: "confirmed", context_slot: r.context?.slot ?? null, last_event_slot: lastSlotOf(e.mint), req_t: req, resp_t: +resp.toFixed(3), fill_t: e.fill.t, account: { base, quote_vault: Number(vault) / 1e9, virtual_quote: Number(p.virtualQuote) / 1e9, quote_eff: quoteEff }, reconstructed: { base: e.fill.base, quote_eff: e.fill.quote_sol }, diff_base: +dBase.toFixed(5), diff_quote: +dQuote.toFixed(5), events_between: moved };
    const close = Math.abs(dBase) < 0.01 && Math.abs(dQuote) < 0.01; if (close) vf.within1pct++;
    // comparable only when the account snapshot is at or after the last event we reconstructed from AND no event arrived for this
    // coin while the read was in flight; otherwise a difference is expected and the check is recorded as inconclusive
    const aligned = !moved && e.verify.context_slot != null && e.verify.last_event_slot != null && e.verify.context_slot >= e.verify.last_event_slot;
    e.verify.comparable = aligned;
    if (!close && aligned) { e.verify.suspect = true; e.flags.push("entry verification: account state differs from the reconstructed state at a comparable slot"); vf.suspect++; }
    else if (!close) vf.inconclusive = (vf.inconclusive || 0) + 1;
    vf.done++;
  } catch (x) { vf.failed++; e.verify = { error: String(x.message).slice(0, 160), req_t: req }; }
  store?.saveEntry(e);
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/warm.mjs
====================================================================================================
// warm.mjs — WARM RESTART: the price memory survives a planned stop (owner's decision, 22 Nov 2026).
// On SIGINT/SIGTERM every observed coin's Series (exact observations, minute bars, anchors, coverage marks) and its in-memory
// signal state (age-group references, notes already logged, a SECOND-WAVE setup, the RUG peak / armed setup) are written to one
// file. The next start reads it back IF it is recent (spec WARM_MAX_GAP_S) and complete; otherwise the start is cold, as before.
//   - nothing is rounded, summarised or rebuilt: what is read back is exactly what was in memory at the stop
//   - the time away is NOT observed. It stays in the coverage as a gap; Coverage.up() marks it "warm" (tolerated by hour-scale
//     windows, recorded on every decision that spans it) only if observation resumes within WARM_MAX_GAP_S of the stop
//   - exact windows and fresh crossings never span it (Series.continuous is unchanged)
//   - a crash writes no file → cold start. The file is used once: after reading it is renamed (never read by a later start)
//   - a file that is cut short, older than the limit, from the future, or of another format is ignored → cold start
// No key or secret is read or written here. The file holds prices, volumes and buyer wallet addresses already in memory.
import { openSync, writeSync, closeSync, renameSync, readFileSync, existsSync, unlinkSync } from "node:fs";
import { Series } from "./series.mjs";
import { WARM_MAX_GAP_S, SPEC_VER } from "./spec.mjs";

const FORMAT = 1;
// EVERY gap is kept, however old (eighth review, C1): state can depend on observation from hours or days back (a RUG peak has
// no time limit), and a gap that is dropped would make a broken stretch look unbroken. They are a few numbers each.
const covOut = (c) => ({ gaps: c.gaps, upSince: c.upSince, down_since: c.down ? c.lastDown : null });

/** write the snapshot (synchronously: the process is stopping). Returns { coins, bytes, ms }. */
export function saveWarm(file, engine, t = Date.now() / 1000) {
  const t0 = Date.now(), tmp = file + ".tmp", fd = openSync(tmp, "w"); let n = 0, bytes = 0;
  const line = (o) => { const s = JSON.stringify(o) + "\n"; bytes += s.length; writeSync(fd, s); };
  try {
    line({ warm: FORMAT, t, spec: SPEC_VER, cov: covOut(engine.cov), pollCov: covOut(engine.pollCov) });
    for (const [mint, c] of engine.coins) { const sr = c.series; if (sr.lastT === null && !sr.bf.size) continue; // never observed: nothing to keep
      const groups = {}; for (const [ag, g] of Object.entries(c.groups || {})) groups[ag] = { ref: g.ref ?? null, done: g.done || {}, logged: g.logged || {} };
      // carried state is saved only while its own provenance holds AT THE STOP: a RUG peak / setup that is no longer steady up to
      // now, or a SECOND-WAVE setup whose exact coverage has broken, is not written (it would be dropped at the next trade anyway)
      const rug = c.rug?.pk && sr.steady(c.rug.pk.t, t) ? c.rug : null, sw = c.sw && sr.covered(c.sw.seedT, t, true) ? c.sw : null;
      line({ m: mint, mode: c.mode, pool: c.pool ?? null, createTs: c.createTs ?? null, supply: c.supply ?? null, lostAt: c.lostAt ?? null, sw, rug, groups, ...(c.gateHold ? { hold: c.gateHold } : {}), s: sr.dump() }); n++; }
    line({ end: true, coins: n });
  } finally { closeSync(fd); }
  renameSync(tmp, file); // atomic: a file that exists is complete
  return { coins: n, bytes, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
}

/** read the snapshot back into a restored engine. Call AFTER store.restore (coins, modes and one-buy flags come from the
 *  database and win) and BEFORE engine.restartGap / the feed. Returns { loaded, why?, coins, skipped, age_s, stopped_at }. */
/** `fresh` (older-11): the snapshot was written by ANOTHER run's database and is carried into a new one (main.mjs,
 *  OLDER_WARM_FROM). Only what was OBSERVED comes across — prices, bars, coverage, the watched peaks and setups. What the other
 *  run had DECIDED does not: its one-buy marks and its already-logged notes stay behind, so the new run's first buy of a coin is
 *  a first buy and its refusals are logged from the start. */
export function loadWarm(file, engine, now = Date.now() / 1000, { fresh = false } = {}) {
  if (!existsSync(file)) return { loaded: false, why: "no snapshot (first start, or the last stop was not a planned one)" };
  let lines; try { lines = readFileSync(file, "utf8").split("\n").filter(Boolean); } catch (e) { return { loaded: false, why: "snapshot unreadable: " + e.message }; }
  const used = () => { try { renameSync(file, file + ".used"); } catch { try { unlinkSync(file); } catch {} } }; // one use only, whatever happens next
  let h, end; try { h = JSON.parse(lines[0]); end = JSON.parse(lines[lines.length - 1]); } catch { used(); return { loaded: false, why: "snapshot damaged" }; }
  if (h?.warm !== FORMAT) { used(); return { loaded: false, why: "snapshot of another format" }; }
  if (!end?.end || end.coins !== lines.length - 2) { used(); return { loaded: false, why: "snapshot incomplete" }; }
  const age = now - h.t; used();
  if (!(age >= 0)) return { loaded: false, why: "snapshot from the future (clock changed)" };
  if (age > WARM_MAX_GAP_S) return { loaded: false, why: `snapshot is ${Math.round(age)} s old (limit ${WARM_MAX_GAP_S} s)`, age_s: Math.round(age) };
  const rows = []; try { for (let i = 1; i < lines.length - 1; i++) rows.push(JSON.parse(lines[i])); } catch { return { loaded: false, why: "snapshot damaged" }; } // parsed in full before anything is applied
  // coverage: the earlier gaps come back as they were; the stop itself is closed by the next up() — "warm" only if it is short
  // An outage that was ALREADY under way at the stop is an ordinary outage up to the stop — its cause had nothing to do with the
  // restart and it is never forgiven (eighth review, C2). Only the stretch from the stop itself can become a warm gap.
  const back = (cov, s) => { cov.gaps = (s?.gaps || []).map((g) => g.slice()); cov.upSince = s?.upSince ?? null; if (s?.down_since != null && s.down_since < h.t) cov.gaps.push([s.down_since, h.t]); cov.resume(h.t); };
  back(engine.cov, h.cov); back(engine.pollCov, h.pollCov); // sampling: the same rule, closed by the first price reply
  let n = 0, skipped = 0; const curM = Math.floor(now / 60);
  for (const d of rows) {
    const had = engine.coins.has(d.m), c = engine.coin(d.m, { pool: d.pool, createTs: d.createTs, mode: d.mode });
    if (c.mode !== d.mode) { skipped++; continue; } // the database (and an unfinished entry's method) decides how a coin is observed: no mixed evidence
    c.series = Series.load(d.m, d.mode === "polled" ? engine.pollCov : engine.cov, d.s); c.warm = true;
    if (d.supply) c.supply = d.supply; c.lostAt = d.lostAt ?? null; c.sw = d.sw ?? null; c.rug = d.rug ?? null;
    if (!fresh && d.hold) c.gateHold = d.hold; // a book held on this coin after a buy that got no reading stays held over a planned restart (second opinion, 5)
    if (!fresh) for (const [ag, g] of Object.entries(d.groups || {})) { const x = (c.groups[ag] ||= { ref: null, done: {}, logged: {} }); x.ref = g.ref ?? x.ref; Object.assign(x.done, g.done || {}); Object.assign(x.logged, g.logged || {}); } // one-buy flags: the database's and the snapshot's together
    for (const m of c.series.bars.keys()) if (m >= curM - 1) (engine.dirtyBars.get(d.m) || engine.dirtyBars.set(d.m, new Set()).get(d.m)).add(m); // the stop minute's bar is still to be saved
    if (!had) engine.stats.warmNewCoins = (engine.stats.warmNewCoins || 0) + 1;
    n++;
  }
  engine.stats.warm = { coins: n, skipped, stopped_at: h.t, age_s: +age.toFixed(1), from_spec: h.spec ?? null, carried_from_another_run: fresh || undefined };
  return { loaded: true, coins: n, skipped, age_s: +age.toFixed(1), stopped_at: h.t };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/src/web.mjs
====================================================================================================
// web.mjs — the project's page (visual reference: the old books page). GET / (page), GET /api/state (JSON).
// Optional key: if OLDER_SITE_KEY is set, every request needs ?k=<key>. Read-only.
import http from "node:http";
import { bookRows, overlap } from "./report.mjs";
import * as S from "./spec.mjs";
import { BOOKS_PAGE, booksApi } from "./page.mjs";

export function startWeb({ port, store, engine, status, run = () => null }) { // run(): older-11 — the run's own books and settings, or null (every book, as before)
  const KEY = process.env.OLDER_SITE_KEY || "";
  const cache = new Map(), BUILD = String(process.env.OLDER_COMMIT || "dev").slice(0, 12) + ":" + Math.round(Date.now() / 1000); // changes on every start: the books page reloads itself when it sees a new one
  const state = (pop = "") => {
    const hit = cache.get(pop); if (hit && Date.now() - hit.at < 15000) return hit;
    const all = store.allEntries(), entries = pop ? all.filter((e) => (e.pop || "pumpswap-canonical") === pop) : all;
    const pops = {}; for (const e of all) { const k = (e.pop || "pumpswap-canonical") + " · " + (e.mode || "live") + " · " + (e.exec || "reconstructed-pool-state"); pops[k] = (pops[k] || 0) + 1; }
    const costs = { server: Number(process.env.OLDER_COST_SERVER_USD_MO || 0), gmgn: Number(process.env.OLDER_COST_GMGN_USD_MO || 30), rpc: Number(process.env.OLDER_COST_RPC_USD_MO || 0) };
    const R = run(), on = R?.books?.length ? new Set([...R.books, "*"]) : null; // a run that chose its books: the table, the overlap and the refusal counts show those books only
    const out = { at: Date.now(), spec: S.SPEC_VER, pop, populations: pops, run: R, rows: bookRows(entries, store.noteCounts()).filter((r) => !on || on.has(r.family)), overlap: overlap(entries).filter((o) => !on || (on.has(o.a) && on.has(o.b))), notes: store.noteCounts().filter((n) => !on || on.has(n.family)), status: status(), disk: store.disk(), costs,
      recent: entries.slice(-40).reverse().map((e) => ({ snap: e.snap ?? null, win: e.features ? { w1: e.features.w1 ?? null, w3: e.features.w3 ?? null, w5: e.features.w5 ?? null, vol_surge5: e.features.vol_surge5 ?? null, buyers15: e.features.buyers15 ?? null } : null, id: e.id, mint: e.mint, ag: e.ag, families: e.families, t: e.decisionT, mc: Math.round(e.signalMc), status: e.status, startup: !!e.startup, pop: e.pop || "pumpswap-canonical", mode: e.mode || "live", exec: e.exec || "reconstructed-pool-state", origin: e.token?.origin ?? null, risk: e.token?.risk_status ?? null, cadence: e.token?.cadence_s ?? e.evidence?.trigger?.cadence_s ?? null, route: e.fill?.quote?.route ?? null, fill_delay: e.fill?.delay_s ?? null, state_age: e.fill?.state_age_s ?? null, verify: e.verify ? (e.verify.error ? "error" : e.verify.suspect ? "SUSPECT" : `Δ ${(e.verify.diff_quote * 100).toFixed(2)}%`) : null, versions: e.versions.map((v) => ({ stop: v.stop, policy: v.policy, stall: v.stall, status: v.status, reason: v.reason, half: !!v.half, ret: v.exit ? +v.exit.ret.toFixed(3) : null })), runner_max: e.runner.maxX ? +e.runner.maxX.toFixed(2) : null, flags: e.flags })) };
    cache.set(pop, out); return out;
  };
  http.createServer((req, res) => {
    const u = new URL(req.url, "http://x");
    if (KEY && (u.searchParams.get("k") || u.searchParams.get("key")) !== KEY) { res.writeHead(403); return res.end("key required"); }
    if (u.pathname === "/api/books") { // one stop / exit policy / age group / population at a time; cached 8 s per selection
      const sk = Number(u.searchParams.get("stake")), sel = { stop: Number(u.searchParams.get("stop") || 0.25), policy: u.searchParams.get("policy") === "scale" ? "scale" : "trail", ag: u.searchParams.get("ag") || "", pop: u.searchParams.get("pop") || "", stake: S.STAKE_VIEWS.includes(sk) ? sk : S.STAKE_SOL, first: u.searchParams.get("buys") === "first", checks: ["pass", "late"].includes(u.searchParams.get("chk")) ? u.searchParams.get("chk") : "all" }, ck = "b|" + JSON.stringify(sel);
      let hit = cache.get(ck); if (!hit || Date.now() - hit.atMs > 8000) { hit = { atMs: Date.now(), body: JSON.stringify(booksApi(store.allEntries(), { ...sel, since: store.since ? store.since(Date.now() / 1000) : null, build: BUILD, run: run() })) }; cache.set(ck, hit); }
      res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "application/json" }); return res.end(hit.body); }
    if (u.pathname === "/table") { res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" }); return res.end(PAGE); }
    if (u.pathname === "/api/state") { res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "application/json" }); return res.end(JSON.stringify(state(u.searchParams.get("pop") || ""))); }
    if (u.pathname === "/") { res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" }); return res.end(BOOKS_PAGE); }
    res.writeHead(404); res.end();
  }).listen(port, () => console.log(`[web] :${port}`));
  return { state };
}

const PAGE = `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Older Coins Books</title>
<style>

.card{background:#141825;border:1px solid #232a3b;border-radius:10px;padding:12px;margin:12px 0}

table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:12.5px}th,td{padding:5px 7px;border-bottom:1px solid #1f2535;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}th{color:#9aa3b5;font-weight:600;position:sticky;top:0;background:#141825}
th.l,td.l{text-align:left}.tw{overflow:auto;max-height:70vh}.pos{color:#4ade80}.neg{color:#f87171}.mut{color:#6b7280}.open{color:#fbbf24}
select{background:#0f1320;color:#e6e8ef;border:1px solid #232a3b;border-radius:6px;padding:4px}
</style></head><body><div class="wrap">
<h1>Older coins — paper books <span class="sub" id="hd">loading…</span></h1>
<div class="sub" id="intro">PAPER ONLY. Pump.fun coins on their <b>canonical SOL-quoted PumpSwap pool only</b> (other venues are not covered — see Coverage), 8 hours to 90 days old at entry, entry market cap $40K–$1M, 0.2 SOL an entry. 29 entry families (21 from 8 hours old in 4 age groups; 8 from migration on in 5) × 5 trailing stops (20 / 25 / 30 / 40 / 60%) = 620 rows, each under two exit policies (pure trailing; sell half at 2× then trail) = 1,240 comparisons. Returns are after pool fees, price impact and a declared 0.0005 SOL per transaction. Fills are priced from the pool state in force at signal + 1 s (buy) and trigger + 0.7 s (sell). Rows sharing coins or entries are <b>correlated</b> (Overlap table) — not 1,240 independent results. Small samples are provisional.</div>
<div class="card"><div class="kpis" id="kpis"></div></div>
<div class="card"><h2>Books</h2>Population <select id="fpop"><option value="">all (mixed methods — see note)</option><option value="pumpswap-canonical">PumpSwap · live events · pool-state fills</option><option value="solana-wide">Solana-wide · sampled prices · routed quotes</option></select> Family <select id="ff"><option value="">all</option></select> Exit <select id="fp"><option value="trail">pure trailing</option><option value="scale">half at 2×, trail rest</option><option value="">both</option></select> Age <select id="fa"><option value="">all</option><option>A0</option><option>A1</option><option>A2</option><option>A3</option><option>A4</option></select>
<div class="tw"><table id="bt"></table></div><div class="sub"><b>Two observation methods are kept apart.</b> "PumpSwap · live" rows come from every decoded pool event and fills priced on the reconstructed pool. "Solana-wide" rows come from <b>sampled</b> prices (cadence shown per entry) and routed quotes: they can miss fast triggers, highs and stop crossings between samples, and HH-BREAK is not evaluated there (no volume source). A quote is an expected output, not a confirmed on-chain fill. Summing overlapping rows is not a funded portfolio's return. Fills priced before the reserve reading is validated are simulation only — the tile above says UNVALIDATED until transaction evidence exists.</div><div class="sub">avg / quantiles = after-cost return per entry (on cost: 0.2 SOL + the buy transaction). "startup" = entries in the first 24 h after the project first started (kept in the totals). −40% is the project's severe-loss measure; −50% / −60% shown too. "ex top 3" removes the three best coins. H1 / H2 = first / second half of closed trades by fill time. Runners = entries whose after-cost value reached 2×/3×/5×/10× of cost within 24 h of the fill (counted even after an exit; separate from profit).</div></div>
<div class="card"><h2>Overlap between families</h2><div class="tw"><table id="ov"></table></div></div>

<div class="card"><h2>Latest entries</h2><div class="tw"><table id="re"></table></div></div>
</div><script>
const $=(i)=>document.getElementById(i),k=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("k"),pc=(x)=>x==null?'<span class=mut>–</span>':'<span class='+(x>0?'pos':x<0?'neg':'')+'>'+(x*100).toFixed(1)+'%</span>',sol=(x)=>x==null?'–':'<span class='+(x>0?'pos':x<0?'neg':'')+'>'+x.toFixed(3)+'</span>',ago=(t)=>t?Math.round(Date.now()/1000-t)+' s ago':'–';
let S=null;
async function load(){const q=new URLSearchParams();if(k)q.set('k',k);if($('fpop').value)q.set('pop',$('fpop').value);S=await (await fetch('/api/state?'+q)).json();draw();}
function draw(){const st=S.status,d=S.disk,c=S.costs;
if(S.run){const R=S.run,fam=[...new Set(S.rows.map(r=>r.family))];$('intro').innerHTML='PAPER ONLY. Run <b>'+(R.name||'')+'</b>: the '+fam.length+' books of this run only ('+fam.join(', ')+'). Pump.fun coins on their canonical SOL-quoted PumpSwap pool, '+R.min_age_h+' hours to 90 days old at entry (the VOL, RUG and DIP books: any age after migration; a buy under 8 hours old is in age group A0), 0.2 SOL an entry, 5 trailing stops (20 / 25 / 30 / 40 / 60%), each under two exit policies (pure trailing; sell half at 2× then trail) = '+S.rows.length+' rows.'+(R.gates?' Every buy must pass the GMGN checks read at the buy; a missing or late reading cancels it.':'')+(R.rebuy?'':' Buying again is off.')+' Returns are after pool fees, price impact and a declared 0.0005 SOL per transaction. Rows sharing coins or entries are <b>correlated</b> (Overlap table). Small samples are provisional.'}
$('hd').textContent='spec '+S.spec+(S.run&&S.run.name?' · run '+S.run.name:'')+' · '+new Date(S.at).toLocaleString('en-GB',{timeZone:'Europe/London'})+' UK';
const net=S.rows.filter(r=>r.stop===0.2).reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.net_sol||0),0);
const dv=st.decoder,tv=st.tx_validation;
const kp=[['Feed',st.feed.connected?'up':'DOWN','last message '+ago(st.feed.lastMsg)+' · avg lag '+(st.feed.lag_ms_avg??'–')+' ms · reconnects '+st.feed.reconnects],['Events',st.feed.emitted,'held '+st.feed.held+' / replayed '+st.feed.replayed+' / rejected '+st.feed.rejected],['Coins in memory',st.engine.coins,'creation unknown: '+st.engine.noCreation+' · over 90 days: '+st.engine.tooOld],['Entries',st.engine.entries,'candidates '+st.engine.cands+' · open '+(st.engine.open??0)],['Reserve reading: '+dv.mode,(tv?'validated':'UNVALIDATED'),'fits pre '+dv.fitPre+' · post '+dv.fitPost+' · both '+dv.fitBoth+' · neither '+dv.fitNeither+' · chain ok '+dv.chainOk+' / bad '+dv.chainBad],['Entry checks',(st.verify?.done??0)+' read','within 1%: '+(st.verify?.within1pct??0)+' · suspect: '+(st.verify?.suspect??0)+' · failed: '+(st.verify?.failed??0)],['Solana-wide',(st.poller?st.poller.watched+' watched':'off'),st.poller?('hot '+st.poller.hot+' · avg sample gap '+(st.poller.cadence_avg_s??'–')+' s · omitted '+st.poller.omitted+' · no route '+st.poller.noRoute+' · Jupiter credits '+st.jupiter.credits):'Jupiter adapter not enabled'],['SOL/USD',st.sol.px?'$'+st.sol.px.toFixed(2):'none',st.sol.src||''],['GMGN',st.gmgn.calls+' calls',st.gmgn.limited+' rate-limited · '+(st.gmgn.lastErr||'ok')],['Back-fill',st.backfill.done+' done','queue '+st.backfillQ+' · refs ok '+st.backfill.refsOk+' / missing '+st.backfill.refsMissing],['Disk',d.db_mb+' MB db','WAL '+d.wal_mb+' MB · free '+d.free_gb+' GB · bars '+d.bars],['Running costs','$'+(c.server+c.gmgn+c.rpc)+'/month','server $'+c.server+' · GMGN $'+c.gmgn+' · RPC $'+c.rpc+' — separate from paper returns']];
$('kpis').innerHTML=kp.map(x=>'<div class=k><b>'+x[1]+'</b><span>'+x[0]+' · '+x[2]+'</span></div>').join('');
const ff=$('ff');if(ff.options.length<2)[...new Set(S.rows.map(r=>r.family))].forEach(f=>ff.add(new Option(f,f)));
const rows=S.rows.filter(r=>(!ff.value||r.family===ff.value)&&(!$('fa').value||r.ag===$('fa').value)&&(!$('fp').value||r.policy===$('fp').value));
$('bt').innerHTML='<tr><th class=l>Book</th><th>coins</th><th>filled</th><th>open</th><th>closed</th><th>est.</th><th>re-buys</th><th>cancelled</th><th>unres.</th><th>net SOL</th><th>avg</th><th>win</th><th>q10</th><th>median</th><th>q90</th><th>≤−40%</th><th>≤−50%</th><th>≤−60%</th><th>ex top 3 net</th><th>ex top 3 avg</th><th>H1 avg</th><th>H2 avg</th><th>hold min</th><th>stake SOL</th><th>2×/3×/5×/10×</th><th>startup n / net</th><th class=l>exits</th></tr>'+rows.map(r=>'<tr><td class=l>'+r.book+'</td><td>'+r.mints+'</td><td>'+r.filled+'</td><td class=open>'+r.open+'</td><td>'+r.closed+'</td><td>'+(r.estimated_exits?r.estimated_exits+' ('+sol(r.estimated_net_sol)+')':'')+'</td><td>'+(r.rebuys_closed||r.rebuys_open?r.rebuys_closed+' ('+sol(r.rebuys_net_sol)+')'+(r.rebuys_open?' +'+r.rebuys_open+' open':''):'')+'</td><td>'+r.cancelled+'</td><td>'+r.unresolved+'</td><td>'+sol(r.net_sol)+'</td><td>'+pc(r.avg_ret)+'</td><td>'+(r.win_rate==null?'–':(r.win_rate*100).toFixed(0)+'%')+'</td><td>'+pc(r.q10)+'</td><td>'+pc(r.median)+'</td><td>'+pc(r.q90)+'</td><td>'+r.loss40+'</td><td>'+r.loss50+'</td><td>'+r.loss60+'</td><td>'+sol(r.ex_top3_net)+'</td><td>'+pc(r.ex_top3_avg)+'</td><td>'+pc(r.h1_avg)+'</td><td>'+pc(r.h2_avg)+'</td><td>'+(r.hold_median_min??'–')+'</td><td>'+(r.stake_committed_sol??0)+'</td><td>'+r.runners.x2+'/'+r.runners.x3+'/'+r.runners.x5+'/'+r.runners.x10+' of '+r.runners.n+(r.runners.partial?' ('+r.runners.partial+' partial)':'')+'</td><td>'+r.startup_entries+' / '+(r.startup_net_sol??0)+'</td><td class=l>'+Object.entries(r.exit_reasons).map(e=>e[0]+' '+e[1]).join(', ')+'</td></tr>').join('');
$('ov').innerHTML='<tr><th class=l>Family A</th><th class=l>Family B</th><th>shared coin·age</th><th>shared entries</th><th>A total</th><th>B total</th></tr>'+S.overlap.filter(o=>o.shared_mint_ages).map(o=>'<tr><td class=l>'+o.a+'</td><td class=l>'+o.b+'</td><td>'+o.shared_mint_ages+'</td><td>'+o.shared_entries+'</td><td>'+o.a_n+'</td><td>'+o.b_n+'</td></tr>').join('');
const inv=st.inventory;$('nt').innerHTML='<tr><th class=l>What</th><th class=l>Reason</th><th>count</th></tr>'+(inv?'<tr><td class=l>Inventory</td><td class=l>'+(inv.complete?'':'<b>INCOMPLETE</b> '+(inv.error||inv.stopped||'')+' · ')+'PumpSwap SOL pools '+inv.solPools+' · canonical '+inv.canonical+' · non-canonical '+inv.nonCanonical+' · priced '+inv.priced+' · ≥ $'+inv.minMc+' '+inv.aboveMin+' · in band '+inv.inBand+'</td><td></td></tr>':'')+'<tr><td class=l>Feed</td><td class=l>events on non-canonical pools (not priced) · non-SOL pools · coins whose history had to restart</td><td>'+st.feed.nonCanonical+' · '+st.feed.nonSol+' · '+(st.engine.coinLost||0)+'</td></tr><tr><td class=l>Entries by method</td><td class=l>'+Object.entries(S.populations).map(x=>x[0]+': '+x[1]).join(' &nbsp;|&nbsp; ')+'</td><td></td></tr><tr><td class=l>Solana-wide coverage</td><td class=l>'+(st.poller?'discovered '+st.poller.discovered+' · already live (not polled twice) '+st.poller.skippedLive+' · no metadata '+st.poller.noMeta+' · history restarted '+st.poller.lost+' · origins '+JSON.stringify(st.poller.by_origin)+' · risk '+JSON.stringify(st.poller.risk):'adapter off: only canonical PumpSwap pools are observed')+'</td><td></td></tr><tr><td class=l>Not covered</td><td class=l>Live (event-level) observation of Meteora, Raydium, Orca; a complete start-up inventory of already-existing coins outside PumpSwap; pump.fun bonding-curve history except via GMGN back-fill</td><td></td></tr>'+S.notes.map(n=>'<tr><td class=l>'+n.family+'</td><td class=l>'+n.reason+'</td><td>'+n.n+'</td></tr>').join('');
$('re').innerHTML='<tr><th>#</th><th class=l>coin</th><th class=l>origin · method</th><th>age</th><th class=l>families</th><th>mc</th><th class=l>status</th><th>fill delay / state age s</th><th>account check</th><th class=l>exits (stop: return)</th><th>best ×</th></tr>'+S.recent.map(e=>'<tr><td>'+e.id+'</td><td class=l><a href="https://gmgn.ai/sol/token/'+e.mint+'" target=_blank>'+e.mint.slice(0,6)+'</a></td><td class=l>'+(e.origin||(e.pop==='pumpswap-canonical'?'pump.fun':'?'))+' · '+e.mode+(e.cadence?' '+e.cadence+' s':'')+(e.route?' · '+e.route.join(' → '):'')+(e.risk&&e.risk!=='clean'?' · risk '+e.risk:'')+'</td><td>'+e.ag+'</td><td class=l>'+e.families.join(', ')+'</td><td>$'+e.mc.toLocaleString()+'</td><td class=l>'+e.status+(e.startup?' · startup':'')+(e.flags.length?' ('+e.flags.join('; ')+')':'')+'</td><td>'+(e.fill_delay??'–')+' / '+(e.state_age??'–')+'</td><td>'+(e.verify??'–')+'</td><td class=l>'+e.versions.filter(v=>!$('fp').value||v.policy===$('fp').value).map(v=>Math.round(v.stop*100)+(v.policy==='scale'?'h':'')+(v.stall?'s':'')+': '+(v.ret==null?v.status:(v.ret*100).toFixed(0)+'% '+v.reason)).join(' · ')+'</td><td>'+(e.runner_max??'–')+'</td></tr>').join('');}
$('fpop').onchange=load;$('ff').onchange=draw;$('fa').onchange=draw;$('fp').onchange=draw;load();setInterval(load,30000);
</script></body></html>`;
